# arc CLI P0 Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** A loopback `arc` CLI can attach to a running Arcane editor, headless runtime, or dedicated server, call the P0 command set (including play/step), and get one JSON envelope back — the envelope, endpoints, and exit codes in the spec, not a private dialect.

**Architecture:** `ArcaneCore` owns the protocol, the command registry, a 127.0.0.1 HTTP listener, the descriptor, and the main-thread dispatcher. Each host arms that listener and drains the queue once per tick before it simulates. Frame-spanning work (screenshot, compare, wait, `play.step`) is a job: the command arms it and returns, and the dispatcher steps it once per later tick. A capture job copies into a per-frame-slot host buffer and completes when the render ticket that rode with that copy is published back from that slot, even if `host.frame` does not move; it does not call `DeviceWaitIdle`. The listener is one service thread running one `PollSockets` loop. `arc.exe` is a thin console app over `Arcane::Arc::Cli` in Core; it never links `ArcaneClient`. Astra 1.0 lane step 6 has already landed `Optional` and nested `$defs`/`$ref`; this plan does not edit Astra. One capture or compare is in flight per host. A second call is `host.busy` with `detail.state` `capture-pending`, and the client retries that busy answer until `--timeout` (default 30 s; the flag is seconds and `ParseGlobals` stores milliseconds). Acceptance owns its child hosts through an RAII guard whose destructor kills every child. Schema `default` values are injected on the Arcane side by default-constructing the args struct and writing it with `ReflectionJsonWriter`. The settings-era `RemoteCVarService` and the editor's `DiagnosticStore` move into shapes every host can call.

**Tech Stack:** C++23, premake5, MSBuild (VS 2026), Catch2 v3, rapidcheck, nlohmann::json, Astra ECS, Win32 DACL, PowerShell.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-05-arc-cli-design.md`. Section 0 decisions 0.1–0.28 are accepted as recommended. Section 0.29 amendments AM1–AM6 are binding. This plan is s13 P0 plus the "P0 additions (2026-10-09)" paragraph (play|stop|pause|resume|step, AM1–AM6, `ecs.archetypes`). It does not build P1–P4. Research `docs/research/2026-10-05-arc-cli-research.md` is background. Where this plan and the spec disagree on a wire field, an endpoint, or an exit code, **the spec wins**. Where they disagree on a line number or a type that has moved, the **Tree facts** below win — they were read off this worktree (`feat/arc-cli`). This is rev 7.1 of the plan (rev 1 was `657bd773`, rev 2 was `ca3d3f61`, rev 3 was `6d4da827`, rev 4 was `1da89547`, rev 5 was `a8add14b`, rev 6 was `cef4dd01`, rev 7 was `5535ff3c`). Rev 2 replaced the flattened envelope, the `/events` route, the blocking step, the zero-fill Astra defaults, and the hard-coded ABI 55/56. Rev 3 makes capture an async readback, moves schema defaults onto Arcane's `ReflectionJsonWriter`, drives refusal tests through the real accept path, blesses acceptance into a scratch reference, and gives `--follow` one SSE lifetime. Rev 4 keys capture completion on the render ticket rather than `host.frame`, extracts the BGRA row normalization, makes the listener one poll loop, and writes the `$defs` emitter in full. Rev 5 refuses a second capture or compare while one is in flight with `host.busy` `capture-pending` (the client retries that busy answer), makes acceptance child hosts an RAII guard, and moves the Astra `Optional` attribute, nested `$defs`/`$ref`, and the Astra sync out to Astra 1.0 lane step 6. Rev 6 makes that overlap a real host test (capture B is refused while A is pending, then retried only after A's render ticket retires, and both PNGs are valid), retries busy answers until `--timeout` (default 30 s) rather than twice, and keeps an Arcane `$defs` emitter only as Task 4's fallback when Astra's `Optional` attribute is absent. Rev 7 (Grok review AX1–AX6) makes `--timeout` seconds (`ParseGlobals` stores milliseconds, and acceptance argv stays in seconds), defines the client's hung/died exit 7 path (`--hang-main 60`, a 250 ms liveness and heartbeat watch, the newest `.arcdiag` written after the call started), asserts explain history as Console / 45 / empty module, adds the ServerAdmin sites at `RemoteCVarService.cpp:75` and `:161` plus an Editor-context explain of a non-protected Editor-audience cvar, keeps one `operator|` in `Arcane` and re-exports it from the editor header, and deletes the `CVarType::Quat` arm. Rev 7.1 (Codex verify AV1–AV3) threads an explicit `CVarContext context` through `RemoteCVarService`'s `ReadValue`, `List`, `RunCommand`, and `Audit` (header declarations, definitions, and every call site; `Handle` passes `request.context`; no helper reads a `ServerAdmin` literal), matches the hang acceptance to its own child (the status row whose `pid` equals `pi.dwProcessId`, then the call passes `--pid` of that pid), and makes `ParseGlobals` start from a default-initialized `GlobalOptions` (`timeoutMs` 30000) so an omitted flag restores the default, with the omitted-timeout test using a fresh object.

## Global Constraints

- **Scope.** P0 only. Do not build the action registry (0.25, settings S4, P1), undo or entity writes (0.15, P1), `editor.selection.set` (P1), game-module hot-reload of commands (the registry *mechanism* is P0; loading them from `HotReloadPlugin` is P1), ImGui ArcUi (P2), `arc run` / `.arcscenario` (P2), public `arc job` / `--detach` / `build` / `test` (P3), the `github` format (P3 — `--format github` is refused, not ignored), MCP, the arc shell (P4), `POST /arc/v1/batch` (P1, s8.3), or a Linux process-start oracle (Appendix A). `net.*` is reserved (AM3) and is not implemented; `ecs.archetypes` is. Internal jobs exist from P0 (s7.7).
- **Protocol (spec s6, binding).** One arc envelope, not JSON-RPC. Protocol major `1`. Endpoints, exactly s6.1: `GET /arc/v1/status` (listener thread, every state), `POST /arc/v1/call` (main), `GET /arc/v1/stream?topics=...` (SSE), `GET /arc/v1/catalog` (listener snapshot). There is no `/events` route. The request object is s6.2: `{id, command, args, options:{world, readOnly, timeoutMs}}`. The field name is `command`, not `name`. The response body is the s6.3 envelope: `ok`, `protocol`, `id`, `command`, `host` `{kind, pid, world, frame, state, catalog, pluginGeneration}`, `data`, `errors`, `warnings`, `notifications`, `artifacts`, `undo`, `timing` `{queuedMs, ranMs}`. `ok:true` never coexists with a non-empty `errors`. The client adds `client` `{exitCode, retried, descriptor}` only when it prints. Bearer token on every endpoint, including `GET /status`. Loopback only.
- **Exit table (s6.6 + AM1 + AM6).** 0 ok (including compare verdicts Passed / PassedOnFallback / Skipped). 1 internal error in `arc` itself (`internal`). 2 usage/refusal: unknown verb, flag, or argument; ambiguous target; schema failure; **and `capture.no-renderer` (AM1)**. 3 auth or permission: `auth.denied`, `auth.throttled`, `permission.denied`, `permission.read-only`, `cvar.protected`. 4 no host: nothing to attach to, a stale descriptor, or a protocol-major mismatch. 5 `host.busy`, provably not run, retryable — every `host.busy` state, including minimized, queue-full, and capture-pending (AM6). 6 the host answered and the operation failed (`entity.not-found`, `compare.missing-reference`, and the other ran-and-failed codes). 7 unreachable, died, or restarting. 8 a check ran and did not pass (`compare.failed` / verdict `Failed`, `wait` timeout) with `ok:true` and empty `errors`. 130/143 signals. Worst-exit rank (AM2): `7 > 6 > 8 > 5 > 4 > 3 > 2 > 1 > 0`.
- **Nothing blocks the main thread (0.12).** A command that must wait for a frame, a tick, a settle, or a reload arms a job (s7.7) and returns. The dispatcher steps each running job **once** per `Drain`. `play.step N` does not call `Advance` N times inside one `Drain`. A capture or compare job does not call `NriGraphContext::ReadCapture` and does not call `DeviceWaitIdle`. It copies into one slot of a host buffer. It completes when the readback ticket stored with that copy is published (ruling P3 (a), below), not when `host.frame` advances.
- **Controller ruling P2 (binding).** (a) Capture reuses the PickNode async-readback pattern (`ArcaneClient/src/Arcane/Render/Nri/nodes/PickOutlineNodes.cpp` `RecordReadback` at 617, the drain-first comment at 628–635, the `memcpy` at 639–641): copy into a per-frame-slot host buffer and read that slot when the render ring reuses it; no fence query, no `DeviceWaitIdle`. The job does not complete on a sim frame. Ruling P3 (a) is the completion rule. (b) Schema defaults are injected on the Arcane side. Astra's change is only a correct `required` list (the `Optional` attribute) plus nested `$defs`/`$ref`. Arc's schema adapter default-constructs the args struct and writes it with `Arcane::ReflectionJsonWriter` (`ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Serialization/ReflectionJson.hpp:351`, constructor `:365`, `Visit` `:369`), then sets `"default"` per property. (c) The refusal tests drive the real listener accept path (a peer-check seam on the accepted socket's address, or a test listener whose peer predicate is injected) and send a forged `Host` header; each fails if its check is removed. (d) `SanitizeRelaunchLine` is called as `std::span<const std::string>` (`HostConfig.hpp:426`), with complete test code. (e) Acceptance compare blesses into a scratch reference, then requires exit 0 against it and exit 8 against a poisoned copy. There is no accepted-failure branch. The `crash-gpu` arm is gated like G1 (`ARCANE_DIAG_DESK`, `ArcaneTests/src/CrashWitnessTest.cpp:121-123`). (f) One follow path: the SSE `/stream` subscription owns the lifetime; the host-side follow job is keyed to that subscriber and cancelled when the stream closes; that is tested.
- **Controller ruling P4 (binding).** (a) ONE capture in flight per host: a capture/compare call arriving while one is pending answers host.busy{state:"capture-pending"} (exit 5); the client's existing automatic retry of provably-not-run busy answers covers it; test: two overlapping screenshot calls both succeed, and the second reports one busy retry. (b) the acceptance owns its child hosts through an RAII scope guard (destructor kills every child), so any REQUIRE abort cleans up; test the guard with a forced failure path. Task 27 owns the in-flight gate and the host overlap test: capture A is armed and not yet retired, capture B answers `host.busy` `capture-pending` and is not run, render frames advance until A's ticket retires, the retried B then succeeds, and both PNGs are valid. Task 28 applies the same gate to compare. Task 14's client retries exit 5 with backoff until `--timeout` (default 30 s, not a fixed two tries) and owns the mock-host test that stays busy for longer than 60 ms and then frees. Task 3 adds `capture-pending` to the exit-table examples. Task 30 owns the guard and the forced-failure test.
- **Controller ruling P3 (binding).** (a) capture completion is keyed to a RENDER submission / readback-slot ticket (the PickNode `m_ticket` pattern), never `host.frame` or a sim tick; tests: idle editor and idle headless runtime captures complete. (b) the capture hook reuses the existing normalization (row padding dropped, BGRA->RGBA) by calling or extracting the `NriGraphContext.cpp` ~2103-2124 code; test with a multi-colour, non-grey probe that would catch swapped channels. (c) the listener is one service thread running a non-blocking poll/select event loop with a per-connection state machine: `/status` is answered inline; a `/call` is parked until its main-thread result (or job completion) is published to the listener through a queue; SSE connections are written as frames arrive; the idle timeout applies per connection; the in-flight status test proves it. (d) The `$defs` emitter is written in full in Astra 1.0 lane step 6, which is not a task of this plan. Task 4's default path consumes that schema and does not contain an Arcane emitter. The Arcane emitter exists only as Task 4's fallback sub-task, and only when `struct Optional` is absent from `ThirdParty/Astra/include/Astra/Reflection/Attribute.hpp`. That sub-task's tests live there. Verified on this worktree: `PickNode::RecordReadback` is `ArcaneClient/src/Arcane/Render/Nri/nodes/PickOutlineNodes.cpp:617`. The drain-first comment is `:628-635`. The `memcpy` is `:639-641`. `m_probeTicket = m_ticket[frameSlot]` is `:645`. `m_pending[frameSlot] = true` is `:689` and `m_ticket[frameSlot] = ticket` is `:692`. `layout.offset = frameSlot * m_readbackStride` is `:677`. The normalization loop is anonymous-namespace code inside `NriGraphContext::ReadCapture` at `ArcaneClient/src/Arcane/Render/Nri/NriGraphContext.cpp:2103-2124` (`IsBgraFormat` at `:79-82`, `DeviceWaitIdle` still at `:2093` on that cover path only). It is not a public function. `ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Net/TcpSocket.hpp` exports no poll and no `SetNonBlocking`. `select` appears only inside `ConnectSocketWithTimeout` (`:273-393`), which restores blocking mode before it returns (`:374-390`). Task 6 adds `SetNonBlocking` and `PollSockets` beside that function and tests them in `ListenerTest.cpp`.
- **Controller ruling AX (binding).** (1) `--timeout <s>` is seconds, decimal allowed (spec s4.6, line 361). `ParseGlobals` stores `timeoutMs = max(1, std::llround(s * 1000.0))`. `--timeout 2` is 2000, `--timeout 0.5` is 500, and an omitted flag stays 30000. Acceptance argv stays in seconds. Wire `options.timeoutMs` stays milliseconds. Nothing multiplies again on the way into `Call` or `WaitArgs`. (2) A hung or dead target is exit 7. The hang spawn is `--hang-main 60` (a bare flag stays 0 and never sleeps). While a call is pending, and before the body is sent, the client checks that pid's liveness and the descriptor heartbeat every 250 ms. Process gone is `host.died`. A heartbeat stale by the same `IsHung` rule (older than 10 s, which is under the 15 s hang) is `host.hung`. Both are exit 7. The envelope names the newest `.arcdiag` under `<exe dir>/diagnostics` written after this call started, when one exists (spec s4.7 line 370, s12.1 line 812). The acceptance test waits until `arc status` reports that runtime hung, then calls, and requires exit 7. (3) The explain test asserts what `Explain` stores: `SetBy` `Console`, value 45, empty `module`. No caller field is added. (4) `RemoteCVarService.cpp:75` and `:161` are in the ServerAdmin list. An explain of a non-protected Editor-audience cvar from `CVarContext::Editor` succeeds. (5) One `operator|` lives in namespace `Arcane` beside `MaskFrom`. The editor header re-exports `MaskOf`, `MaskFrom`, `MatchesDiagnosticFilter`, and `operator|`. `EditorDiagnosticStoreTest.cpp` is not edited. (6) `CVarValueToJson` has no `CVarType::Quat` arm.
- **Controller ruling AV (binding).** (1) Thread an explicit `CVarContext context` parameter through `RemoteCVarService`'s `ReadValue`, `List`, `RunCommand`, and `Audit`: header declarations (`RemoteCVarService.hpp:67`, `:68`, `:69-70`, `:71-72`), definitions (`RemoteCVarService.cpp:60`, `:66-67`, `:81`, `:112-114`), and every call site. `Handle` (`:131`) passes `request.context`. No helper reads a `CVarContext::ServerAdmin` literal, and the new parameter has no default. (2) The hang acceptance matches its own child: find the status row whose `pid` equals `pi.dwProcessId`, and call with `--pid` of that pid. (3) `ParseGlobals` starts from a default-initialized `GlobalOptions` (`timeoutMs` 30000) so an omitted flag restores the default. The omitted-timeout test also uses a fresh object.
- **Units and build rules.** MKS. `/MD` (`staticruntime "off"`). No `/fp:fast`. UTF-8 without BOM. ASCII comments. Shaders stay data. Do not put `arc` on `PATH` (the name collides with Phabricator Arcanist).
- **Dist.** The macro is `ARC_BUILD_DIST` (`premake5.lua` Dist filter, first at line 202). The spec's `ARCANE_DIST` spelling (s10.6) is stale; use `ARC_BUILD_DIST`. Every new `Arcane::Arc` translation unit is born inside `#if !defined(ARC_BUILD_DIST)`. `arc.exe`'s Dist `main` prints `arc is compiled out of Dist builds` and returns 2. A Dist host that sees `--arc`, `--arc-descriptor`, or `--arc-idle-exit` exits 2 with that same sentence.
- **ABI.** Next ABI after `main` at execution time. `feat/physics-events` already sets ABI **55**. Once that merges and takes no further bump, arc's two bumps are **56** then **57**. This worktree's `kGamePluginABIVersion` is still 54 (`ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Plugin/PluginABI.hpp:1131`); do not describe 54 as 55, and do not hard-code 55 then 56. At Task 0 and again at Task 10, re-read `main`'s `PluginABI.hpp`, assign `that value + 1`, write the ledger for the integer you actually assigned, and restamp both manifests to it. Expected: Task 0 → 56 (`RemoteCVarRequest` / `RemoteCVarResponse`), Task 10 → 57 (`HostConfig` arc fields). New `Arcane::Arc` types are additive exports and do not bump. `ServerConfig` is the server exe's own struct, not the game ABI. Do not add Arc headers to `kGameFacingHeaders` (`ArcaneTests/src/ArcaneSpellingGuardTest.cpp:34`, walked at `:216`). Each game-ABI bump restamps `ReferenceProject/ReferenceProject.arcproj` (today `"abi": 54`) and `D:\dev\starworks\Aphelyon\Aphelyon.arcproj`. In Aphelyon, stage only the arcproj.
- **Repos and branches.** Arcane work stays on `feat/arc-cli` in `D:\dev\starworks\Arcane-arc`. The vendored stamp in `ThirdParty/Astra/VENDORED.txt` is commit `c46a90ede5584c5aae5692da02889e2f8f35425b`, branch `dev`, synced 2026-10-02, scope `include/` only, until Astra 1.0 lane step 6 syncs a newer one. This plan does not commit in `D:\dev\starworks\Astra` and does not edit `ThirdParty/Astra`. Never push. Never edit `ThirdParty/Astra` in place. Never touch Astra `vendor/Unreal`. `scripts/sync-vendor.ps1` does not exist; the Astra mirror is `scripts/sync-astra.ps1` (`-Source` default `D:\dev\starworks\Astra`, switch `-DryRun`).
- **Untracked Astra benches.** `bench-compare/*` (and any other untracked bench files) under `D:\dev\starworks\Astra` belong to the user. They never fail a task, never get staged, and never get deleted. This plan does not edit Astra. Stage named files only.

- **Build Arcane** from PowerShell (MSYS mangles `/p:`). `/FS` is not in `premake5.lua`; pass it.
  - Generate (after any added or removed `.cpp`, and after premake edits): `cmd /c call "D:\dev\starworks\Arcane-arc\GenerateProjects.bat"`
  - Build: `& "C:\Program Files\Microsoft Visual Studio\18\Community\MSBuild\Current\Bin\amd64\MSBuild.exe" D:\dev\starworks\Arcane-arc\Arcane.slnx /p:Configuration=Debug /m:8 /nodeReuse:false /p:CL=/FS /v:m`
  - Tests **from the exe dir**. PowerShell eats brackets, so:
    `Set-Location D:\dev\starworks\Arcane-arc\bin\Debug-windows-x86_64-md\ArcaneTests; cmd /c "ArcaneTests.exe \"[arc][tag]\""`
    Record the `Randomness seeded to:` line on failure.
- **ReferenceProject** (only Tasks 0, 10, and 30, and only because the game ABI moved or the acceptance runs the game): set `ARCANE_SDK=D:\dev\starworks\Arcane-arc`, then `bin\Debug-windows-x86_64-md\arcbuild\arcbuild.exe build --project ReferenceProject --config Debug`, **before** `Arcane.slnx`. A post-build stages the other config's DLL; a Debug-CRT game DLL inside a Release host crashes. Other tasks build `Arcane.slnx` only.
- **Golden gate** is the acceptance phase gate, not a per-task requirement: `powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File scripts\golden-gate.ps1` after Debug hosts exist. Do not re-bless. `editor-ui` is a shared slot.
- **Cvar macro.** Remote commands use `ARC_REMOTE_COMMAND`. `ARC_COMMAND` already registers console commands (`ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Config/CVarDecl.hpp:44`).
- **Threading.** The listener thread never touches the registry, the cvar pending store, undo, or ImGui. `GET /status` and `GET /catalog` read atomics. Everything else runs on the host main thread inside `Drain`, after the event pump and before the sim step. `Drain` returns after one step of each job; it does not sleep and it does not loop a job to completion. The listener is one `ServiceThread` (`ServiceThread.hpp:31`). Its body is one `PollSockets` loop. `GET /status` is answered on that thread. A `/call` is parked on that thread until `RequestQueue::Complete` publishes the result. The service thread does not call `RequestQueue::Wait`.
- **Tick (AM1).** On a fixed-tick host (ArcaneServer, or a headless runtime with no on-screen presenter), one "frame" is one sim tick. `host.frame` counts sim advances only. It is not `io.frameCount` (`ArcaneRuntime/src/RuntimeFrame.cpp` CaptureTail increments that) and not a free-running render counter. Render-only idle iterations may pump and render; they must not increment `host.frame`.

### Tree facts (verified here; do not follow a stale spec line)

- `RemoteCVarService`, `CVarContext`, and `Audience` are in this tree (`CVarTypes.hpp:102` context, `:106` audience, `SetBy` at `:75-86`). The v54 ledger comment that says the settings arc is "not on main yet" is stale relative to this branch.
- `CreateListenSocket(port, bindAddr = nullptr)` binds `INADDR_ANY` when `bindAddr` is null (`ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Net/TcpSocket.hpp:186`, `:203-206`). The listener passes `"127.0.0.1"`. `InitSockets` is `:168`. `ConnectSocketWithTimeout` (`:273`) is the only `select` in the file (`:341`). It sets non-blocking with `ioctlsocket` / `FIONBIO` (`:294`) and restores blocking before return (`:377-390`). There is no `PollSockets` and no exported `SetNonBlocking`. Task 6 adds both next to `ConnectSocketWithTimeout` and does not rewrite that function. `kMaxConnections` (16) is under `FD_SETSIZE` (64).
- `EditorLock::ReadLive` reads `editor.lock` only (`Project.cpp:796`). Descriptor liveness is a new `EditorLock::IsLive(const Info&)`, extracted from the three tells at `Project.cpp:807-837`. Do not point the descriptor at `ReadLive`. Do not change the Hub lock contract.
- `Arcane::Crypto::GenerateSecureToken` is `ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Crypto/Crypto.hpp:74`. `HmacSha256Hex` is `:264`. `HexEquals` is `:274`. The class is header-only. There is no `Sha256Hex` yet; Task 8 adds it beside `HmacSha256Hex`. Do not add `Crypto.cpp`.
- `Diagnostics::SetSink` is `Diagnostics.hpp:800`. `ClearSinkIfCurrent` is `:809`. `Diagnostics::Publish(key, span)` is `:812`. `DiagSeverity` is `Info` / `Warning` / `Error` at `:741`. `Diagnostic` fields are `severity`, `scope`, `code`, `message`, `detail`, `locator` at `:779`. `DiagLocator::Entity` packs a `uint64` at `:760`.
- `DiagnosticStore` today is `Arcane::Editor` in `ArcaneEditor/src/Panels/DiagnosticStore.hpp`. Real methods: `Publish(key, span)` `:61`, `Count(DiagSeverity)` `:65`, `Snapshot()` `:68`, `Filtered(mask, search)` `:69`, `InstallAsEngineSink()` `:79`, `UninstallEngineSink()` `:80`. `MatchesDiagnosticFilter` is `:48`. `SeverityMask` is `:23`. There is no `Count()`, no `ForEach`, no `Uninstall`, and no `Diagnostic::Severity`. The editor installs the sink at `DiagnosticStore.cpp:117-119`. `SeverityMask`, `operator|` (`:24-27`), `MaskOf` (`:28`), `MaskFrom` (`:38-42`), and `MatchesDiagnosticFilter` are all in `Arcane::Editor` (`:19-49`). `MaskFrom` applies `|` at `:40-41`. `EditorDiagnosticStoreTest.cpp:95` is `using Arcane::Editor::MaskFrom`. `:100-109` call `Arcane::Editor::MatchesDiagnosticFilter`. `:123` is `SeverityMask::Warning | SeverityMask::Info`. `ProblemsPanel.cpp:55` calls `MaskFrom` inside `namespace Arcane::Editor`. `EditorPanels.cpp:1198` calls `MatchesDiagnosticFilter` the same way. That test file is not edited. ArcaneTests source-compiles the editor cpp at `premake5.lua:1418` (comment `:1414-1417`). C4251 is already disabled at `premake5.lua:39`.
- `CVarType` (`CVarTypes.hpp:15-30`) is Bool, Int32, UInt32, Int64, UInt64, Float32, Float64, String, Color, Vec2, Vec3, Vec4, Enum. There is no Quat, and `CVarValue` (`:175-199`) has no `AsQuat`. Color and Enum are not number arrays. `CVarRegistry::Execute` passes an empty source module into `Set` (`CVarRegistry.cpp:1393`, the `{}` before `ctx`). `CVarHistoryRecord` is `{by, value, module}` (`CVarRegistry.hpp:53-58`). Registration pushes `{Default, published, empty module}` (`CVarRegistry.cpp:748`). `module` is not the caller id. The caller id is `CVarAuditRecord::callerId` (`CVarRegistry.hpp:198`). `UnregisterModule` erases history by `module` (`CVarRegistry.cpp:949`), and `Set` replaces a record by `(by, module)` (`:879`). Do not store `callerId` in `module`.
- `--hang-main` defaults to `"0"` (`HostConfig.cpp:178`) and is stored in `hangMainFrame` at `:259`. `RuntimeFrame.cpp:484` sleeps only when `hangMainFrame != 0`. The sleep is inside `RenderGraph` (`:452`), after the beat for that frame, for `kHangMainSeconds`, then the host continues (`:487-489`, `HostConfig.hpp:348-354`). `CaptureTail` increments `io.frameCount` at `RuntimeFrame.cpp:795`, which is the counter `:484` compares. `kHangMainSeconds` is `DiagnosticsSettings{}.hangSeconds + 3u` (`HostConfig.hpp:401`). `hangSeconds` defaults to 12 (`DiagnosticsSettingsData.hpp:23`), so the sleep is 15 s and `HostConfigTest.cpp:196` pins `kHangMainSeconds == 15`. The hang watchdog's threshold is `g_cfg.hangSeconds` (`Diagnostics.cpp:1919`). A report with exit code 0 leaves the process running (`Diagnostics.hpp:236`). `IsHung` is a descriptor heartbeat older than 10 s (`now - heartbeatUnixMs > 10000`). 10 < 12 < 15, so during the sleep the descriptor is hung before the watchdog writes `<exe dir>/diagnostics/*.arcdiag`, and both happen before the sleep ends. `Drain` is what calls `TouchHeartbeat` (Task 11), on the main thread, so the sleep stops the descriptor heartbeat. The listener thread still answers `GET /status`.
- Astra `JsonSchemaGenerator::Options::includeDefaults` is false and marked "not supported yet" (`ThirdParty/Astra/include/Astra/Reflection/JsonSchema.hpp:25`). It stays that way. Astra does not emit `"default"`. The required-list pushes every non-hidden field (`:84-107`). Astra 1.0 lane step 6 skips hidden fields and `IsOptional()` fields. This plan does not edit that header. `Generate(const TypeMeta&)` is `:43`. `WriteFieldSchema` is `:208`. `GetJsonType` defaults an unknown class to `"object"` (`:348`). There is no nlohmann in Astra. Keep it that way. `FieldInfo::IsSerializable()` is `FieldInfo.hpp:278`.

## Review Focus

These five are the inputs most likely to bite a user that a happy-path test would miss. Each one has a pinning test in the task named, written in that task's own step.

1. **The listener forgets the bind address and calls `CreateListenSocket(port)`.** The default is `INADDR_ANY` (`TcpSocket.hpp:203-206`), so the port is reachable off-machine. A reasonable person expects the socket's bound address to be `127.0.0.1` and nowhere else. Pinned in Task 6 (`"the listen socket is bound to 127.0.0.1"`).
2. **A request carries the right bearer token and also an `Origin` header, or a `Host` that is not `127.0.0.1:<port>` or `localhost:<port>`, or the TCP peer is not `127.0.0.1`.** DNS rebinding and a future bind slip. A reasonable person expects refusal before the command runs. Pinned in Task 6 (`"a good token does not survive Origin"`, `"a forged Host is refused by the listener"`, and `"the accept loop closes a peer the predicate refuses"`). Removing the Origin check, the Host check, or the peer predicate makes its test fail.
3. **The editor is minimized.** A queued `cvar.get` must still run, a screenshot must return `host.busy` with `state:"minimized"` and exit 5 without grabbing pixels, and the waiter must return rather than hang. Pinned in Task 13 (`"minimized: cvar.get runs, screenshot does not, and the waiter returns"`).
4. **The token is written to a log line, an envelope, a descriptor dump, or `arc --verbose`.** A reasonable person expects the secret absent from all four. Pinned in Task 14 (`"verbose and human output redact the bearer token"`), with the listener-side half in Task 6 (`"a refused request does not log the token"`).
5. **`play.step 10` on an `--arc` headless runtime that was not given `--frames`, or the same step on ArcaneServer.** A reasonable person expects `host.frame` to advance by exactly 10 and then stop, counting sim ticks rather than rendered frames, **and** expects `GET /status` to be answered while those 10 ticks are still in flight. Pinned in Task 29 (`"step 10 advances host.frame by exactly 10 while status still answers"` and `"ArcaneServer step 10 counts ticks, not rendered frames"`).

These are also pinned, in the task that owns them, and are not a sixth header line: a recycled pid (Task 7), a Protected cvar read from the Editor context (Task 20), `--all` worst-exit when one host is busy (5) and another is dead (7) (Task 18), a compare that ran and failed (`ok:true`, exit 8) (Task 28), auth exit 3 (Task 3), `capture.no-renderer` exit 2 (Task 3 and Task 27), the 30 s idle close (Task 6), and an idle capture that completes on a render ticket while `host.frame` stays put (Task 27's frame-stays-10 case, Task 30's idle editor bless and idle headless `screenshot window`). Also pinned the same way, not as a sixth numbered line: a second capture while one is in flight answers `host.busy` with `state` `capture-pending` and exit 5, and the retried call succeeds only after that ticket retires, with both PNGs valid (Task 27); the client keeps retrying a busy answer for longer than 60 ms, until `--timeout` (default 30 s) (Task 14); the acceptance host guard kills its children on a forced failure (Task 30); `--timeout` is seconds and the stored field is milliseconds (Task 14); a runtime launched with `--hang-main 60` is reported hung by `arc status` on the row whose `pid` equals that child's `pi.dwProcessId`, and the following `cvar get --pid <that pid>` exits 7 with `host.hung` (Task 30).

- Produces: `context` appended **last** on the request so `{op, name, value, callerId}` still compiles. Structured strings appended **after** `text` so `{ok, text}` still compiles.

```cpp
struct RemoteCVarRequest {
    std::string op;
    std::string name;
    std::string value;
    std::string callerId;
    CVarContext context = CVarContext::ServerAdmin; // LAST
};

struct RemoteCVarResponse {
    bool ok = false;
    std::string text;
    std::string valueJson = "null";
    std::string type;
    std::string setBy;
    std::string audience;
    std::string scope;
    std::string apply;
    std::string flags;
    std::string publishedJson;
    std::string pendingJson;
    std::string historyJson; // JSON array of {by, value, module}
};

nlohmann::json CVarValueToJson(const CVarValue& value);
```

`Handle` (`RemoteCVarService.cpp:131`) passes `request.context` into `ReadValue`, `List`, `RunCommand`, and `Audit`, and into the `Execute` calls that sit in `Handle` (`:153`, `:161`, `:163`, `:176`). - [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Add to `RemoteCVarServiceTest.cpp`, and add `#include <nlohmann/json.hpp>` beside the existing includes. Leave `Req()`'s 4-field aggregate as it is. `Knob` and `AuditLog` are the anonymous-namespace helpers at lines 16–35, visible to every case in this file. Do not invent a second registrar. Real fixture names already in that file: `test.server.tick` (Server, Int32 60), `test.server.password` (Server, Protected, `"hunter2"`), `editor.secret` (Editor, Protected, `"editorhorse"`). The new Editor case registers `editor.visible` through that same `Knob`.

```cpp
TEST_CASE("Editor context can read an Editor-audience cvar the ServerAdmin list hides", "[arc][cvar]") {
    CVarRegistry reg;
    Knob(reg, "editor.visible", CVarValue::Int32(3), Audience::Editor);
    Knob(reg, "editor.secret", CVarValue::String("editorhorse"), Audience::Editor, CVarFlags::Protected);
    RemoteCVarService svc(reg);
    RemoteCVarRequest req{"get", "editor.visible", "", "arc-test", CVarContext::Editor};
    const RemoteCVarResponse res = svc.Handle(req);
    CHECK(res.ok);
    CHECK(res.text.find('3') != std::string::npos);
    CHECK(res.valueJson == "3");
    RemoteCVarRequest hidden{"get", "editor.visible", "", "admin-1"};
    CHECK_FALSE(svc.Handle(hidden).ok);
}

TEST_CASE("Protected get is masked in the Editor context", "[arc][cvar]") {
    CVarRegistry reg;
    Knob(reg, "editor.secret", CVarValue::String("editorhorse"), Audience::Editor, CVarFlags::Protected);
    RemoteCVarService svc(reg);
    RemoteCVarRequest req{"get", "editor.secret", "", "arc-test", CVarContext::Editor};
    const RemoteCVarResponse res = svc.Handle(req);
    CHECK_FALSE(res.ok);
    CHECK(res.text.find("protected") != std::string::npos);
    CHECK(res.text.find("editorhorse") == std::string::npos);
    CHECK(res.valueJson == "null");
}

TEST_CASE("explain returns structured history and still masks Protected", "[arc][cvar]") {
    CVarRegistry reg;
    Knob(reg, "test.server.tick", CVarValue::Int32(60), Audience::Server);
    Knob(reg, "test.server.password", CVarValue::String("hunter2"), Audience::Server, CVarFlags::Protected);
    RemoteCVarService svc(reg);
    RemoteCVarRequest set{"set", "test.server.tick", "45", "arc-test", CVarContext::ServerAdmin};
    REQUIRE(svc.Handle(set).ok);
    RemoteCVarRequest exp{"explain", "test.server.tick", "", "arc-test", CVarContext::ServerAdmin};
    const RemoteCVarResponse res = svc.Handle(exp);
    CHECK(res.ok);
    CHECK(res.text.find("Console") != std::string::npos);
    CHECK(res.setBy == "Console");
    CHECK(res.valueJson == "45");
    const auto history = nlohmann::json::parse(res.historyJson);
    REQUIRE(history.is_array());
    REQUIRE(history.size() >= 2);
    CHECK(history.front()["by"] == "Default");
    CHECK(history.front()["value"] == 60);
    CHECK(history.front()["module"] == "");
    CHECK(history.back()["by"] == "Console");
    CHECK(history.back()["value"] == 45);
    CHECK(history.back()["module"] == "");
    CHECK(res.historyJson.find("arc-test") == std::string::npos);
    RemoteCVarRequest prot{"explain", "test.server.password", "", "arc-test", CVarContext::Editor};
    const RemoteCVarResponse masked = svc.Handle(prot);
    CHECK_FALSE(masked.ok);
    CHECK(masked.text.find("hunter2") == std::string::npos);
    CHECK(masked.valueJson == "null");
    CHECK(masked.historyJson.empty());
}

TEST_CASE("Editor context explains a non-protected Editor-audience cvar", "[arc][cvar]") {
    CVarRegistry reg;
    AuditLog audit;
    Knob(reg, "editor.visible", CVarValue::Int32(3), Audience::Editor);
    RemoteCVarService svc(reg, &AuditLog::Sink, &audit);
    RemoteCVarRequest set{"set", "editor.visible", "9", "arc-test", CVarContext::Editor};
    REQUIRE(svc.Handle(set).ok);
    REQUIRE(audit.records.size() == 1);
    CHECK(audit.records.front().context == CVarContext::Editor);
    RemoteCVarRequest exp{"explain", "editor.visible", "", "arc-test", CVarContext::Editor};
    const RemoteCVarResponse res = svc.Handle(exp);
    CHECK(res.ok);
    CHECK(res.text.find("unknown") == std::string::npos);
    CHECK(res.valueJson == "9");
    CHECK(res.setBy == "Console");
    CHECK(res.audience == "Editor");
}

TEST_CASE("omitted context stays ServerAdmin", "[arc][cvar]") {
    RemoteCVarRequest req{"get", "test.server.tick", "", "tester"};
    CHECK(req.context == CVarContext::ServerAdmin);
}
```

`ReadValue` passes `context` to `Execute` (`:62`). `Audit` assigns `record.context = context` (`:75`). `List` passes `context` to `CVarRegistry::List` (`:83`), `CanRead` (`:92`), and `Execute` (`:100`). `RunCommand` passes `context` to `Execute` (`:125`) and to its `Audit` call (`:127`). `Handle` passes `request.context` at every call: `List` (`:138`), `RunCommand` (`:145`), the get `Execute` (`:153`), the explain probe `Execute` (`:161`), the `cvar_explain` `Execute` (`:163`), `ReadValue` (`:171`, `:175`, `:181`), `Audit` (`:172`, `:187`), and the set `Execute` (`:176`). Leaving the explain probe at `:161` as `ServerAdmin` fails the Editor explain case. The Protected early return at line 160 still runs first, so the Protected explain case does not pin 161.

Color and Enum use `FormatCVarValue` wrapped as a JSON string, and so does the default arm. The Protected refusal assigns `valueJson = "null"` itself and does not call this function. glm quaternions in `ReflectionJson` / `SchemaFor` stay `[x, y, z, w]` (`ReflectionJson.hpp:217`); that is a different type.

### Task 1: Move `DiagnosticStore` into ArcaneCore

No ABI bump: a new Core type plus a using-alias. The editor header keeps the old name so panels do not all change includes.

**Files:**
- Create: `ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Base/DiagnosticStore.hpp`
- Create: `ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Base/DiagnosticStore.cpp`
- Modify: `ArcaneEditor/src/Panels/DiagnosticStore.hpp` (becomes an alias)
- Modify: `ArcaneEditor/src/Panels/DiagnosticStore.cpp` (delete the file; its methods moved)
- Modify: `premake5.lua` (delete the ArcaneTests source-compile of that editor cpp: the comment at lines 1414–1417 and the file line at 1418)
- Test: `ArcaneTests/src/Arc/DiagnosticStoreMoveTest.cpp`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the class in `ArcaneEditor/src/Panels/DiagnosticStore.hpp` as it is today. `Count(Arcane::DiagSeverity)` line 65. `Snapshot()` line 68. `Filtered(SeverityMask, std::string_view)` line 69. `InstallAsEngineSink()` line 79. `UninstallEngineSink()` line 80. `Publish(std::string_view key, std::span<const Diagnostic>)` line 61. `MatchesDiagnosticFilter` line 48. `SeverityMask` line 23. `Diagnostics::Publish(key, span)` at `Diagnostics.hpp:812`. `ClearSinkIfCurrent(Sink, void*)` at `:809`. `DiagSeverity::{Info, Warning, Error}` at `:741`. `Diagnostic` at `:779`.
- Produces: the same class in namespace `Arcane`, no ImGui include. Editor header:

```cpp
#pragma once
#include <Arcane/Base/DiagnosticStore.hpp>
namespace Arcane::Editor {
using DiagnosticStore = Arcane::DiagnosticStore;
using SeverityMask = Arcane::SeverityMask;
using Arcane::operator|;
using Arcane::MaskOf;
using Arcane::MaskFrom;
using Arcane::MatchesDiagnosticFilter;
}
```

Move `SeverityMask`, `operator|`, `MaskOf`, `MaskFrom`, and `MatchesDiagnosticFilter` with the class, same signatures, into namespace `Arcane`. One `operator|` lives in `Arcane`, immediately after `SeverityMask` and before `MaskOf`. `MaskFrom` applies `|` (`DiagnosticStore.hpp:40-41`), and argument-dependent lookup for an enum whose home namespace is `Arcane` does not search `Arcane::Editor`. The editor header does not define a second `operator|`. `using Arcane::operator|;` re-exports the overload set. Mark `class DiagnosticStore` and `MatchesDiagnosticFilter` `ARC_CORE_API`. `operator|`, `MaskOf`, and `MaskFrom` stay inline constexpr in the header, with no export macro. `Clear` and `ClearAll` (`DiagnosticStore.hpp:62-63`) move with the class. `InstallAsEngineSink` still calls `Diagnostics::SetSink`. `UninstallEngineSink` still calls `ClearSinkIfCurrent(&DiagnosticStore::SinkTrampoline, this)`. Do not rename it to `Uninstall`. The process-wide sink stays last-writer-wins. The class holds a mutex and STL members. C4251 is already disabled workspace-wide (`premake5.lua:39`). Do not add a suppression. Core's `files` glob is `src/**.cpp` (`premake5.lua:139-142`), so the new cpp is picked up by `GenerateProjects.bat`.

```cpp
#include <catch2/catch_test_macros.hpp>

TEST_CASE("Core DiagnosticStore installs, counts by severity, and uninstalls", "[arc]") {
    Arcane::DiagnosticStore store;

    Arcane::Diagnostic row;
    row.severity = Arcane::DiagSeverity::Error;

    CHECK(store.Count(Arcane::DiagSeverity::Error) == 1);
    CHECK(store.Count(Arcane::DiagSeverity::Warning) == 0);

    CHECK_FALSE(Arcane::MatchesDiagnosticFilter(snap[0], Arcane::SeverityMask::Warning, "hello"));
    CHECK_FALSE(Arcane::MatchesDiagnosticFilter(snap[0], Arcane::SeverityMask::Error, "absent"));
    store.UninstallEngineSink();
    Arcane::Diagnostic later = row;
    later.message = "after uninstall";
    const Arcane::Diagnostic laterRows[] = { later };
    Arcane::Diagnostics::Publish("arc-move", laterRows);
    CHECK(store.Count(Arcane::DiagSeverity::Error) == 1);
    CHECK(store.Snapshot()[0].message == "hello store");
}
```

`MatchesDiagnosticFilter` is `noexcept` and returns bool (`DiagnosticStore.hpp:48`): the mask must include the row's severity and the search text must occur.

### Task 2: Sequenced log ring

Not the crash backlog. `kBacklogLines` is 512 (`Log.hpp:109`) and the backlog is a torn read (`Log.hpp:134-140`). Do not reuse `FreezeBacklog`. There is no `Log::Info`. The engine logger is `Log::Engine()` (`Log.hpp:36`), an `spdlog::logger*`.

**Files:**
- Modify: `ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Base/Log.hpp`
- Create: `ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Base/LogRing.cpp`
- Test: `ArcaneTests/src/Arc/LogRingTest.cpp`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Log::Engine()` and `AttachFileSink` (`Log.hpp:100`).
- Produces:

```cpp
struct LogRecord {
    std::uint64_t seq = 0;     // starts at 1; never repeats in-process
    std::int64_t unixMs = 0;
    int level = 0;             // spdlog level as int
    std::string logger;
    std::string text;
};

using LogSinkFn = void (*)(const LogRecord& record, void* user);

struct LogSubscription { std::uint64_t id = 0; };

namespace Log {
LogSubscription Subscribe(LogSinkFn fn, void* user);
void Unsubscribe(LogSubscription sub);
std::vector<LogRecord> ReadSince(std::uint64_t seqExclusive, std::size_t maxLines);
std::size_t RingCapacity(); // default 4096
}
```

The ring is process-wide, mutexed, separate from the crash backlog. Overflow drops the oldest. `ReadSince(0)` returns the retained window. A subscriber runs on the logging thread and must not re-enter `Log`. Capacity is the cvar `log.arc.ringLines` (Dev, default 4096), read when the ring is first touched and on each publish if the cvar changed. Declare it with `ARC_CVAR` in the cpp, `CVarFlags::Dev`, `Audience::Editor`, `SettingScope::PreferencesMachine`, help `"How many log lines arc logs retains."`. Dist's registry refuses Dev cvars; the ring then stays at 4096.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

```cpp
#include <catch2/catch_test_macros.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Base/Log.hpp>

static int g_hits = 0;
static void OnLog(const Arcane::LogRecord& rec, void*) {
    if (rec.text.find("arc-ring-line") != std::string::npos)
        ++g_hits;
}

TEST_CASE("Subscribe observes a line and ReadSince resumes from seq", "[arc]") {
    g_hits = 0;
    const auto sub = Arcane::Log::Subscribe(&OnLog, nullptr);
    Arcane::Log::Engine()->info("arc-ring-line one");
    Arcane::Log::Engine()->info("arc-ring-line two");
    CHECK(g_hits == 2);
    const auto all = Arcane::Log::ReadSince(0, 16);
    REQUIRE_FALSE(all.empty());
    const auto tail = Arcane::Log::ReadSince(all.front().seq, 16);
    REQUIRE_FALSE(tail.empty());
    CHECK(tail.front().seq == all.front().seq + 1);
    Arcane::Log::Unsubscribe(sub);
    const int frozen = g_hits;
    Arcane::Log::Engine()->info("arc-ring-line three");
    CHECK(g_hits == frozen);
    const auto after = Arcane::Log::ReadSince(tail.back().seq, 16);
    REQUIRE_FALSE(after.empty());
    CHECK(after.back().text.find("arc-ring-line three") != std::string::npos);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run the test to verify it fails**

Generate, Debug build, filter `Subscribe observes a line`. Expected: FAIL to compile. `Log::Subscribe` does not exist.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the ring**

One TU owns the ring, the mutex, and the subscriber vector. On each log call that already formats a line, also push a `LogRecord` and invoke subscribers. Do not take the backlog lock and the ring lock in opposite orders; the ring has its own mutex and does not call `FreezeBacklog`. `seq` is a `uint64_t` incremented under the mutex. `ReadSince` copies out records with `seq > seqExclusive`, up to `maxLines`, in order. Wire the cvar as specified. Changing `log.arc.ringLines` downward drops oldest until `size == capacity`.

- [ ] **Step 4: Run the test**

Same filter. Expected: PASS. Two delivered lines, monotonic `seq`, unsubscribe silence, and the third line still readable via `ReadSince`.

### Task 3: Envelope, error codes, exit table (s6.3, s6.4, s6.6, AM1, AM6)

**Files:**
- Create: `ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Arc/Protocol.hpp`
- Create: `ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Arc/Protocol.cpp`
- Test: `ArcaneTests/src/Arc/ProtocolTest.cpp`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: nlohmann::json (already on ArcaneCore).
- Produces: namespace `Arcane::Arc`. The host envelope has no `result`, no `hostFrame`, and no top-level `exitCode`. Exit is derived by `ExitFromHost`.

```cpp
inline constexpr std::uint32_t kProtocol = 1;
inline constexpr std::uint32_t kMaxBodyBytes = 4u * 1024u * 1024u;
inline constexpr std::uint32_t kMaxHeaderBytes = 16u * 1024u;
inline constexpr std::uint32_t kMaxConnections = 16;
inline constexpr std::uint32_t kMaxSseSubscribers = 8;
inline constexpr std::uint32_t kQueueDepthDefault = 64;
inline constexpr std::uint32_t kFrameBudgetCommands = 8;
inline constexpr double kFrameBudgetMs = 4.0;
inline constexpr std::uint32_t kIdleConnectionSeconds = 30;

enum class HostKind : std::uint8_t { Editor, Runtime, Server };
enum class HostState : std::uint8_t { Starting, Ready, Playing, Paused, Reloading, Stopping, Hung };
enum class Access : std::uint8_t { Observe, Drive };
enum class Mutates : std::uint8_t { None, Scene, Project, Preferences, PlayMode };
enum class PlayPolicy : std::uint8_t { Any, EditOnly, PlayingOnly };
enum class ExecThread : std::uint8_t { Main, Service, Job };

struct Error {
    std::string code;
    std::string message;
    std::string remediation;
    nlohmann::json detail = nlohmann::json::object();
    nlohmann::json locator = nullptr;
    std::vector<std::string> candidates;
    std::string check;
};

struct HostBlock {
    std::string kind;                 // "editor" | "runtime" | "server"
    std::uint32_t pid = 0;
    std::string world;
    std::uint64_t frame = 0;
    std::string state;
    std::uint32_t catalog = 0;
    std::uint32_t pluginGeneration = 0;
};

struct Artifact {
    std::string kind;
    std::string path;
    std::uint64_t bytes = 0;
    std::string sha256;
    std::optional<std::uint32_t> width;
    std::optional<std::uint32_t> height;
    std::string format;
};

struct UndoBlock {
    nlohmann::json label = nullptr;   // string or JSON null
    bool undoable = false;
    std::string reason;               // play-mode | runtime-host | not-undoable-command
    std::uint64_t transaction = 0;
};

struct Timing {
    double queuedMs = 0;
    double ranMs = 0;
};

struct Envelope {
    bool ok = false;
    int protocol = 1;
    std::string id;
    std::string command;
    HostBlock host;
    nlohmann::json data = nlohmann::json::object();
    std::vector<Error> errors;
    std::vector<Error> warnings;
    std::vector<Error> notifications;
    std::vector<Artifact> artifacts;
    std::optional<UndoBlock> undo;    // nullopt serializes as null
    Timing timing;
};

struct ClientBlock {
    int exitCode = 0;
    bool retried = false;
    std::string descriptor;
    std::string token; // print-time only; ToJson(Envelope) never emits this
};

struct HostStatus {
    std::uint32_t protocol = kProtocol;
    HostKind hostKind = HostKind::Editor;
    std::uint32_t pid = 0;
    std::uint64_t processStart = 0;
    std::uint16_t port = 0;
    std::string state;
    std::uint64_t frame = 0;
    std::string world;
    std::uint32_t abi = 0;
    std::string project;
    std::uint32_t catalogueGeneration = 0;
    std::uint32_t pluginGeneration = 0;
    bool reloadOk = true;
    std::string reloadReason;
    std::uint64_t reloadAtFrame = 0;
    std::vector<std::string> capabilities;
    std::int64_t heartbeatUnixMs = 0;
    bool minimized = false;
};

const char* ToString(HostKind kind);
nlohmann::json ToJson(const Envelope& env);
nlohmann::json ToJson(const HostStatus& status); // no token field exists on HostStatus
Envelope ParseEnvelope(const nlohmann::json& j);
Envelope MakeOk(std::string command, nlohmann::json data, HostBlock host);
Envelope MakeError(std::string command, std::string code, std::string message, std::string remediation);
int ExitFromHost(const Envelope& env);
int WorstExit(std::span<const int> codes);
```

`MakeError` sets `ok = false`, pushes one `Error`, and leaves `data` an object. It does not take an exit code. `MakeOk` sets `ok = true` and clears `errors`.

`ExitFromHost` switches on the first error code. Empty errors and `ok` return 0, except: `data.verdict == "Failed"` or `data.timedOut == true` returns 8 (the check ran; `ok` stays true). A non-ok envelope with an empty code returns 2.

| code | exit |
|---|---|
| `internal` | 1 |
| `arg.invalid`, `arg.unknown`, `arg.missing`, `arg.inapplicable`, `command.unknown`, `command.unavailable`, `command.host-kind`, `target.ambiguous`, `component.ambiguous`, `entity.ambiguous`, `capture.no-renderer` | 2 |
| `auth.denied`, `auth.throttled`, `permission.denied`, `permission.read-only`, `cvar.protected` | 3 |
| `protocol.mismatch`, `target.none` | 4 |
| `host.busy` (any `detail.state`, including `minimized`, `queue-full`, `capture-pending`, `modal`, `reloading`, `connections`), and `capture.minimized` | 5 |
| `entity.not-found`, `component.unknown`, `component.absent`, `compare.missing-reference`, `cvar.unknown`, `cvar.type-mismatch`, `reflect.unreadable`, and any other code ending in `.not-found` | 6 |
| `host.unreachable`, `host.died`, `host.restarting`, `host.hung` | 7 |
| `compare.failed`, `wait.timeout` | 8 |

`compare.failed` and `wait.timeout` are not put in `errors` on the success-shaped check path. `ExitFromHost` still maps those codes to 8 if a caller does put them in `errors`, and also returns 8 when `ok` is true, `errors` is empty, and `data.verdict` is `"Failed"` or `data.timedOut` is true. `capture.no-renderer` is exit **2**, not 6. Auth codes are exit **3**, not 2. The wire code for a bad token is `auth.denied`, not `auth.rejected`.

`ToJson(Envelope)` always emits `ok`, `protocol`, `id`, `command`, `host`, `data`, `errors`, `warnings`, `notifications`, `artifacts`, `undo`, `timing`, even when the arrays are empty. `undo` is `null` when the optional is empty. When `undo` is set, the object is `{label, undoable, transaction}` (spec s6.3 and s8.2), and it includes `reason` when `UndoBlock::reason` is non-empty (`play-mode`, `runtime-host`, `not-undoable-command`). An empty `reason` omits that key. `host` is an object with `kind`, `pid`, `world`, `frame`, `state`, `catalog`, `pluginGeneration`. `timing` is `{queuedMs, ranMs}`. `ToJson` does not emit `client`, `exitCode`, `result`, or `hostFrame`. `ok:true` with a non-empty `errors` is a programming error: `MakeOk` clears `errors`, and a test asserts that.

`WorstExit` rank is `7 > 6 > 8 > 5 > 4 > 3 > 2 > 1 > 0`. Unknown codes sort as 2. Empty span returns 0.

Whole header and cpp wrapped in `#if !defined(ARC_BUILD_DIST)` except the includes. Provide an empty `#else` so the include is legal. Do not export the symbols from a Dist build.

A match means the lane has synced. The default path below is the one that runs: `using ::Astra::Optional;` beside the other aliases in `Reflection.hpp` (lines 25–40), and `SchemaFor` does not define or call an Arcane `$defs` emitter. No match means the lane is late: skip that `using`, do not invent `Optional` on the default path, and execute the labelled fallback sub-task. - Produces:

```cpp
namespace Arcane::Arc {

struct Call {
    std::string id;
    std::string command;
    nlohmann::json args = nlohmann::json::object();
    std::string world;          // options.world
    bool readOnly = false;      // options.readOnly
    std::uint32_t timeoutMs = 30000; // milliseconds, copied from the wire; the listener does not scale
    std::string callerId;
};

struct CommandResult {
    Envelope envelope;
    std::uint64_t completesJob = 0; // non-zero: dispatcher holds the waiter until that job finishes
};

using CommandFn = CommandResult (*)(const Call& call, void* user);

struct Desc {
    std::string name;           // dotted wire name, the value of Call::command
    std::string summary;
    Access access = Access::Observe;
    Mutates mutates = Mutates::None;
    PlayPolicy play = PlayPolicy::Any;
    ExecThread thread = ExecThread::Main;
    bool needsFrame = false;
    std::string module;         // empty = host
    const Astra::TypeMeta* argsMeta = nullptr;
    CommandFn fn = nullptr;
    void* user = nullptr;
};

class CommandRegistry {
public:
    static CommandRegistry& Get();
    bool Register(Desc desc, std::string* error);
    void UnregisterModule(std::string_view module);
    std::uint32_t Generation() const;
    const Desc* Find(std::string_view name) const;
    std::vector<const Desc*> List() const;
    nlohmann::json SchemaOf(std::string_view name) const;
    nlohmann::json Catalogue() const;
    CommandResult Invoke(const Call& call);
};

nlohmann::json SchemaFor(const Astra::TypeMeta& meta);

#define ARC_REMOTE_COMMAND(ident, nameLit, summaryLit, access, mutates, play, thread, ArgsType, fn) \
    static const bool ident = ::Arcane::Arc::CommandRegistry::Get().Register( \
        ::Arcane::Arc::Desc{(nameLit), (summaryLit), (access), (mutates), (play), (thread), \
                            false, {}, &Astra::TypeMetaOf<ArgsType>(), (fn), nullptr}, nullptr)
}
```

`TypeMetaOf<T>()` returns `Astra::GetMeta<T>()`. `Register` returns false when `summary` is empty, `name` is empty or has an empty segment or a segment outside `[a-z0-9]+` split on `.`, the name is already registered, `argsMeta == nullptr`, or `access == Drive && mutates == None`.

`UnregisterModule("")` does **not** drop host commands (`module` empty). `UnregisterModule("fixture")` drops that module and increments `Generation()`. `Catalogue()` is `{"generation": N, "commands":[...]}` sorted by name. `Invoke` on an unknown name returns `MakeError(call.command, "command.unknown", ..., "arc commands")` and lists up to 8 sorted names that share the first segment in `candidates`. `Invoke` does not apply the state gate (Task 8 does) and does not check `readOnly`.

`SchemaFor` is ruling P2 (b). It calls `Astra::JsonSchemaGenerator` with default `Options` (`includeDefaults` stays false; the Astra string has no `"default"`). It parses that string with `nlohmann::json::parse`. When `meta.isDefaultConstructible && meta.defaultConstruct`, it placement-news one instance, walks `meta.fields`, and writes each `IsSerializable()` field with `Arcane::ReflectionJsonWriter` (`ReflectionJson.hpp:365` and `:369`). It then sets `"default"` on each property from that JSON, including `$defs` entries reached through `$ref`. A type that is not default-constructible gets no `"default"` keys and `defaultConstruct` is not called. After the defaults are stamped, a property whose field `typeHash` equals `Astra::TypeID<glm::vec2>::Hash()` (and vec3, vec4, quat, mat3, mat4) is rewritten to `{"type":"array","minItems":N,"maxItems":N,"items":{"type":"number"}}` where N is 2, 3, 4, 4, 9, 16, and an existing `"default"` is copied onto the rewritten object. Quat's 4 is `[x,y,z,w]`. The writer already emits that array (`WriteGlm` at `ReflectionJson.hpp:138`). Walk `meta.fields` and `properties` together; skip hidden fields on both sides.

**Default path (the lane check matched).** `SchemaFor` parses Astra's string and stops there for shape. It does not define `ApplySchemaAdapter`, `EmitDef`, or `FieldIsOptional`, and it does not rewrite `required` or `$ref`. Astra already omitted `Optional` fields from `required` and already emitted `"$ref": "#/$defs/<typeName>"` plus one top-level `$defs` object. A `$defs` entry is `type`, `properties`, `required`, `additionalProperties`, and it does not contain a `"$defs"` key. The Arcane code after the parse is only the `ReflectionJsonWriter` default stamp and the glm array rewrite. No other task edits Astra or grows a schema generator.

**Fallback sub-task (the lane check did not match). Execute this only then.** Today's `Generate` still lists every non-hidden field in `required`, still emits `"type":"object"` for a nested class, and still ignores `includeDefaults`. This sub-task wraps that current behaviour behind the same adapter the lane would have shipped. It is not part of the default path. No other task changes. Define this in `namespace Arcane::Attr` instead of `using ::Astra::Optional`, and do not edit the vendored header:

```cpp
struct Optional : ::Astra::AttributeBase<Optional> {
    constexpr Optional() noexcept = default;
};
```

`SchemaFor` detects it with `field.HasAttribute<Arcane::Attr::Optional>()`. After `nlohmann::json::parse` and before the `ReflectionJsonWriter` stamp, call `ApplySchemaAdapter`. The adapter (1) removes fields with that attribute from `required` and (2) rewrites a nested non-enum, non-glm field whose `GetMeta(typeHash)` is non-null into `"$ref": "#/$defs/<typeName>"`, emitting one top-level `$defs` object. The required list inside a def skips hidden fields and `Optional` fields. Walk pending defs by index, because writing a def appends its children. Defaults stay on the `ReflectionJsonWriter` path. glm fields are not refs; the default path's rewrite turns them into arrays. `IsGlmWire` is the helper the default path already has for that rewrite. The adapter code and its test are under **Fallback sub-task** after Step 3. Do not paste them into the default `SchemaFor`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement registry, schema defaults, and the `using`**

Default path only. Implement the registry rules in **Produces**. `List()` sorts by `name`. `SchemaFor` is this function. It does not hand-format scalars. The writer is the only producer of default JSON. `Schema.cpp` includes `<glm/vec2.hpp>`, `<glm/vec3.hpp>`, `<glm/vec4.hpp>`, `<glm/gtc/quaternion.hpp>`, `<glm/mat3x3.hpp>`, `<glm/mat4x4.hpp>`, and `Arcane/Reflection.hpp`. `Reflection.hpp` has `using ::Astra::Optional;`. There is no `ApplySchemaAdapter`, no `EmitDef`, and no `FieldIsOptional` on this path. Do not rewrite `required` or `$ref`.

```cpp
namespace {

bool IsGlmWire(std::uint64_t typeHash) {
    return typeHash == Astra::TypeID<glm::vec2>::Hash()
        || typeHash == Astra::TypeID<glm::vec3>::Hash()
        || typeHash == Astra::TypeID<glm::vec4>::Hash()
        || typeHash == Astra::TypeID<glm::quat>::Hash()
        || typeHash == Astra::TypeID<glm::mat3>::Hash()
        || typeHash == Astra::TypeID<glm::mat4>::Hash();
}

int GlmItemCount(std::uint64_t typeHash) {
    if (typeHash == Astra::TypeID<glm::vec2>::Hash()) return 2;
    if (typeHash == Astra::TypeID<glm::vec3>::Hash()) return 3;
    if (typeHash == Astra::TypeID<glm::vec4>::Hash()) return 4;
    if (typeHash == Astra::TypeID<glm::quat>::Hash()) return 4;
    if (typeHash == Astra::TypeID<glm::mat3>::Hash()) return 9;
    if (typeHash == Astra::TypeID<glm::mat4>::Hash()) return 16;
    return 0;
}

}  // namespace

nlohmann::json Arcane::Arc::SchemaFor(const Astra::TypeMeta& meta)
{
    Astra::JsonSchemaGenerator gen;
    nlohmann::json schema = nlohmann::json::parse(gen.Generate(meta));
    if (meta.isDefaultConstructible && meta.defaultConstruct)
    {
        void* instance = ::operator new(meta.size, std::align_val_t{meta.alignment});
        meta.defaultConstruct(instance);
        nlohmann::json written = nlohmann::json::object();
        Arcane::ReflectionJsonWriter writer(written);
        for (const Astra::FieldInfo& field : meta.fields)
        {
            if (!field.IsHidden() && field.IsSerializable())
                writer.Visit(field, instance);
        }
        if (meta.destruct)
            meta.destruct(instance);
        ::operator delete(instance, std::align_val_t{meta.alignment});

        auto stamp = [&](auto&& self, nlohmann::json& node, const nlohmann::json& value) -> void {
            if (!node.contains("properties") || !value.is_object())
                return;
            for (auto it = node["properties"].begin(); it != node["properties"].end(); ++it)
            {
                if (!value.contains(it.key()))
                    continue;
                (*it)["default"] = value[it.key()];
                if (!it->contains("$ref") || !value[it.key()].is_object() || !schema.contains("$defs"))
                    continue;
                const std::string ref = (*it)["$ref"].get<std::string>();
                const auto slash = ref.rfind('/');
                if (slash == std::string::npos)
                    continue;
                const std::string defName = ref.substr(slash + 1);
                if (schema["$defs"].contains(defName))
                    self(self, schema["$defs"][defName], value[it.key()]);
            }
        };
        stamp(stamp, schema, written);
    }
    if (schema.contains("properties") && schema["properties"].is_object()) {
        for (const Astra::FieldInfo& field : meta.fields) {
            if (field.IsHidden() || !IsGlmWire(field.typeHash))
                continue;
            const std::string fname(field.name);
            if (!schema["properties"].contains(fname) || !schema["properties"][fname].is_object())
                continue;
            auto& prop = schema["properties"][fname];
            nlohmann::json keptDefault = prop.contains("default") ? prop["default"] : nlohmann::json();
            const int n = GlmItemCount(field.typeHash);
            prop = nlohmann::json{{"type", "array"}, {"minItems", n}, {"maxItems", n},
                                  {"items", {{"type", "number"}}}};
            if (!keptDefault.is_null())
                prop["default"] = std::move(keptDefault);
        }
    }
    return schema;
}
```

`ArcSchemaNoDefault` has `isDefaultConstructible == false`, so the `if` does not run and the dump contains no `"default"`. `ArcSchemaOuter`'s `plain` default is 7 and `ArcSchemaInner.n` default is 4 because the writer read the live member initializers. Do not zero the instance before `defaultConstruct`. The glm loop runs after the stamp and keeps `"default"` when it replaces `"type"`. Quat's count is 4 and the writer's array is `[x,y,z,w]`.

#### Fallback sub-task: Arcane `$defs` emitter

Skip this entire sub-task when the lane check matched (`struct Optional` is in `ThirdParty/Astra/include/Astra/Reflection/Attribute.hpp`). On that path, copy the test case below into `RegistryTest.cpp` and do not copy the adapter. The case then passes because Astra already emitted `$ref`, one `$defs` object, and a `required` list without `Optional` fields. `Schema.cpp` still has no `ApplySchemaAdapter`.

Execute this sub-task only when `struct Optional` is absent. No other task changes. Add the local `Arcane::Attr::Optional` from **Interfaces**. Paste `FieldIsOptional`, `EmitDef`, and `ApplySchemaAdapter` into the anonymous namespace next to `IsGlmWire`. Call `ApplySchemaAdapter(schema, meta)` immediately after `nlohmann::json::parse` and before the default-construct stamp. Add the test case below to `RegistryTest.cpp`. These are this sub-task's tests. They fail on today's generator and pass once the adapter runs. Do not keep the adapter if a later sync adds `struct Optional`; delete the three functions, the call, and the local attribute, and leave the test.

```cpp
bool FieldIsOptional(const Astra::FieldInfo& field) {
    return field.HasAttribute<Arcane::Attr::Optional>();
}

void EmitDef(nlohmann::json& defs, const Astra::TypeMeta& meta,
             std::vector<const Astra::TypeMeta*>& pending) {
    const std::string name(meta.typeName);
    if (defs.contains(name))
        return;
    nlohmann::json def = nlohmann::json::object();
    def["type"] = "object";
    def["properties"] = nlohmann::json::object();
    def["additionalProperties"] = false;
    nlohmann::json required = nlohmann::json::array();
    for (const Astra::FieldInfo& field : meta.fields) {
        if (field.IsHidden())
            continue;
        const std::string fname(field.name);
        def["properties"][fname] = nlohmann::json::object();
        if (!FieldIsOptional(field))
            required.push_back(fname);
        const Astra::TypeMeta* nested = Astra::GetMeta(field.typeHash);
        if (nested == nullptr || nested->isEnum || IsGlmWire(field.typeHash) || nested == &meta)
            continue;
        def["properties"][fname]["$ref"] = std::string("#/$defs/") + std::string(nested->typeName);
        pending.push_back(nested);
    }
    if (!required.empty())
        def["required"] = std::move(required);
    defs[name] = std::move(def);
}

void ApplySchemaAdapter(nlohmann::json& schema, const Astra::TypeMeta& meta) {
    if (!schema.contains("properties") || !schema["properties"].is_object())
        schema["properties"] = nlohmann::json::object();
    if (!schema.contains("$defs") || !schema["$defs"].is_object())
        schema["$defs"] = nlohmann::json::object();
    nlohmann::json required = schema.contains("required") && schema["required"].is_array()
        ? schema["required"] : nlohmann::json::array();
    std::vector<const Astra::TypeMeta*> pending;
    for (const Astra::FieldInfo& field : meta.fields) {
        if (field.IsHidden())
            continue;
        const std::string fname(field.name);
        if (FieldIsOptional(field)) {
            nlohmann::json kept = nlohmann::json::array();
            for (const auto& item : required) {
                if (!item.is_string() || item.get<std::string>() != fname)
                    kept.push_back(item);
            }
            required = std::move(kept);
        }
        const Astra::TypeMeta* nested = Astra::GetMeta(field.typeHash);
        if (nested == nullptr || nested->isEnum || IsGlmWire(field.typeHash))
            continue;
        if (!schema["properties"].contains(fname) || !schema["properties"][fname].is_object())
            schema["properties"][fname] = nlohmann::json::object();
        auto& prop = schema["properties"][fname];
        if (!prop.contains("$ref"))
            prop["$ref"] = std::string("#/$defs/") + std::string(nested->typeName);
        prop.erase("type");
        pending.push_back(nested);
    }
    if (required.empty())
        schema.erase("required");
    else
        schema["required"] = std::move(required);
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < pending.size(); ++i)
        EmitDef(schema["$defs"], *pending[i], pending);
    if (schema["$defs"].empty())
        schema.erase("$defs");
}
```

```cpp
TEST_CASE("fallback adapter drops Optional from required and emits one $defs object", "[arc][registry]") {
    auto& reg = Arcane::Arc::CommandRegistry::Get();
    std::string err;
    Arcane::Arc::Desc ok;
    ok.name = "demo.opt";
    ok.summary = "optional field";
    ok.module = "fallback";
    ok.argsMeta = Astra::GetMeta<ArcDemoArgs>();
    ok.fn = &DemoFn;
    REQUIRE(reg.Register(ok, &err));
    const auto flat = reg.SchemaOf("demo.opt");
    if (flat.contains("required"))
        CHECK(flat["required"].dump().find("\"n\"") == std::string::npos);
    reg.UnregisterModule("fallback");
    CHECK(reg.Find("demo.opt") == nullptr);

    const auto schema = Arcane::Arc::SchemaFor(*Astra::GetMeta<ArcSchemaOuter>());
    CHECK(schema["properties"]["child"]["$ref"] == "#/$defs/ArcSchemaInner");
    CHECK(schema["$defs"]["ArcSchemaInner"]["properties"]["n"]["default"] == 4);
    CHECK_FALSE(schema["$defs"]["ArcSchemaInner"].contains("$defs"));
    const auto& innerReq = schema["$defs"]["ArcSchemaInner"];
    if (innerReq.contains("required"))
        CHECK(innerReq["required"].dump().find("\"n\"") != std::string::npos);
}
```

`ArcSchemaInner.n` stays in that def's `required` because the field is not `Optional`. `ArcDemoArgs.n` does not, because it is. The `$defs` object has no nested `"$defs"` key. On the default path this case is only the consumption check described above.

### Task 5: HTTP/1.1 parser and the s10.1 header checks

Parser only. Sockets, the peer check, and the idle close are Task 6. The token compare and the redaction helper live here so Task 6 and Task 14 share them.

**Files:**
- Create: `ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Arc/Http.hpp`
- Create: `ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Arc/Http.cpp`
- Test: `ArcaneTests/src/Arc/HttpTest.cpp`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `kMaxBodyBytes`, `kMaxHeaderBytes` (Task 3). `Crypto::GenerateSecureToken` (`Crypto.hpp:74`). `Crypto::HexEquals` (`Crypto.hpp:274`).
- Produces:

```cpp
namespace Arcane::Arc {

struct HttpRequest {
    std::string method;
    std::string target;
    std::string version;
    std::vector<std::pair<std::string, std::string>> headers; // names lowercased
    std::string body;
};

struct HttpResponse {
    int status = 200;
    std::string reason = "OK";
    std::vector<std::pair<std::string, std::string>> headers;
    std::string body;
    std::string Serialize() const;
};

enum class TransportRefuse { None, Origin, Host, Auth, Bounds, Chunked, Version, Throttled };

struct TransportCheck {
    TransportRefuse refuse = TransportRefuse::None;
    std::string code;       // auth.denied or auth.throttled
    std::string message;    // never contains the token
};

std::optional<HttpRequest> ParseHttpRequest(std::string_view bytes, std::string* error);
TransportCheck CheckTransport(const HttpRequest& req, std::string_view portDecimal,
                              std::string_view tokenHex);
std::string Header(const HttpRequest& req, std::string_view nameLower);
std::string RedactSecrets(std::string_view text, std::string_view tokenHex);
std::string MakeToken(); // GenerateSecureToken(32); 64 hex chars
}
```

`ParseHttpRequest` requires `HTTP/1.1`. It rejects `Transfer-Encoding: chunked` (`error = "chunked"`). `Content-Length` over `kMaxBodyBytes` or a header block over `kMaxHeaderBytes` returns nullopt with `error = "bounds"`. A short read returns nullopt with `error = "partial"`. Header names are stored lowercased. The body is the bytes after `\r\n\r\n`, length `Content-Length` (0 if absent). Non-JSON `Content-Type` on a POST with a body is `error = "content-type"` (the listener maps that to `arg.invalid`, exit 2, and does not queue).

`CheckTransport`, in order:

1. Any header named `origin` → `Origin`, code `auth.denied`, message `origin is not accepted`.
2. `Host` equals `127.0.0.1:` + `portDecimal` or `localhost:` + `portDecimal`, case-insensitive. Anything else → `Host`, `auth.denied`.
3. `Authorization` must be `Bearer <tokenHex>` with a single space. Compare with `HexEquals`. Mismatch or absence → `Auth`, `auth.denied`. Do not put the received token in `message`.

`RedactSecrets` replaces every occurrence of `tokenHex` with `<redacted>` and every `Bearer` + 64 hex chars with `Bearer <redacted>`.

```cpp
#include <catch2/catch_test_macros.hpp>
#include <rapidcheck/catch.h>
#include <Arcane/Arc/Http.hpp>

TEST_CASE("parser rejects HTTP/1.0, chunked, and an oversized body", "[arc][http]") {
    std::string err;
    auto v = Arcane::Arc::ParseHttpRequest("GET /arc/v1/status HTTP/1.0\r\nHost: 127.0.0.1:9\r\n\r\n", &err);
    CHECK_FALSE(v);
    CHECK(err == "version");
    auto c = Arcane::Arc::ParseHttpRequest(
        "POST /arc/v1/call HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: 127.0.0.1:9\r\nTransfer-Encoding: chunked\r\n\r\n", &err);
    CHECK_FALSE(c);
    CHECK(err == "chunked");
    std::string big = "POST /arc/v1/call HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: 127.0.0.1:9\r\nContent-Length: 5000000\r\n\r\n";
    auto b = Arcane::Arc::ParseHttpRequest(big, &err);
    CHECK_FALSE(b);
    CHECK(err == "bounds");
}

TEST_CASE("a good token does not survive Origin or a foreign Host", "[arc][http]") {
    const std::string token = Arcane::Arc::MakeToken();
    REQUIRE(token.size() == 64);
    std::string err;
    const std::string okRaw =
        "GET /arc/v1/status HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: 127.0.0.1:9\r\nAuthorization: Bearer " + token + "\r\n\r\n";
    auto ok = Arcane::Arc::ParseHttpRequest(okRaw, &err);
    REQUIRE(ok);
    CHECK(Arcane::Arc::CheckTransport(*ok, "9", token).refuse == Arcane::Arc::TransportRefuse::None);

    const std::string originRaw = okRaw.substr(0, okRaw.size() - 2) + "Origin: http://evil\r\n\r\n";
    auto withOrigin = Arcane::Arc::ParseHttpRequest(originRaw, &err);
    REQUIRE(withOrigin);
    auto origin = Arcane::Arc::CheckTransport(*withOrigin, "9", token);
    CHECK(origin.refuse == Arcane::Arc::TransportRefuse::Origin);
    CHECK(origin.code == "auth.denied");
    CHECK(origin.message.find(token) == std::string::npos);

    const std::string foreign =
        "GET /arc/v1/status HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: 10.0.0.8:9\r\nAuthorization: Bearer " + token + "\r\n\r\n";
    auto fr = Arcane::Arc::ParseHttpRequest(foreign, &err);
    REQUIRE(fr);
    CHECK(Arcane::Arc::CheckTransport(*fr, "9", token).refuse == Arcane::Arc::TransportRefuse::Host);

    const std::string local =
        "GET /arc/v1/status HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: localhost:9\r\nAuthorization: Bearer " + token + "\r\n\r\n";
    auto lh = Arcane::Arc::ParseHttpRequest(local, &err);
    REQUIRE(lh);
    CHECK(Arcane::Arc::CheckTransport(*lh, "9", token).refuse == Arcane::Arc::TransportRefuse::None);

    const std::string bad =
        "GET /arc/v1/status HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: 127.0.0.1:9\r\nAuthorization: Bearer " + std::string(64, 'a') + "\r\n\r\n";
    auto denied = Arcane::Arc::ParseHttpRequest(bad, &err);
    REQUIRE(denied);
    CHECK(Arcane::Arc::CheckTransport(*denied, "9", token).code == "auth.denied");
}

TEST_CASE("redaction removes the token and not the surrounding text", "[arc][http]") {
    const std::string token = Arcane::Arc::MakeToken();
    const std::string line = "Authorization: Bearer " + token + " host=127.0.0.1";
    const std::string out = Arcane::Arc::RedactSecrets(line, token);
    CHECK(out.find(token) == std::string::npos);
    CHECK(out.find("127.0.0.1") != std::string::npos);
    CHECK(out.find("<redacted>") != std::string::npos);
}

TEST_CASE("property: header order does not admit Origin", "[arc][http]") {
    rc::prop("origin anywhere is refused", [] {
        const std::string token = Arcane::Arc::MakeToken();
        const auto noise = *rc::gen::container<std::string>(rc::gen::inRange<char>('a', 'z'));
        const std::string raw =
            "GET /arc/v1/status HTTP/1.1\r\nX-Noise: " + noise +
            "\r\nOrigin: http://x\r\nHost: 127.0.0.1:9\r\nAuthorization: Bearer " + token + "\r\n\r\n";
        std::string err;
        auto req = Arcane::Arc::ParseHttpRequest(raw, &err);
        RC_ASSERT(req.has_value());
        RC_ASSERT(Arcane::Arc::CheckTransport(*req, "9", token).refuse == Arcane::Arc::TransportRefuse::Origin);
    });
}
```
