### Task 6: Loopback listener (s6.1 endpoints, peer refusal, 30 s idle close)

**Files:**
- Modify: `ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Net/TcpSocket.hpp` (add `SetNonBlocking` and `PollSockets` after `ConnectSocketWithTimeout`, which ends at line 393. Add `#include <span>`. On the POSIX branch add `#include <fcntl.h>`. Do not edit `ConnectSocketWithTimeout`.)
- Create: `ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Arc/Listener.hpp`
- Create: `ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Arc/Listener.cpp`
- Test: `ArcaneTests/src/Arc/ListenerTest.cpp` (the poll cases live here. `premake5.lua` globs `ArcaneTests/src/**.cpp`, so a second new test cpp would need another generate. Do not add one.)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tasks 3 and 5. `CreateListenSocket(uint16_t port, const char* bindAddr = nullptr)` (`TcpSocket.hpp:186`). `ServiceThread` (`ServiceThread.hpp:31`, `RequestStop` `:50`). A **dedicated** `RateLimiter` (`RateLimiter.hpp:26`), not `g_rateLimitingEnabled`. Config: `maxAttempts = 20`, `windowSeconds = 10`, `cooldownSeconds = 10` (use the real `RateLimiter::Config` field names at lines 55–60). Ruling P3 (c): one service thread, one poll loop.
- Produces:

```cpp
namespace Arcane::Arc {

bool PeerIsLoopback(const sockaddr_in& peer); // true only for 127.0.0.1

class RequestQueue {
public:
    explicit RequestQueue(std::size_t depth = kQueueDepthDefault);
    bool TryPush(std::uint64_t id, Call call);          // false when full; does not run the command
    bool TryPop(std::uint64_t& id, Call& call);
    void Complete(std::uint64_t id, CommandResult result);
    bool Wait(std::uint64_t id, CommandResult& out, std::chrono::milliseconds timeout);
    bool TryTake(std::uint64_t id, CommandResult& out); // never waits; false if that id is not complete
    std::uint64_t OldestPendingId() const;              // earliest TryPush id not yet Complete; 0 if none
    using WakeFn = void (*)(void* user);
    void SetWake(WakeFn fn, void* user);                // Complete calls fn after publishing; null is legal
    std::size_t Depth() const;
};

enum class SseLife : std::uint8_t { Opened, Closed };

struct SseEvent {
    std::uint64_t subscriberId = 0;
    std::string topic;             // the topics= query value, e.g. "logs"
    SseLife life = SseLife::Opened;
};

struct ListenerAtoms {
    std::atomic<std::shared_ptr<const HostStatus>> status;
    std::atomic<std::shared_ptr<const nlohmann::json>> catalog; // GET /catalog snapshot
    std::mutex sseMu;
    std::vector<SseEvent> sseEvents; // listener pushes; TakeSseEvents swaps
};

std::vector<SseEvent> TakeSseEvents(ListenerAtoms& atoms);

class LoopbackHttpTransport {
public:
    LoopbackHttpTransport(RequestQueue& queue, ListenerAtoms& atoms, std::string tokenHex,
                          std::chrono::milliseconds idleClose = std::chrono::seconds(kIdleConnectionSeconds));
    void Start();
    void Stop();
    std::uint16_t Port() const;
    std::uintptr_t ListenSocket() const;
    std::chrono::milliseconds IdleClose() const;
    using PeerPredicate = bool (*)(const sockaddr_in& peer, void* user);
    // Default predicate is PeerIsLoopback with a null user. The accept loop is the only caller.
    void SetPeerPredicate(PeerPredicate pred, void* user);
    bool EnqueueSse(std::uint64_t subscriberId, std::string_view data); // false if that subscriber is gone
};

class ArcListener {
public:
    ArcListener(RequestQueue& queue, ListenerAtoms& atoms, std::string tokenHex,
                std::chrono::milliseconds idleClose = std::chrono::seconds(kIdleConnectionSeconds));
    void Start();
    void Stop();
    std::uint16_t Port() const;
    std::uintptr_t ListenSocket() const;
    const std::string& Token() const;
    std::chrono::milliseconds IdleClose() const;
    ListenerAtoms& Atoms();
    LoopbackHttpTransport& Transport();
    void SetPeerPredicate(LoopbackHttpTransport::PeerPredicate pred, void* user);
    bool EnqueueSse(std::uint64_t subscriberId, std::string_view data);
};
}
```

`Start` calls `InitSockets()` then `CreateListenSocket(0, "127.0.0.1")`, then `SetNonBlocking` on that listen socket. `getsockname` is how `Port()` learns the port. It also opens a loopback wake pair (listen on `127.0.0.1:0`, connect, accept, close the temporary listener) and `SetNonBlocking` on both ends. `RequestQueue::SetWake` is installed so `Complete` sends one byte on the write end. A `send` that returns `WSAEWOULDBLOCK` / `EAGAIN` means the listener is already due to wake; ignore that error. Production hosts use the default `idleClose`, which is `kIdleConnectionSeconds` (30). Tests may pass a shorter duration; no production call site passes one.

One `ServiceThread` runs the loop below. It is the only thread that accepts, reads, and writes these sockets. It never calls `RequestQueue::Wait`, `Invoke`, or `CommandRegistry`.

```cpp
enum class ConnState : std::uint8_t { Reading, Parked, Streaming, Sending, Closing };

struct Conn {
    SocketType sock = INVALID_SOCK;
    ConnState state = ConnState::Reading;
    std::string inbuf;
    std::string outbuf;
    std::uint64_t callId = 0;
    std::uint64_t subscriberId = 0;
    std::string sseTopic;
    std::chrono::steady_clock::time_point lastRead{};
};
```

Each iteration builds a `std::vector<PollInterest>`: the listen socket `wantRead`, the wake socket `wantRead`, and each connection. A connection in `Reading`, `Parked`, or `Streaming` sets `wantRead`. A connection with a non-empty `outbuf` sets `wantWrite`. `PollSockets(fds, 50)` is the wait. `50` is milliseconds, so `Stop` and the idle clock are noticed without a socket event.

When the listen socket is readable, `accept`. `WSAEWOULDBLOCK` / `EAGAIN` means the poll raced and there is no socket; leave the loop running. Any other accept error is logged and the loop continues. The accept loop, and only the accept loop, does this on that new socket:

1. `getpeername` on **that accepted socket** into a `sockaddr_in`. Call `m_peerPred(peer, m_peerUser)`. The default predicate is `PeerIsLoopback`. If `getpeername` fails or the predicate returns false, close **the accepted socket**, log `peer refused` plus the numeric address through `RedactSecrets`, and do not read it. Do not queue. Tests inject the predicate with `SetPeerPredicate` before `Start`. They do not call an accept helper with the client socket.
2. Otherwise `SetNonBlocking` and push a `Conn` in `Reading` with `lastRead = now`.

When a `Reading` connection is readable, `recv` appends to `inbuf`. `n == 0` or a hard error moves it to `Closing`. A non-blocking `WSAEWOULDBLOCK` / `EAGAIN` leaves the buffer and stays `Reading`. When `ParseHttpRequest` returns a request, run `CheckTransport` on that connection. Origin or a `Host` that is not `127.0.0.1:<port>` or `localhost:<port>` → append HTTP 403, body `MakeError("", "auth.denied", ...)`, exit derived as 3, state `Sending`. These do not consume the auth budget. Auth failure → `RateLimiter::Allow("arc-auth")`. While `Allow` is true, HTTP 401, code `auth.denied`. When `Allow` is false, HTTP 429, code `auth.throttled`. Neither message contains the token. A forged `Host` is refused even when the bearer is valid and no `Origin` header is present. State becomes `Sending`.

The idle clock runs only while `state == Reading`. If `now - lastRead >= idleClose`, close the socket and drop the `Conn`. `Parked` and `Streaming` are not idle-closed: a `/call` that takes longer than 30 s, and an SSE connection that is quiet, stay open. The 200 ms test connects and sends nothing, so it stays `Reading` and still closes (`recv` returns 0).

Routes, after the checks. Every JSON response is `Content-Type: application/json`, `Content-Length` set to the body size, and `X-Arc-Protocol: 1`. The bytes are appended to `outbuf` and the state becomes `Sending`. Nothing in this thread blocks in `send`.

- `GET /arc/v1/status` — answered inline on this thread from the status `shared_ptr` via `ToJson`. Does not touch the queue. This runs while another connection is `Parked`.
- `GET /arc/v1/catalog` — answered inline from the catalog `shared_ptr`. Does not call `CommandRegistry`.
- `GET /arc/v1/stream?topics=` — SSE. Topics P0 serves: `logs`, `problems`, `state`, `catalog`, `job:<id>`. `watch:` is refused with `arg.inapplicable` exit 2 (public watch is P3). Counts toward `kMaxSseSubscribers` (8); the 9th gets `host.busy` / `detail.state = "connections"` / exit 5 and is not held open. A kept stream appends `HTTP/1.1 200` with `Content-Type: text/event-stream` and moves to `Streaming`. The listener assigns a monotonic subscriber id, pushes `SseEvent{id, topic, Opened}` under `sseMu`, and on socket close (recv 0, error, or `Stop`) pushes `SseEvent{id, topic, Closed}` and drops the socket. It does not arm a job. `EnqueueSse(id, data)` appends `data: <payload>\n\n` to that connection's `outbuf` under the connection mutex and sends one wake byte, or returns false when the id is already closed. `TakeSseEvents` swaps the vector out under the mutex. Follow jobs are Task 22.
- `POST /arc/v1/call` — body is s6.2: `{id, command, args, options:{world, readOnly, timeoutMs}}`. Unknown keys → `arg.unknown` exit 2, not queued. Missing `command` → `arg.missing` exit 2, not queued. A `protocol` member other than 1 → `protocol.mismatch` exit 4, not queued. Absent `protocol` means 1. Otherwise assign a monotonic `std::uint64_t` starting at 1 (the JSON `id` string is copied onto `Call::id` and is not the queue key) and `TryPush` that key. On false: HTTP 200, `host.busy`, `detail.state = "queue-full"`, exit 5, command not called, state `Sending`. On true: store the key on `Conn::callId`, set state `Parked`, and write no response yet. `TryPush` and that state assignment finish before the service thread returns to `PollSockets`, so `Depth() == 1` means the connection is already `Parked`. Do not call `Wait`. The listener thread does not call `Invoke`. Copy a present `options.timeoutMs` integer onto `Call::timeoutMs`. Absent `options` or absent `timeoutMs` leaves 30000. That integer is already milliseconds. The listener does not multiply it, and neither does `host.wait` when it copies `Call::timeoutMs` into `WaitArgs::timeoutMs` (Task 16).
- `POST /arc/v1/batch` and any other path → `command.unknown` exit 2. Do not implement batch.

When the wake socket is readable, `recv` until it would block, then for each `Parked` connection call `TryTake(callId, result)`. True means the main thread has `Complete`d that id (or the job finish path has). Append the HTTP 200 body `ToJson(result.envelope)` and set state `Sending`. False leaves the connection `Parked`. Task 29's `"step 10 advances host.frame by exactly 10 while status still answers"` is this same shape on a live job. Do not remove that test.

`Sending` with `wantWrite`: `send` what the socket accepts, erase that prefix, and on `WSAEWOULDBLOCK` / `EAGAIN` keep the rest. When `outbuf` is empty and the state is `Sending`, a JSON response goes back to `Reading` and refreshes `lastRead` (keep-alive). A `Streaming` connection stays `Streaming` with the socket open.

Connection cap `kMaxConnections` (16): the 17th is accepted and answered `host.busy` / `state:"connections"` / exit 5, then closed. `Stop` calls `RequestStop`, closes the listen socket, and `Complete`s every parked id with `MakeError("", "host.died", "listener stopped", "relaunch")` so the wake loop writes exit 7. A client drop while `Parked` (`recv` returns 0) closes that connection; a later `Complete` for its id finds no `Conn` and the result is discarded.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

`SocketType` and `CloseSocket` are macros in `TcpSocket.hpp`. Do not write `Arcane::Net::SocketType`.

```cpp
#include <catch2/catch_test_macros.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Arc/Listener.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Arc/Protocol.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Net/TcpSocket.hpp>
#include <chrono>
#include <span>
#include <thread>

static std::string HttpExchangeHost(std::uint16_t port, const std::string& token,
                                    const std::string& hostHeader, const std::string& extraHeaders,
                                    const std::string& requestLine, const std::string& body = {}) {
    REQUIRE(Arcane::InitSockets());
    const SocketType sock = ::socket(AF_INET, SOCK_STREAM, IPPROTO_TCP);
    REQUIRE(sock != INVALID_SOCK);
    sockaddr_in addr{};
    addr.sin_family = AF_INET;
    addr.sin_port = htons(port);
    inet_pton(AF_INET, "127.0.0.1", &addr.sin_addr);
    REQUIRE(::connect(sock, reinterpret_cast<sockaddr*>(&addr), sizeof(addr)) == 0);
    std::string req = requestLine + "\r\nHost: " + hostHeader +
        "\r\nAuthorization: Bearer " + token + "\r\n";
    if (!body.empty()) {
        req += "Content-Type: application/json\r\nContent-Length: " + std::to_string(body.size()) + "\r\n";
    }
    req += extraHeaders;
    req += "\r\n";
    req += body;
    REQUIRE(::send(sock, req.data(), static_cast<int>(req.size()), 0) == static_cast<int>(req.size()));
    std::string raw;
    char buf[4096];
    for (;;) {
        const int n = ::recv(sock, buf, sizeof(buf), 0);
        if (n <= 0) break;
        raw.append(buf, buf + n);
        const auto hdr = raw.find("\r\n\r\n");
        if (hdr == std::string::npos) continue;
        const auto key = raw.find("Content-Length:");
        if (key == std::string::npos || key > hdr) break;
        const int len = std::atoi(raw.c_str() + key + 15);
        if (static_cast<int>(raw.size() - (hdr + 4)) >= len) break;
    }
    CloseSocket(sock);
    return raw;
}

static std::string HttpExchange(std::uint16_t port, const std::string& token,
                                const std::string& extraHeaders, const std::string& requestLine,
                                const std::string& body = {}) {
    return HttpExchangeHost(port, token, "127.0.0.1:" + std::to_string(port),
                            extraHeaders, requestLine, body);
}

TEST_CASE("the listen socket is bound to 127.0.0.1", "[arc][listener]") {
    Arcane::Arc::RequestQueue queue;
    Arcane::Arc::ListenerAtoms atoms;
    atoms.status.store(std::make_shared<const Arcane::Arc::HostStatus>());
    Arcane::Arc::ArcListener listener(queue, atoms, Arcane::Arc::MakeToken());
    listener.Start();
    sockaddr_in bound{};
    int len = sizeof(bound);
    REQUIRE(getsockname(static_cast<SOCKET>(listener.ListenSocket()),
                        reinterpret_cast<sockaddr*>(&bound), &len) == 0);
    char text[64] = {};
    inet_ntop(AF_INET, &bound.sin_addr, text, sizeof(text));
    CHECK(std::string(text) == "127.0.0.1");
    CHECK(listener.Port() == ntohs(bound.sin_port));
    CHECK(listener.Port() != 0);
    CHECK(listener.IdleClose() == std::chrono::seconds(Arcane::Arc::kIdleConnectionSeconds));
    CHECK(Arcane::Arc::kIdleConnectionSeconds == 30);
    listener.Stop();
}

TEST_CASE("status requires the bearer and answers from the atomic", "[arc][listener]") {
    Arcane::Arc::RequestQueue queue;
    Arcane::Arc::ListenerAtoms atoms;

    snap->state = "ready";

    snap->hostKind = Arcane::Arc::HostKind::Runtime;
    atoms.status.store(snap);
    Arcane::Arc::ArcListener listener(queue, atoms, Arcane::Arc::MakeToken());
    listener.Start();
    const std::string body = HttpExchange(listener.Port(), listener.Token(), "", "GET /arc/v1/status HTTP/1.1");
    CHECK(body.find("\"state\":\"ready\"") != std::string::npos);
    CHECK(body.find("\"frame\":4") != std::string::npos);
    CHECK(body.find(listener.Token()) == std::string::npos);
    const std::string denied = HttpExchange(listener.Port(), std::string(64, '0'), "", "GET /arc/v1/status HTTP/1.1");
    CHECK(denied.find("auth.denied") != std::string::npos);
    CHECK(denied.find("auth.rejected") == std::string::npos);

    CHECK(Arcane::Arc::ExitFromHost(env) == 3);
    listener.Stop();
}

    Arcane::Arc::RequestQueue queue;

    listener.Start();
    const std::string port = std::to_string(listener.Port());
    const std::string forged = HttpExchangeHost(listener.Port(), listener.Token(),
                                                "10.0.0.8:" + port, "",
                                                "GET /arc/v1/status HTTP/1.1");
    CHECK(forged.find("403") != std::string::npos);
    CHECK(forged.find("auth.denied") != std::string::npos);

    CHECK(forged.find("Origin") == std::string::npos);
    CHECK(queue.Depth() == 0);
    const std::string ok = HttpExchangeHost(listener.Port(), listener.Token(),
                                            "127.0.0.1:" + port, "",
                                            "GET /arc/v1/status HTTP/1.1");
    CHECK(ok.find("\"state\":\"ready\"") != std::string::npos);
    listener.Stop();
}

struct ArcSeenPeer {
    sockaddr_in peer{};
    int calls = 0;
};

static bool ArcRefusePeer(const sockaddr_in& peer, void* user) {
    auto* seen = static_cast<ArcSeenPeer*>(user);
    seen->peer = peer;
    ++seen->calls;
    return false;
}

TEST_CASE("the accept loop closes a peer the predicate refuses", "[arc][listener]") {
    sockaddr_in forged{};
    forged.sin_family = AF_INET;
    inet_pton(AF_INET, "10.0.0.8", &forged.sin_addr);
    CHECK_FALSE(Arcane::Arc::PeerIsLoopback(forged));
    sockaddr_in loop{};
    loop.sin_family = AF_INET;
    inet_pton(AF_INET, "127.0.0.1", &loop.sin_addr);
    CHECK(Arcane::Arc::PeerIsLoopback(loop));

    Arcane::Arc::RequestQueue queue;
    Arcane::Arc::ListenerAtoms atoms;
    auto snap = std::make_shared<Arcane::Arc::HostStatus>();
    snap->state = "ready";
    atoms.status.store(snap);
    Arcane::Arc::ArcListener listener(queue, atoms, Arcane::Arc::MakeToken());
    ArcSeenPeer seen{};
    listener.SetPeerPredicate(&ArcRefusePeer, &seen);
    listener.Start();
    REQUIRE(Arcane::InitSockets());
    const SocketType client = ::socket(AF_INET, SOCK_STREAM, IPPROTO_TCP);
    sockaddr_in addr{};
    addr.sin_family = AF_INET;
    addr.sin_port = htons(listener.Port());
    inet_pton(AF_INET, "127.0.0.1", &addr.sin_addr);
    REQUIRE(::connect(client, reinterpret_cast<sockaddr*>(&addr), sizeof(addr)) == 0);
    const std::string req = "GET /arc/v1/status HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: 127.0.0.1:" +
        std::to_string(listener.Port()) + "\r\nAuthorization: Bearer " + listener.Token() + "\r\n\r\n";
    REQUIRE(::send(client, req.data(), static_cast<int>(req.size()), 0) == static_cast<int>(req.size()));
    const DWORD timeoutMs = 2000;
    ::setsockopt(client, SOL_SOCKET, SO_RCVTIMEO, reinterpret_cast<const char*>(&timeoutMs), sizeof(timeoutMs));
    char buf[512];
    const int n = ::recv(client, buf, sizeof(buf), 0);
    std::string raw = n > 0 ? std::string(buf, buf + n) : std::string();
    CHECK(seen.calls == 1);
    char text[64] = {};
    inet_ntop(AF_INET, &seen.peer.sin_addr, text, sizeof(text));
    CHECK(std::string(text) == "127.0.0.1");
    CHECK(n == 0);
    CHECK(raw.find("200") == std::string::npos);
    CHECK(raw.find("ready") == std::string::npos);
    CHECK(queue.Depth() == 0);
    CloseSocket(client);
    listener.Stop();
}

TEST_CASE("POST /arc/v1/call reads command and the 65th is queue-full", "[arc][listener]") {
    Arcane::Arc::RequestQueue queue(64);
    Arcane::Arc::ListenerAtoms atoms;
    atoms.status.store(std::make_shared<const Arcane::Arc::HostStatus>());
    atoms.catalog.store(std::make_shared<const nlohmann::json>(nlohmann::json::object()));
    Arcane::Arc::ArcListener listener(queue, atoms, Arcane::Arc::MakeToken());
    listener.Start();
    for (std::uint64_t i = 1; i <= 64; ++i) {
        Arcane::Arc::Call call;
        call.id = std::to_string(i);
        call.command = "demo.look";
        REQUIRE(queue.TryPush(i, call));
    }
    const std::string payload = R"({"id":"r-65","command":"demo.look","args":{}})";
    const std::string body = HttpExchange(listener.Port(), listener.Token(), "",
                                          "POST /arc/v1/call HTTP/1.1", payload);
    CHECK(body.find("\"code\":\"host.busy\"") != std::string::npos);
    CHECK(body.find("queue-full") != std::string::npos);
    CHECK(body.find("\"command\"") != std::string::npos);
    CHECK(queue.Depth() == 64);
    const auto env = Arcane::Arc::ParseEnvelope(nlohmann::json::parse(body.substr(body.find("\r\n\r\n") + 4)));
    CHECK(Arcane::Arc::ExitFromHost(env) == 5);
    const std::string catalog = HttpExchange(listener.Port(), listener.Token(), "", "GET /arc/v1/catalog HTTP/1.1");
    CHECK(catalog.find("200") != std::string::npos);
    listener.Stop();
}

TEST_CASE("PollSockets reports one readable socket and times out the idle one", "[arc][listener]") {
    REQUIRE(Arcane::InitSockets());
    const SocketType listen = Arcane::CreateListenSocket(0, "127.0.0.1");
    REQUIRE(listen != INVALID_SOCK);
    sockaddr_in bound{};
    int len = sizeof(bound);
    REQUIRE(::getsockname(listen, reinterpret_cast<sockaddr*>(&bound), &len) == 0);

    Arcane::PollInterest before[1];
    before[0].socket = listen;
    before[0].wantRead = true;
    CHECK(Arcane::PollSockets(before, 0) == 0);
    CHECK_FALSE(before[0].readable);

    const SocketType client = ::socket(AF_INET, SOCK_STREAM, IPPROTO_TCP);
    REQUIRE(client != INVALID_SOCK);
    REQUIRE(::connect(client, reinterpret_cast<sockaddr*>(&bound), sizeof(bound)) == 0);
    Arcane::PollInterest listening[1];
    listening[0].socket = listen;
    listening[0].wantRead = true;
    CHECK(Arcane::PollSockets(listening, 200) == 1);
    CHECK(listening[0].readable);

    sockaddr_in peerAddr{};
    int peerLen = sizeof(peerAddr);
    const SocketType accepted = ::accept(listen, reinterpret_cast<sockaddr*>(&peerAddr), &peerLen);
    REQUIRE(accepted != INVALID_SOCK);
    REQUIRE(Arcane::SetNonBlocking(accepted, true));
    REQUIRE(Arcane::SetNonBlocking(client, true));
    const char byte = 'Q';
    REQUIRE(::send(client, &byte, 1, 0) == 1);

    const SocketType idleListen = Arcane::CreateListenSocket(0, "127.0.0.1");
    REQUIRE(idleListen != INVALID_SOCK);
    sockaddr_in idleBound{};
    int idleLen = sizeof(idleBound);
    REQUIRE(::getsockname(idleListen, reinterpret_cast<sockaddr*>(&idleBound), &idleLen) == 0);
    const SocketType idleClient = ::socket(AF_INET, SOCK_STREAM, IPPROTO_TCP);
    REQUIRE(::connect(idleClient, reinterpret_cast<sockaddr*>(&idleBound), sizeof(idleBound)) == 0);
    sockaddr_in idlePeer{};
    int idlePeerLen = sizeof(idlePeer);
    const SocketType idleAccepted = ::accept(idleListen, reinterpret_cast<sockaddr*>(&idlePeer), &idlePeerLen);
    REQUIRE(idleAccepted != INVALID_SOCK);
    REQUIRE(Arcane::SetNonBlocking(idleAccepted, true));

    Arcane::PollInterest both[2];
    both[0].socket = accepted;
    both[0].wantRead = true;
    both[1].socket = idleAccepted;
    both[1].wantRead = true;
    const int ready = Arcane::PollSockets(both, 200);
    CHECK(ready >= 1);
    CHECK(both[0].readable);
    CHECK_FALSE(both[1].readable);
    char got = 0;
    REQUIRE(::recv(accepted, &got, 1, 0) == 1);
    CHECK(got == 'Q');
    char extra = 0;
    const int again = ::recv(accepted, &extra, 1, 0);
    CHECK(again < 0);
#ifdef _WIN32
    CHECK(WSAGetLastError() == WSAEWOULDBLOCK);
#else
    CHECK(errno == EAGAIN || errno == EWOULDBLOCK);
#endif

    Arcane::PollInterest idleOnly[1];
    idleOnly[0].socket = idleAccepted;
    idleOnly[0].wantRead = true;
    const auto t0 = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
    const int idleRc = Arcane::PollSockets(idleOnly, 50);
    const auto ms = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(
        std::chrono::steady_clock::now() - t0).count();
    CHECK(idleRc == 0);
    CHECK_FALSE(idleOnly[0].readable);
    CHECK(ms >= 40);
    CHECK(ms < 500);

    CloseSocket(client);
    CloseSocket(accepted);
    CloseSocket(listen);
    CloseSocket(idleClient);
    CloseSocket(idleAccepted);
    CloseSocket(idleListen);
}

TEST_CASE("a parked call does not stop status", "[arc][listener]") {
    Arcane::Arc::RequestQueue queue;
    Arcane::Arc::ListenerAtoms atoms;
    auto snap = std::make_shared<Arcane::Arc::HostStatus>();
    snap->state = "ready";
    atoms.status.store(snap);
    Arcane::Arc::ArcListener listener(queue, atoms, Arcane::Arc::MakeToken());
    listener.Start();
    REQUIRE(Arcane::InitSockets());
    const SocketType caller = ::socket(AF_INET, SOCK_STREAM, IPPROTO_TCP);
    sockaddr_in addr{};
    addr.sin_family = AF_INET;
    addr.sin_port = htons(listener.Port());
    inet_pton(AF_INET, "127.0.0.1", &addr.sin_addr);
    REQUIRE(::connect(caller, reinterpret_cast<sockaddr*>(&addr), sizeof(addr)) == 0);
    const std::string payload = R"({"id":"r-park","command":"demo.look","args":{}})";
    const std::string req = "POST /arc/v1/call HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: 127.0.0.1:" +
        std::to_string(listener.Port()) + "\r\nAuthorization: Bearer " + listener.Token() +
        "\r\nContent-Type: application/json\r\nContent-Length: " + std::to_string(payload.size()) +
        "\r\n\r\n" + payload;
    REQUIRE(::send(caller, req.data(), static_cast<int>(req.size()), 0) == static_cast<int>(req.size()));
    const auto deadline = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + std::chrono::seconds(2);
    while (queue.Depth() == 0 && std::chrono::steady_clock::now() < deadline)
        std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(10));
    REQUIRE(queue.Depth() == 1);
    const std::string status = HttpExchange(listener.Port(), listener.Token(), "",
                                            "GET /arc/v1/status HTTP/1.1");
    CHECK(status.find("200") != std::string::npos);
    CHECK(status.find("\"state\":\"ready\"") != std::string::npos);
    CHECK(queue.Depth() == 1);
    Arcane::Arc::CommandResult done;
    done.envelope = Arcane::Arc::MakeOk("demo.look", nlohmann::json::object(), {});
    const std::uint64_t parked = queue.OldestPendingId();
    REQUIRE(parked != 0);
    queue.Complete(parked, done);
    const DWORD timeoutMs = 2000;
    ::setsockopt(caller, SOL_SOCKET, SO_RCVTIMEO, reinterpret_cast<const char*>(&timeoutMs), sizeof(timeoutMs));
    std::string raw;
    char buf[4096];
    const auto readDeadline = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + std::chrono::seconds(2);
    while (raw.find("demo.look") == std::string::npos && std::chrono::steady_clock::now() < readDeadline) {
        const int n = ::recv(caller, buf, sizeof(buf), 0);
        if (n > 0) raw.append(buf, buf + n);
        else break;
    }
    CHECK(raw.find("200") != std::string::npos);
    CHECK(raw.find("demo.look") != std::string::npos);
    CloseSocket(caller);
    listener.Stop();
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run the test to verify it fails**

Generate, Debug build, filter `[arc][listener]`. Expected: FAIL to compile. `PollSockets` and `SetNonBlocking` are not declared yet.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the poll primitives and the one-thread listener**

Add these after `ConnectSocketWithTimeout` (`TcpSocket.hpp:393`). Do not change that function. `#include <span>` at the top. POSIX also needs `#include <fcntl.h>`.

```cpp
struct PollInterest {
    SocketType socket = INVALID_SOCK;
    bool wantRead = false;
    bool wantWrite = false;
    bool readable = false;
    bool writable = false;
    bool hungup = false;
};

inline bool SetNonBlocking(SocketType sock, bool nonBlocking)
{
#ifdef _WIN32
    u_long mode = nonBlocking ? 1u : 0u;
    return ::ioctlsocket(sock, FIONBIO, &mode) == 0;
#else
    const int flags = ::fcntl(sock, F_GETFL, 0);
    if (flags < 0)
        return false;
    const int next = nonBlocking ? (flags | O_NONBLOCK) : (flags & ~O_NONBLOCK);
    return ::fcntl(sock, F_SETFL, next) == 0;
#endif
}

inline int PollSockets(std::span<PollInterest> fds, int timeoutMs)
{
    fd_set readSet, writeSet, errSet;
    FD_ZERO(&readSet);
    FD_ZERO(&writeSet);
    FD_ZERO(&errSet);
    int maxFd = 0;
    for (PollInterest& fd : fds) {
        fd.readable = false;
        fd.writable = false;
        fd.hungup = false;
        if (fd.socket == INVALID_SOCK)
            continue;
        if (fd.wantRead)
            FD_SET(fd.socket, &readSet);
        if (fd.wantWrite)
            FD_SET(fd.socket, &writeSet);
        FD_SET(fd.socket, &errSet);
        if (static_cast<int>(fd.socket) > maxFd)
            maxFd = static_cast<int>(fd.socket);
    }
    timeval tv{};
    timeval* tvp = nullptr;
    if (timeoutMs >= 0) {
        tv.tv_sec = timeoutMs / 1000;
        tv.tv_usec = (timeoutMs % 1000) * 1000;
        tvp = &tv;
    }
    const int sel = ::select(maxFd + 1, &readSet, &writeSet, &errSet, tvp);
    if (sel <= 0)
        return sel;
    int count = 0;
    for (PollInterest& fd : fds) {
        if (fd.socket == INVALID_SOCK)
            continue;
        fd.readable = FD_ISSET(fd.socket, &readSet) != 0;
        fd.writable = FD_ISSET(fd.socket, &writeSet) != 0;
        fd.hungup = FD_ISSET(fd.socket, &errSet) != 0;
        if (fd.readable || fd.writable || fd.hungup)
            ++count;
    }
    return count;
}
```

`timeoutMs < 0` waits until a socket is ready (`tvp` is null). `0` polls. A positive value waits that many milliseconds. Return `-1` on `select` error, `0` on timeout, otherwise the number of interests that became readable, writable, or hung up. Windows ignores the `nfds` argument; pass `maxFd + 1` anyway, the same way `ConnectSocketWithTimeout` passes `sock + 1` at line 341. `select` clears and the loop above refills `readable` / `writable` / `hungup` from `FD_ISSET`.

The listener is the state machine in **Interfaces**. Bind `"127.0.0.1"` only. Log every refusal through `RedactSecrets` and `Log::Engine()->info`. `TryTake` returns false immediately when that id has no published result. `Wait` stays for tests and other callers; a timeout of 0 returns immediately too, and the service thread still does not call it. `Complete` stores the result, then calls the wake function if one was set. `OldestPendingId` is the smallest key passed to `TryPush` that `Complete` has not yet been called with. The peer predicate runs on the address `getpeername` filled from the accepted socket. `CheckTransport`'s Host refusal is what answers the forged-Host test; deleting that branch turns the forged request into `"state":"ready"`. Deleting the inline `/status` path, or answering `/status` only after `Wait` returns, fails `"a parked call does not stop status"`.

The injected predicate is called once with `127.0.0.1` and the accepted socket is closed (`recv` returns 0) with queue depth 0. The 200 ms idle socket reads 0. The default `IdleClose()` on the first test is 30 seconds. The log subscriber never sees the token. The catalog GET is 200. `PollSockets` on the idle accepted socket returns 0 in at least 40 ms and under 500 ms, and the socket that was sent `'Q'` is readable. While a `POST /arc/v1/call` is parked (`Depth() == 1` and nobody has `Complete`d it), `GET /arc/v1/status` returns 200 and `"state":"ready"`. After `Complete(OldestPendingId())`, the parked client reads a 200 body containing `demo.look`.

**Interfaces:**

- Produces:

```cpp
namespace Arcane::EditorLock {
ARC_CORE_API bool IsLive(const Info& info);
}

namespace Arcane::Arc {
struct Descriptor {
    std::uint32_t protocol = kProtocol;
    HostKind hostKind = HostKind::Editor;
    std::uint32_t pid = 0;
    std::uint64_t processStart = 0;
    std::uint16_t port = 0;
    std::string token;
    std::string project;
    std::int64_t heartbeatUnixMs = 0;
    std::string state;
    std::uint64_t frame = 0;
};

std::filesystem::path DescriptorPath(const std::filesystem::path& projectRoot, HostKind kind, std::uint32_t pid);
std::filesystem::path DescriptorDir(const std::filesystem::path& projectRoot, bool projectless);
bool WriteDescriptorAtomic(const std::filesystem::path& path, const Descriptor& desc);
std::optional<Descriptor> ReadDescriptor(const std::filesystem::path& path);
void TouchHeartbeat(const std::filesystem::path& path, std::int64_t unixMs, std::string state, std::uint64_t frame);
bool IsHung(const Descriptor& desc, std::int64_t nowUnixMs); // heartbeat older than 10s
bool IsStale(const Descriptor& desc); // !EditorLock::IsLive({pid, processStart})
}
```

`DescriptorPath` = `<projectRoot>/Saved/arc/<hostKind>-<pid>.json` with `hostKind` `editor`, `runtime`, or `server`. Projectless dir is `Paths::Get(Location::EditorUserDir) / "arc"`.

`WriteDescriptorAtomic` writes `path + ".tmp"`, applies SDDL `D:P(A;;FA;;;OW)(A;;FA;;;SY)` to the temp file **before** `MoveFileExW(..., MOVEFILE_REPLACE_EXISTING)`, then applies it again to the final path. Failure deletes the temp and returns false. POSIX `#else` uses `fchmod` 0600 before rename. Do not call `EditorLock::ReadLive` on this file.

`IsLive` is the three tells already in `ReadLive` (`Project.cpp:807-837`): `OpenProcess`, `GetProcessTimes` creation equals `info.start` when `info.start != 0`, and the exit time is zero. A start flipped by 1 is not live. `ReadLive` parses the lock file and then returns `IsLive(*info) ? pid : nullopt`. Non-Windows `IsLive` returns false. `IsHung` is `nowUnixMs - heartbeatUnixMs > 10000`. A hung descriptor is reported, not deleted. The token is in the file and is not logged by `WriteDescriptorAtomic`. `ToJson` of a status snapshot has no token; do not add one.

### Task 8: Dispatcher — budget, audit, and one job step per drain

**Files:**
- Create: `ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Arc/Dispatcher.hpp`
- Create: `ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Arc/Dispatcher.cpp`
- Modify: `ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Crypto/Crypto.hpp` (add `Sha256Hex` next to `HmacSha256Hex`, line 264)
- Test: `ArcaneTests/src/Arc/DispatcherTest.cpp`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `RequestQueue` (Task 6), `CommandRegistry::Invoke` (Task 4), `Envelope` (Task 3), `JobTable::StepAll` (Task 9 — declare the call here; Task 9 defines the table. If you implement Task 8 first, `StepAll` is a function on a `JobTable&` the dispatcher holds, and Task 9 fills the body. The test below constructs a `JobTable`.)
- Produces:

```cpp
namespace Arcane::Arc {

struct HostGate {
    std::atomic<bool> minimized{false};
    std::atomic<bool> readOnly{false};
    std::atomic<std::uint64_t> frame{0};
    std::atomic<std::int64_t> heartbeatUnixMs{0};
    std::string state = "ready";
    std::mutex stateMutex;
    std::string world;
    std::uint32_t pid = 0;
    std::string kind = "editor";
    std::uint32_t catalog = 0;
    std::uint32_t pluginGeneration = 0;
    std::string State() const;
    void SetState(std::string s);
    HostBlock Block() const; // fills HostBlock from these fields
};

class ArcDispatcher {
public:
    ArcDispatcher(RequestQueue& queue, CommandRegistry& registry, HostGate& gate,
                  JobTable& jobs, std::filesystem::path auditPath);
    void Drain(double budgetMs, std::uint32_t budgetCommands);
    std::uint32_t LastDrainRan() const;
};

}
```

`Drain` order, every call:

1. `jobs.StepAll(gate)` — each running job's step function runs **at most once**. This is not charged against `budgetCommands`.
2. Then pop commands until the queue is empty, `budgetCommands` commands have been invoked, or `budgetMs` has elapsed (check the clock before starting each command; a command already started finishes).
3. A command with `ExecThread::Job` is invoked. Its `fn` arms a job and returns. If `CommandResult::completesJob != 0`, `Drain` does **not** `Complete` the waiter yet. The job's finish path `Complete`s it. The main thread returns from `Drain` with that job still `Running`.

Cvar `arc.server.frameBudgetMs`, `double`, default `4.0`, `CVarFlags::Dev`, `Audience::Server`, `SettingScope::Project`, help `"Milliseconds of arc commands drained before the next tick."` A positive `Drain` argument wins.

Gate, before `Invoke`:

- `gate.readOnly && desc.access == Drive` → `permission.read-only` exit 3. `fn` not called.
- `desc.play == EditOnly && state == "playing"` → `host.busy` `detail.state = "playing"` exit 5. `fn` not called.
- `desc.play == PlayingOnly && state` is neither `playing` nor `paused` → `host.busy` with the current state, exit 5. `fn` not called.
- `desc.needsFrame && gate.minimized` → `host.busy` `detail.state = "minimized"` exit 5. `fn` not called. (Task 13's test owns the assertion; the check lands here so the flag has one implementation.)

Every completed envelope gets `envelope.host = gate.Block()`, `envelope.command` left as the call's command, and `timing` filled from the queue time and the invoke time. Do not write `hostFrame` or a top-level `exitCode`.

Audit one ndjson line per **Drive** command that was actually invoked, at `auditPath` (`Saved/arc/out`'s sibling `Saved/arc/audit.ndjson`). Spec s10.4 fields, these names: `time` (unix ms), `client` (callerId), `pid`, `command`, `argsDigest` (hex SHA-256 of canonical `args.dump()` with sorted keys), `outcome` (`"ok"` or `"error"`), `errorCode` (empty on ok), `frame`. Do not write the args. Observe commands are not audited. On construction, prune `auditPath.parent_path() / "out"` to the newest 200 files by `last_write_time`.

If one `Invoke` takes longer than `10 * budgetMs`, `Log::Engine()->warn` once with the command name. Do not fail the command.

`Sha256Hex`:

```cpp
static std::string Sha256Hex(std::string_view bytes);
```

on `Arcane::Crypto`, next to `HmacSha256Hex` (`Crypto.hpp:264`), using the SHA-256 primitive HMAC already calls and the existing `ToHex`. Header-only. Do not add `Crypto.cpp` and do not add `Base/Crypto.cpp`.

```cpp
static int g_steps = 0;
static bool StepOnce(Arcane::Arc::Job& job, Arcane::Arc::HostGate& gate, void*) {
    ++g_steps;
    gate.frame.fetch_add(1);
    job.progress.done += 1;
    job.envelope = Arcane::Arc::MakeOk(job.command, nlohmann::json{{"done", job.progress.done}}, gate.Block());
    return job.progress.done >= job.progress.total;
}

TEST_CASE("one Drain steps a frame job once and still runs a queued command", "[arc][dispatch]") {

    g_steps = 0;

                                  Arcane::Arc::Mutates::None, Arcane::Arc::PlayPolicy::Any), &err));
    Arcane::Arc::RequestQueue queue;
    Arcane::Arc::HostGate gate;
    Arcane::Arc::JobTable jobs;
    const auto audit = std::filesystem::temp_directory_path() / "arc-dispatch-job.ndjson";
    Arcane::Arc::ArcDispatcher dispatcher(queue, reg, gate, jobs, audit);
    const auto id = jobs.Arm("play.step", 10, &StepOnce, nullptr);
    Arcane::Arc::Call look;
    look.command = "demo.look";
    REQUIRE(queue.TryPush(1, look));
    dispatcher.Drain(1000.0, 8);
    CHECK(g_steps == 1);
    CHECK(g_ran == 1);
    CHECK(jobs.Find(id)->state == Arcane::Arc::JobState::Running);
    CHECK(gate.frame.load() == 1);
    for (int i = 0; i < 9; ++i)
        dispatcher.Drain(1000.0, 8);
    CHECK(g_steps == 10);
    CHECK(gate.frame.load() == 10);
    CHECK(jobs.Find(id)->state == Arcane::Arc::JobState::Done);
    dispatcher.Drain(1000.0, 8);
    CHECK(g_steps == 10);
    reg.UnregisterModule("dispatch-test");
}
```

### Task 9: Internal job table (s7.7)

Public `arc job` and `--detach` are P3. This table is what screenshot, compare, wait, and `play.step` arm.

**Files:**
- Create: `ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Arc/Jobs.hpp`
- Create: `ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Arc/Jobs.cpp`
- Test: `ArcaneTests/src/Arc/JobsTest.cpp`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Envelope`, `HostGate` (Tasks 3 and 8).
- Produces: the s7.7 row `{id, command, state, progress{done,total,label}, startedFrame, envelope}`.

```cpp
namespace Arcane::Arc {

struct JobId { std::uint64_t value = 0; };

enum class JobState : std::uint8_t { Queued, Running, Done, Failed, Cancelled };

struct JobProgress {
    std::uint64_t done = 0;
    std::uint64_t total = 0;
    std::string label;
};

struct Job;
using JobStepFn = bool (*)(Job& job, HostGate& gate, void* user); // true = finished

struct Job {
    JobId id;
    std::string command;
    JobState state = JobState::Queued;
    JobProgress progress;
    std::uint64_t startedFrame = 0;
    Envelope envelope;
    std::int64_t finishedUnixMs = 0;
    JobStepFn step = nullptr;
    void* user = nullptr;
    std::uint64_t waiterId = 0; // RequestQueue id to Complete when the job finishes; 0 = none
    std::uint64_t subscriberId = 0; // SSE follow jobs only; 0 = not a follow job
};

class JobTable {
public:
    JobId Arm(std::string command, std::uint64_t total, JobStepFn step, void* user);
    void BindWaiter(JobId id, std::uint64_t waiterId);
    void SetSubscriber(JobId id, std::uint64_t subscriberId);
    void CancelBySubscriber(std::uint64_t subscriberId); // Running rows with that id become Cancelled
    void StepAll(HostGate& gate);          // each Running job, at most one step
    void Finish(JobId id, Envelope result, JobState state);
    const Job* Find(JobId id) const;
    bool AnyRunning(std::initializer_list<std::string_view> commands) const;
    void Prune(std::int64_t nowUnixMs);    // finished older than 10 min, then cap 64
    std::size_t FinishedCount() const;
    void SetQueue(RequestQueue* queue);    // Finish Completes waiterId when non-zero
};
}
```

`Jobs.hpp` includes `<initializer_list>`. `AnyRunning` returns true when any row whose `command` is one of the names is `Running`. A finished or `Cancelled` row does not count. Ruling P4 (a) uses it so a second capture or compare does not `Arm`. `Arm` assigns an id starting at 1, state `Running`, `progress.total = total`, `progress.done = 0`. `StepAll` calls `step` once. When `step` returns true, state becomes `Done` if `envelope.ok`, otherwise `Failed`, and `finishedUnixMs` is set. `Finish` does the same for a caller that already has the envelope (a capture that failed before arming does not leave a `Running` row). `Prune` drops finished jobs with `now - finishedUnixMs > 600000`, then drops the oldest finished until at most 64 remain. Running jobs are never pruned.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

```cpp
TEST_CASE("finished jobs prune at 10 minutes and at 64", "[arc][job]") {
    Arcane::Arc::JobTable table;
    std::vector<Arcane::Arc::JobId> ids;
    for (int i = 0; i < 70; ++i) {
        auto id = table.Arm("capture.viewport", 1, nullptr, nullptr);
        table.Finish(id, Arcane::Arc::MakeOk("capture.viewport", nlohmann::json::object(), {}),
                     Arcane::Arc::JobState::Done);
        ids.push_back(id);
    }
    table.Prune(0);
    CHECK(table.FinishedCount() == 64);
    CHECK(table.Find(ids.front()) == nullptr);
    CHECK(table.Find(ids.back()) != nullptr);
    auto live = table.Arm("host.wait", 1, nullptr, nullptr);
    table.Prune(9'000'000'000);
    CHECK(table.Find(live) != nullptr);
    CHECK(table.Find(live)->state == Arcane::Arc::JobState::Running);
}

TEST_CASE("StepAll advances one running job by one and leaves the next for later", "[arc][job]") {
    Arcane::Arc::JobTable table;
    int hits = 0;
    auto id = table.Arm("play.step", 3,
        [](Arcane::Arc::Job& job, Arcane::Arc::HostGate&, void* user) {
            *static_cast<int*>(user) += 1;
            job.progress.done += 1;
            return job.progress.done >= job.progress.total;
        }, &hits);
    Arcane::Arc::HostGate gate;
    table.StepAll(gate);
    CHECK(hits == 1);
    CHECK(table.Find(id)->state == Arcane::Arc::JobState::Running);
    CHECK(table.Find(id)->progress.done == 1);
    table.StepAll(gate);
    table.StepAll(gate);
    CHECK(hits == 3);
    CHECK(table.Find(id)->state == Arcane::Arc::JobState::Done);
    table.StepAll(gate);
    CHECK(hits == 3);
}

TEST_CASE("AnyRunning is true only for a Running row of the named command", "[arc][job]") {
    Arcane::Arc::JobTable table;
    CHECK_FALSE(table.AnyRunning({"capture.window", "capture.compare"}));
    auto id = table.Arm("capture.window", 1, nullptr, nullptr);
    CHECK(table.AnyRunning({"capture.window"}));
    CHECK(table.AnyRunning({"capture.compare", "capture.window"}));
    CHECK_FALSE(table.AnyRunning({"capture.compare"}));
    table.Finish(id, Arcane::Arc::MakeOk("capture.window", nlohmann::json::object(), {}),
                 Arcane::Arc::JobState::Done);
    CHECK_FALSE(table.AnyRunning({"capture.window"}));
}

TEST_CASE("CancelBySubscriber cancels only the keyed job", "[arc][job]") {
    Arcane::Arc::JobTable table;
    int hits = 0;
    auto follow = table.Arm("log.follow", 0,
        [](Arcane::Arc::Job&, Arcane::Arc::HostGate&, void* user) {
            *static_cast<int*>(user) += 1;
            return false;
        }, &hits);
    table.SetSubscriber(follow, 7);
    auto other = table.Arm("play.step", 3, nullptr, nullptr);
    table.CancelBySubscriber(7);
    CHECK(table.Find(follow)->state == Arcane::Arc::JobState::Cancelled);
    CHECK(table.Find(other)->state == Arcane::Arc::JobState::Running);
    Arcane::Arc::HostGate gate;
    table.StepAll(gate);
    CHECK(hits == 0);
    table.CancelBySubscriber(7);
    CHECK(table.Find(other)->state == Arcane::Arc::JobState::Running);
}
```

The 10-minute rule: finish one job, read `finishedUnixMs` from `Find`, `Prune(finished + 599999)` keeps it, `Prune(finished + 600001)` drops it. Put that in another `TEST_CASE` in the same file. `StepAll` skips every state other than `Running`, so a cancelled follow job's step is not called.

- [ ] **Step 2: Run the test to verify it fails**

Generate, Debug build, filter `[arc][job]`. Expected: FAIL to compile.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the table**

`std::vector<Job>` plus a mutex. `Arm` with a null step is legal for the prune test; `StepAll` skips a null step and skips any state other than `Running`. `Finish` on an already-finished id is a no-op. `CancelBySubscriber` sets every `Running` job whose `subscriberId` equals the argument to `Cancelled` and does not call its step. A second call is a no-op. `subscriberId` 0 is never cancelled by that function.

- [ ] **Step 4: Run the test**

Filter `[arc][job]`. Expected: PASS. Re-run `[arc][dispatch]`: Task 8's `StepOnce` test still passes against this table.

**Interfaces:**

- Produces:

```cpp
namespace Arcane::Arc {

struct Arming {
    static bool Allow(NetMode mode, bool distBuild, bool editorEnableCvar);
};

class HostSession {
public:
    struct Config {
        HostKind kind;
        CVarContext cvarContext;
        std::filesystem::path projectRoot;
        std::filesystem::path descriptorOverride;
        std::uint32_t idleExitSeconds = 600;
        bool readOnly = false;
        bool holdSim = false;          // Task 12: --arc && maxFrames == 0
        std::vector<std::string> capabilities;
    };
    explicit HostSession(Config config);
    void Start();
    void Drain();
    void Stop(std::string exitReason);
    bool IdleExpired() const;
    HostGate& Gate();
    TickGate& Tick();                   // defined in Task 12; declare it now
    void NoteReload(bool ok, std::string reason, std::uint64_t frame);
    std::uint16_t Port() const;
    const std::filesystem::path& DescriptorFile() const;
    bool TakeQuit() const;              // host.quit sets it; Task 16
    void RequestQuit();
};
}
```

`Arming::Allow` is false for `NetMode::Client`, for `distBuild`, or for `editorEnableCvar == false`. `Standalone`, `DedicatedServer`, and `ListenServer` return true when the other two inputs allow it. The editor process uses `CVarContext::Editor` even while play topology is a client. A process whose primary `Runtime::Mode()` is `NetMode::Client` does not call `Start`.

`editor.arc.enable`, inside `#if !defined(ARC_BUILD_DIST)`:

```cpp
ARC_CVAR(cvar_editorArcEnable, "editor.arc.enable", bool, true,
    .flags = CVarFlags::Dev,
    .audience = Audience::Editor,
    .scope = SettingScope::PreferencesMachine,
    .help = "Serve the arc CLI on 127.0.0.1.");
```

When the published value becomes false, the editor calls `Stop("arc-disabled")` and does not restart until it becomes true again.

Capabilities, sorted, written onto `HostStatus`. All three include `cvar`, `console`, `ecs`, `logs`, `problems`, `stats`, `play`. Editor also adds `capture`, `compare`, `scene`, `selection`. Runtime also adds `capture`, `compare`, `scene`. Server does **not** add `capture` or `compare`. `play` on the server is the tick gate (Task 29), not a renderer.

`Start` writes the descriptor at `DescriptorPath` unless `descriptorOverride` is set. `Drain` calls `dispatcher.Drain`, republishes `HostStatus` and the catalog JSON into the listener atomics, and calls `TouchHeartbeat` when a second has elapsed. `NoteReload` copies `PluginHost::Generation()` into `pluginGeneration` plus `reloadOk`, `reloadReason`, `reloadAtFrame`. Do not change `Reload`'s `bool` return.

`exitReason`: the chain in `ArcaneRuntime/src/RuntimeApp.cpp` at line 1264 gains `"arc-quit"` when `TakeQuit()` is true, and `"arc-idle"` when `IdleExpired()` is true. Add the same two strings to the server's exit path. `IdleExpired()` is true when `idleExitSeconds > 0` and the last completed command (or `Start`, if none) is older than that many seconds. `0` disables it.

Do not add a `HostConfig` field for read-only. `Start` sets `gate.readOnly` when `ARC_READ_ONLY` is `1` or `GetCommandLineW` contains the exact token `--read-only`.

### Task 12: AM1 tick gate

**Files:**
- Create: `ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Arc/TickGate.hpp`
- Create: `ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Arc/TickGate.cpp`
- Modify: `ArcaneServer/src/ServerApp.cpp` (around `Advance` at 191 and `framesTicked` at 210)
- Modify: `ArcaneRuntime/src/RuntimeApp.cpp` (around `AdvanceSim` at 814)
- Modify: `ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Arc/HostSession.hpp` (the `Tick()` declared in Task 11)
- Test: `ArcaneTests/src/Arc/TickGateTest.cpp`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `HostSession::Gate()` (Task 11). `RunLoop::SetPaused` / `RequestSingleStep` are the editor path and are not this class.
- Produces:

```cpp
namespace Arcane::Arc {
enum class TickDecision : std::uint8_t { Idle, Step, Run };

class TickGate {
public:
    void SetRunning(bool running);
    void SetPaused(bool paused);
    void RequestSteps(std::uint32_t n);
    TickDecision Consume();
    bool Running() const;
    bool Paused() const;
    std::uint32_t StepsRemaining() const;
};
}
```

`Consume`:

| running | paused | steps | result |
|---|---|---|---|
| false | * | 0 | Idle |
| false | * | >0 | Step, then decrement |
| true | false | * | Run, and clear a leftover step count |
| true | true | 0 | Idle |
| true | true | >0 | Step, then decrement |

When `--arc` and `maxFrames == 0`, `HostSession::Config::holdSim` is true and `Start` leaves the gate Idle (`running == false`, `paused == true`). `--frames N` leaves `holdSim` false so the scripted run still finishes. Default `holdSim` is false.

`host.frame` increments by 1 only in the branch that calls `Advance` / `AdvanceSim`. Idle iterations do not increment it. `ArcaneRuntime/src/RuntimeFrame.cpp`'s `io.frameCount++` stays where it is.

Server loop after this task:

```cpp
if (decision != Arcane::Arc::TickDecision::Idle) {
    m_runtime->Advance(/* existing args */);
    if (m_arc) m_arc->Gate().frame.fetch_add(1);
    ++framesTicked;
}
```

Keep `Advance`'s real arguments. A host with no `m_arc` takes the `Run` branch and behaves as today. Headless runtime uses the same `Consume` check around `AdvanceSim` at line 814. An Idle headless iteration may still render; it must not bump `gate.frame`. The editor loop does not use `TickGate` for the viewport; it keeps rendering every non-minimized frame. Editor `host.frame` is written by Task 29 from the play tick, not from the ImGui frame.

**Interfaces:**

- Produces:

```cpp
namespace Arcane::Arc::Cli {

enum class Format { Json, Human, Ndjson };

struct RenderOptions {
    Format format = Format::Json;
    bool tty = false;
};

struct Printed {
    std::string stdoutText;
    std::string stderrText;
};

Printed Render(const Envelope& env, const ClientBlock& client, RenderOptions opt);
// json: ToJson(env) plus "client":{exitCode, retried, descriptor}. One object.
// ndjson: one {"type":"progress",...} line per notification that carries detail.progress,
//         then {"type":"result","count":1,"envelope":<ToJson plus client>}.
// human: one summary line on stdout ("ok <command>" or "error <code>"), data keys as
//        "key: value", artifacts as paths. Diagnostics (errors, warnings) on stderr.
// --format github is not a Format. ParseGlobals returns false and exit 2.

struct GlobalOptions {
    Format format = Format::Human; // TTY default; non-TTY default is Json
    bool json = false;
    bool verbose = false;
    bool tty = false;
    bool readOnly = false;
    bool yes = false;
    bool noRetry = false;
    bool retryOnDeath = false;
    std::uint32_t timeoutMs = 30000; // milliseconds. --timeout <s> is seconds; ParseGlobals scales.
    std::string project;
    std::string hostKind;       // editor | runtime | server, optional
    std::uint32_t pid = 0;
};

bool ParseGlobals(int argc, char** argv, GlobalOptions& out, std::string* error);
struct CallRequest {
    std::string command;
    nlohmann::json args = nlohmann::json::object();
};

struct ExchangeResult {
    Envelope envelope;
    bool connectionRefusedBeforeBody = false;
};

using ExchangeFn = ExchangeResult (*)(const std::string& requestBody, void* user);

Printed Call(const CallRequest& req, const GlobalOptions& opt, ExchangeFn exchange, void* user, ClientBlock* client);
int Run(int argc, char** argv); // arc.exe main calls this. Run parses argv, builds an ExchangeFn over the live socket, and calls Call.

struct TargetWatch {
    std::string command;
    std::uint32_t pid = 0;
    std::uint64_t processStart = 0;
    std::filesystem::path descriptorPath;
    std::filesystem::path diagnosticsDir;
    std::int64_t requestUnixMs = 0;
};

// nullopt: the pid is live and the descriptor heartbeat is fresh.
// An engaged optional is a finished error envelope. The caller does not post it.
std::optional<Envelope> ObserveTarget(const TargetWatch& watch, std::int64_t nowUnixMs);
}
```

`--json` equals `--format json` and wins over TTY detection (AM4). A non-TTY stdout with no format flag is JSON. `--format github` sets `error` to `unknown format github` and `Run` returns 2; it does not fall through to human. `Render` runs `RedactSecrets` on both streams with the token it was given; `Run` passes the descriptor token. `--verbose` logs the request line after redaction. `--version` prints `Arcane arc` and returns 0. Dist `main` (`#if defined(ARC_BUILD_DIST)`) prints `arc is compiled out of Dist builds` and returns 2, so the exe still links.

The client retries a busy answer with backoff until `--timeout`, not for a fixed two tries. The bound is `GlobalOptions::timeoutMs`, default 30000. `--no-retry` disables busy retries. Exit 7 retries only with `--retry-on-death`, and then on that same until-timeout loop. That retry is ruling P4 (a). `host.busy` with `detail.state` `capture-pending` is exit 5 and provably not run, so it is one of the answers this retry already covers. Do not add a second retry policy. `Call` is the function the busy-host test drives. It does not open a socket. `Run` builds one `ExchangeFn` over the real connection and calls `Call`. The first sleep is 10 ms, the second is 50 ms, and each later sleep doubles, capped at 1000 ms. A retry is issued only when `elapsed + nextSleep < timeoutMs`. A connection refused before the body was sent is exit 5 and uses this loop. A connection refused after the body was sent is not retried. `ParseGlobals` sets `GlobalOptions::noRetry` from `--no-retry`. `--timeout <s>` is seconds, decimal allowed (spec s4.6, line 361). Parse the whole token with `std::strtod`. A token that is not fully consumed, `ERANGE`, a non-finite value, a negative value, an empty token, a missing value, or a rounded millisecond count above `std::numeric_limits<std::uint32_t>::max()` makes `ParseGlobals` return false, `error` contains `timeout`, and `Run` returns 2. Otherwise store `timeoutMs = std::max(1u, static_cast<std::uint32_t>(std::llround(seconds * 1000.0)))`. Use `std::llround` (half away from zero). `std::lround` returns `long`, which is 32-bit on Windows, so a multi-hour timeout overflows it. Check the range before the cast: `llround` of a value outside `long long` is undefined. `--timeout 0` and any positive value that rounds below 1 ms store 1. `ParseGlobals` begins by assigning `out = GlobalOptions{}`. That value is the struct's default, and its `timeoutMs` member initializer is 30000. Flags present in this argv are applied after that assignment, so an omitted `--timeout` stores 30000 even when the caller reused an `out` a previous parse had set to 500. The omitted-timeout check below passes a fresh `GlobalOptions`, not the object that just parsed `--timeout 0.5`. `1e3` is 1000 seconds because `strtod` consumes it. `2s` is a refusal because the trailing `s` is not consumed. `Call` posts `opt.timeoutMs` as the wire integer `options.timeoutMs` and does not multiply. The case that assigns `opt.timeoutMs = 200` below is 200 milliseconds of that field. The flag `--timeout 200` would store 200000.

`Call` posts `{"id","command","args","options":{"timeoutMs"}}` with `opt.timeoutMs` already in milliseconds. The exit it retries on is `connectionRefusedBeforeBody ? 5 : ExitFromHost(result.envelope)`. It does not trust a side channel. There is no attempt cap. `client->retried` is set true when a retry is issued. `Render` prints that flag. `Call` has no pid and does not call `ObserveTarget`. The busy-host tests keep driving `Call` through `ExchangeFn`.

**Target watch.** `ObserveTarget` is the client's mid-call observation. It does not sleep, does not spawn, and does not terminate a process. `EditorLock::IsLive` (`Project.cpp:807-837`, extracted in Task 7) is the liveness test: `OpenProcess`, creation time equals `processStart` when `processStart != 0`, and the exit time is zero. A start flipped by 1 is not live.

- `!IsLive({pid, processStart})` → `host.died`. Died wins when the heartbeat is also stale.
- Otherwise `ReadDescriptor` fails → `host.hung`. A live process with no readable heartbeat is hung, because a fresh heartbeat cannot be proved.
- Otherwise `IsHung(desc, nowUnixMs)` → `host.hung`. `IsHung` is `nowUnixMs - heartbeatUnixMs > 10000`. `now == heartbeat` and `now == heartbeat + 10000` are not hung. `+ 10001` is hung.
- Otherwise return `nullopt`.

Both envelopes are `MakeError(watch.command, code, message, "relaunch")`. `host.pid` is `watch.pid`. When the descriptor was read, `host.state` is `desc.state` and `host.kind` is `ToString(desc.hostKind)`. `errors[0].detail.lastState` is that state, or `""` when the descriptor was not read. `detail.exitCode` is present only for `host.died`, and only when this `{pid, processStart}` opened and `GetProcessTimes` showed a non-zero exit time. `IsLive` closes the handle it opens (`Project.cpp:820`). `ObserveTarget` opens the pid again, with `PROCESS_QUERY_LIMITED_INFORMATION`, and records `exitCode` only when that second open's creation time matches `processStart` and the exit time is non-zero. The value is `GetExitCodeProcess`'s `DWORD`, including `259` (`STILL_ACTIVE`) when the exit time already proved the process has exited. A creation-time mismatch is a different process: omit `exitCode`. An `OpenProcess` failure omits it too. The function never calls `TerminateProcess`.

`detail.arcdiag` is the newest `*.arcdiag` directly under `watch.diagnosticsDir` whose `last_write_time`, converted with `std::chrono::clock_cast<std::chrono::system_clock>`, is strictly after `requestUnixMs`. Omit the key when none qualifies. Equal mtimes tie-break by the lexicographically greater `generic_string()`. `ObserveTarget` does not wait for the watchdog. `IsHung`'s 10 s is under `hangSeconds` (12) and under the 15 s sleep, so the first hung sample usually has no report yet.

`Run`'s live-socket `ExchangeFn` (installed in Task 15, once `Select` has a host) samples this function. The first sample is immediate, before `send`. Later samples run every 250 ms while the response read is outstanding: `PollSockets` with a 250 ms timeout, and every wake, including a timeout, calls `ObserveTarget` again before the socket is checked. `requestUnixMs` is `system_clock` milliseconds, taken once when that call starts, before the first sample and before the body is written. An engaged optional is returned with `connectionRefusedBeforeBody = false`, and the body is not written when it has not been written yet. A complete HTTP response already buffered is parsed and kept. A selected host that then dies or hangs is `host.died` or `host.hung` (exit 7), not `target.none` (4) and not connection-refused (5). Exit 7 still retries only with `--retry-on-death`, on `Call`'s existing loop. `arc status` does not call `ObserveTarget`.

```cpp
Printed Arcane::Arc::Cli::Call(const CallRequest& req, const GlobalOptions& opt,
                               ExchangeFn exchange, void* user, ClientBlock* client) {
    nlohmann::json body = {
        {"id", "c1"},
        {"command", req.command},
        {"args", req.args},
        {"options", {{"timeoutMs", opt.timeoutMs}}},
    };
    const std::string payload = body.dump();
    ClientBlock local;
    if (client == nullptr)
        client = &local;
    client->retried = false;
    ExchangeResult last{};
    const auto started = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
    int pauseMs = 10;
    for (;;) {
        last = exchange(payload, user);
        const int code = last.connectionRefusedBeforeBody ? 5 : ExitFromHost(last.envelope);
        const bool busy = code == 5 && !opt.noRetry;
        const bool death = code == 7 && opt.retryOnDeath;
        const auto elapsed = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(
            std::chrono::steady_clock::now() - started).count();
        if ((busy || death) && elapsed + pauseMs < static_cast<long long>(opt.timeoutMs)) {
            client->retried = true;
            std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(pauseMs));
            pauseMs = pauseMs < 50 ? 50 : std::min(1000, pauseMs * 2);
            continue;
        }
        client->exitCode = code;
        break;
    }
    RenderOptions ro;
    ro.format = opt.format;
    return Render(last.envelope, *client, ro);
}
```

```cpp
    const auto printed = Arcane::Arc::Cli::Render(env, {}, opt);
    CHECK(printed.stdoutText.find("\"type\":\"result\"") != std::string::npos);
    CHECK(printed.stdoutText.find("\"count\":1") != std::string::npos);
    CHECK(printed.stdoutText.find("\"envelope\"") != std::string::npos);
}

struct BusyHost {
    int posts = 0;
    int busyAnswers = 0;
    int busyForPosts = 0;
    std::chrono::steady_clock::time_point freeAt{};
    bool useFreeAt = false;
    std::string busyState;
};

static Arcane::Arc::Cli::ExchangeResult BusyExchange(const std::string& body, void* user) {
    auto* transport = static_cast<BusyHost*>(user);
    ++transport->posts;
    Arcane::Arc::Cli::ExchangeResult result;
    const auto cmd = nlohmann::json::parse(body).at("command").get<std::string>();
    const bool stillBusy = transport->posts <= transport->busyForPosts
        || (transport->useFreeAt && std::chrono::steady_clock::now() < transport->freeAt);
    if (stillBusy) {
        ++transport->busyAnswers;
        result.envelope = Arcane::Arc::MakeError(cmd, "host.busy", "one capture is pending", "retry");
        result.envelope.errors[0].detail = nlohmann::json{{"state", "capture-pending"}};
        transport->busyState = result.envelope.errors[0].detail["state"].get<std::string>();
        return result;
    }
    Arcane::Arc::HostBlock host;
    host.kind = "runtime";
    host.state = "ready";
    result.envelope = Arcane::Arc::MakeOk(cmd, nlohmann::json{{"width", 2}, {"height", 1}}, host);
    result.envelope.command = cmd;
    return result;
}

TEST_CASE("a busy host longer than 60 ms is retried until it frees", "[arc][client]") {
    BusyHost transport;
    transport.busyForPosts = 3;
    transport.useFreeAt = true;
    transport.freeAt = std::chrono::steady_clock::now() + std::chrono::milliseconds(150);
    Arcane::Arc::Cli::GlobalOptions opt;
    opt.format = Arcane::Arc::Cli::Format::Json;
    opt.noRetry = false;
    opt.timeoutMs = 30000;
    Arcane::Arc::Cli::CallRequest shot;
    shot.command = "capture.window";
    shot.args = nlohmann::json::object();
    const auto started = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
    Arcane::Arc::ClientBlock client;
    const auto printed = Arcane::Arc::Cli::Call(shot, opt, &BusyExchange, &transport, &client);
    const auto elapsedMs = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(
        std::chrono::steady_clock::now() - started).count();
    const auto json = nlohmann::json::parse(printed.stdoutText);
    CHECK(json["ok"] == true);
    CHECK(json["command"] == "capture.window");
    CHECK(json["client"]["retried"] == true);
    CHECK(json["client"]["exitCode"] == 0);
    CHECK(client.retried == true);
    CHECK(client.exitCode == 0);
    CHECK(transport.busyAnswers >= 3);
    CHECK(transport.posts == transport.busyAnswers + 1);
    CHECK(transport.busyState == "capture-pending");
    CHECK(elapsedMs >= 60);
    CHECK(elapsedMs < 5000);

    opt.noRetry = true;
    transport.posts = 0;
    transport.busyAnswers = 0;
    transport.busyForPosts = 5;
    transport.useFreeAt = false;
    Arcane::Arc::ClientBlock refusedClient;
    const auto refused = Arcane::Arc::Cli::Call(shot, opt, &BusyExchange, &transport, &refusedClient);
    const auto refusedJson = nlohmann::json::parse(refused.stdoutText);
    CHECK(refusedClient.exitCode == 5);
    CHECK(refusedClient.retried == false);
    CHECK(transport.posts == 1);
    CHECK(transport.busyAnswers == 1);
    CHECK(refusedJson["ok"] == false);
    CHECK(refusedJson["errors"][0]["code"] == "host.busy");
    CHECK(refusedJson["errors"][0]["detail"]["state"] == "capture-pending");
    CHECK(refusedJson["client"]["exitCode"] == 5);
    CHECK(refusedJson["client"]["retried"] == false);
}

TEST_CASE("busy retries stop at --timeout", "[arc][client]") {
    BusyHost transport;
    transport.busyForPosts = 100;
    Arcane::Arc::Cli::GlobalOptions opt;
    opt.format = Arcane::Arc::Cli::Format::Json;
    opt.noRetry = false;
    opt.timeoutMs = 200;
    Arcane::Arc::Cli::CallRequest shot;
    shot.command = "capture.window";
    const auto started = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
    Arcane::Arc::ClientBlock client;
    const auto printed = Arcane::Arc::Cli::Call(shot, opt, &BusyExchange, &transport, &client);
    const auto elapsedMs = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(
        std::chrono::steady_clock::now() - started).count();
    const auto json = nlohmann::json::parse(printed.stdoutText);
    CHECK(json["ok"] == false);
    CHECK(client.exitCode == 5);
    CHECK(client.retried == true);
    CHECK(transport.posts >= 4);
    CHECK(transport.posts < 8);
    CHECK(elapsedMs >= 60);
    CHECK(elapsedMs < 1000);
    CHECK(json["errors"][0]["detail"]["state"] == "capture-pending");
}

static std::int64_t UnixMs(std::chrono::system_clock::time_point tp) {
    return std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(tp.time_since_epoch()).count();
}

TEST_CASE("ObserveTarget reports host.hung with the newest later .arcdiag and host.died when the pid is gone", "[arc][client]") {
    const auto root = std::filesystem::temp_directory_path() / "arc-observe";
    std::filesystem::remove_all(root);
    const auto diag = root / "diagnostics";
    std::filesystem::create_directories(diag);
    Arcane::Arc::Descriptor d;
    d.protocol = 1;
    d.hostKind = Arcane::Arc::HostKind::Runtime;
    d.pid = ::GetCurrentProcessId();
    d.processStart = Arcane::EditorLock::Self().start;
    d.port = 9;
    d.state = "ready";
    d.heartbeatUnixMs = 5000;
    const auto descPath = root / "runtime.json";
    REQUIRE(Arcane::Arc::WriteDescriptorAtomic(descPath, d));
    Arcane::Arc::Cli::TargetWatch watch;
    watch.command = "cvar.get";
    watch.pid = d.pid;
    watch.processStart = d.processStart;
    watch.descriptorPath = descPath;
    watch.diagnosticsDir = diag;
    CHECK_FALSE(Arcane::Arc::Cli::ObserveTarget(watch, d.heartbeatUnixMs));
    CHECK_FALSE(Arcane::Arc::Cli::ObserveTarget(watch, d.heartbeatUnixMs + 10000));
    const auto now = std::chrono::system_clock::now();
    watch.requestUnixMs = UnixMs(now - std::chrono::minutes(30));
    auto hung = Arcane::Arc::Cli::ObserveTarget(watch, d.heartbeatUnixMs + 10001);
    REQUIRE(hung);
    CHECK(hung->command == "cvar.get");
    CHECK(hung->errors[0].code == "host.hung");
    CHECK(hung->errors[0].remediation == "relaunch");
    CHECK(Arcane::Arc::ExitFromHost(*hung) == 7);
    CHECK(hung->host.pid == d.pid);
    CHECK(hung->host.state == "ready");
    CHECK(hung->errors[0].detail["lastState"] == "ready");
    CHECK_FALSE(hung->errors[0].detail.contains("arcdiag"));

    const auto older = diag / "old.arcdiag";
    const auto newer = diag / "new.arcdiag";
    { std::ofstream(older) << "old"; }
    std::filesystem::last_write_time(older,
        std::chrono::clock_cast<std::chrono::file_clock>(now - std::chrono::hours(1)));
    auto stillNone = Arcane::Arc::Cli::ObserveTarget(watch, d.heartbeatUnixMs + 10001);
    REQUIRE(stillNone);
    CHECK_FALSE(stillNone->errors[0].detail.contains("arcdiag"));
    { std::ofstream(newer) << "new"; }
    std::filesystem::last_write_time(newer, std::chrono::clock_cast<std::chrono::file_clock>(now));
    auto withFile = Arcane::Arc::Cli::ObserveTarget(watch, d.heartbeatUnixMs + 10001);
    REQUIRE(withFile);
    CHECK(std::filesystem::equivalent(
        std::filesystem::path(withFile->errors[0].detail["arcdiag"].get<std::string>()), newer));

    const auto a = diag / "a.arcdiag";
    const auto m = diag / "m.arcdiag";
    { std::ofstream(a) << "a"; }
    { std::ofstream(m) << "m"; }
    const auto stamp = std::chrono::clock_cast<std::chrono::file_clock>(now + std::chrono::seconds(2));
    std::filesystem::last_write_time(a, stamp);
    std::filesystem::last_write_time(m, stamp);
    auto tie = Arcane::Arc::Cli::ObserveTarget(watch, d.heartbeatUnixMs + 10001);
    REQUIRE(tie);
    CHECK(std::filesystem::equivalent(
        std::filesystem::path(tie->errors[0].detail["arcdiag"].get<std::string>()), m));

    watch.descriptorPath = root / "absent.json";
    auto unread = Arcane::Arc::Cli::ObserveTarget(watch, UnixMs(now));
    REQUIRE(unread);
    CHECK(unread->errors[0].code == "host.hung");
    CHECK(unread->errors[0].detail["lastState"] == "");

    Arcane::Arc::Cli::TargetWatch gone;
    gone.command = "cvar.get";
    gone.pid = 1;
    gone.processStart = Arcane::EditorLock::Self().start + 1;
    gone.descriptorPath = descPath;
    gone.diagnosticsDir = diag;
    gone.requestUnixMs = watch.requestUnixMs;
    auto died = Arcane::Arc::Cli::ObserveTarget(gone, d.heartbeatUnixMs + 10001);
    REQUIRE(died);
    CHECK(died->errors[0].code == "host.died");
    CHECK(Arcane::Arc::ExitFromHost(*died) == 7);
    CHECK_FALSE(died->errors[0].detail.contains("exitCode"));

    STARTUPINFOW si{};
    si.cb = sizeof(si);
    PROCESS_INFORMATION pi{};
    std::wstring cmd = L"C:\\Windows\\System32\\cmd.exe /c exit 3";
    std::vector<wchar_t> mutableCmd(cmd.begin(), cmd.end());
    mutableCmd.push_back(L'\0');
    REQUIRE(CreateProcessW(L"C:\\Windows\\System32\\cmd.exe", mutableCmd.data(), nullptr, nullptr, FALSE,
                           CREATE_NO_WINDOW, nullptr, nullptr, &si, &pi));
    REQUIRE(WaitForSingleObject(pi.hProcess, 5000) == WAIT_OBJECT_0);
    FILETIME created{}, exited{}, kernel{}, userTime{};
    REQUIRE(GetProcessTimes(pi.hProcess, &created, &exited, &kernel, &userTime));
    Arcane::Arc::Cli::TargetWatch exitedWatch;
    exitedWatch.command = "cvar.get";
    exitedWatch.pid = pi.dwProcessId;
    exitedWatch.processStart = (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(created.dwHighDateTime) << 32) | created.dwLowDateTime;
    exitedWatch.descriptorPath = root / "absent.json";
    exitedWatch.diagnosticsDir = diag;
    exitedWatch.requestUnixMs = UnixMs(now);
    auto coded = Arcane::Arc::Cli::ObserveTarget(exitedWatch, UnixMs(now));
    REQUIRE(coded);
    CHECK(coded->errors[0].code == "host.died");
    CHECK(coded->host.pid == pi.dwProcessId);
    CHECK(coded->errors[0].detail["exitCode"] == 3);
    CHECK(Arcane::Arc::ExitFromHost(*coded) == 7);
    CloseHandle(pi.hThread);
    CloseHandle(pi.hProcess);
    std::filesystem::remove_all(root);
}
```

The acceptance call observes hung at 10 s, before the watchdog's 12 s report, so this case is the deterministic `.arcdiag` pin. `old.arcdiag` is an hour behind `requestUnixMs` and is omitted. `new.arcdiag` is later and is attached. `a.arcdiag` and `m.arcdiag` share a later mtime, and the lexicographically greater `generic_string()` wins. The cmd process is held open until after `ObserveTarget` so its pid cannot be recycled; the handle is closed after the checks. `ObserveTarget` only queries it.

`Render` calls `RedactSecrets` on both streams with `client.token` before returning. The second case sets `client.token` and checks the `Printed` `Render` returned. Do not redact again in the test. `ToJson(Envelope)` still omits `client` and `token`.

### Task 15: Discovery, status, selection

**Files:**
- Create: `ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Arc/Cli/Discover.hpp`
- Create: `ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Arc/Cli/Discover.cpp`
- Modify: `ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Arc/Cli/Client.cpp` (Run's live ExchangeFn samples `ObserveTarget`)
- Test: `ArcaneTests/src/Arc/DiscoverTest.cpp`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `ReadDescriptor`, `IsStale`, `IsHung` (Task 7). `ExitFromHost` (Task 3).
- Produces:

```cpp
namespace Arcane::Arc::Cli {
struct LiveHost {
    Descriptor descriptor;
    std::filesystem::path path;
    bool hung = false;
};

struct Scan {
    std::vector<LiveHost> live;
    std::vector<std::filesystem::path> removedStale;
};

Scan ScanProject(const std::filesystem::path& projectRoot);
std::optional<LiveHost> Select(const Scan& scan, const GlobalOptions& opt, Envelope* error);
nlohmann::json StatusData(const Scan& scan);
}
```

`ScanProject` reads `Saved/arc/*.json`. Stale files (`IsStale`) are deleted and listed in `removedStale`. Hung files (`IsHung`, heartbeat older than 10 s) stay, with `hung = true`. `Select`: zero live hosts → `target.none` exit 4. Two or more and neither `--pid` nor `--host` narrows to one → `target.ambiguous` exit 2, `candidates` are `kind:pid`. `--host editor` with two editors and no `--pid` is still ambiguous. A protocol major other than 1 on the chosen descriptor → `protocol.mismatch` exit 4. `Select` still returns a hung host. Hung is not a reason to delete the file or to refuse the selection. `arc status` prints `MakeOk("status", StatusData(scan), {})`. It lists every live host. It does not connect, and it does not call `ObserveTarget`. Each `data.hosts` object keeps the s7.1 fields (`hostKind`, `pid`, `port`, `state`, `frame`, `abi`, `project`, `heartbeatAgeMs`) and adds `hung`, copied from `LiveHost::hung`. The s7.1 example omits `hung` because those rows are fresh (`heartbeatAgeMs` 410 and 120). Spec s4.4 requires a stale heartbeat to be reported hung. `heartbeatAgeMs` is `max(0, nowUnixMs - heartbeatUnixMs)` using the same `nowUnixMs` as `IsHung`. Age 10000 is not hung. Age 10001 is hung. `data.stale` lists removed files. Status does not print tokens. `abi` stays the field s7.1 already names. A status envelope is exit 0.

`Run`, after `Select` succeeds for a command other than `status`, fills a `TargetWatch` and the live `ExchangeFn` samples it on the schedule in Task 14's **Target watch**. `command` is the wire command. `pid`, `processStart`, and `descriptorPath` come from the selected `LiveHost` (`descriptor.pid`, `descriptor.processStart`, `path`). `diagnosticsDir` is the sibling host's report directory. `arc.exe` lives in `bin/<cfg>-windows-x86_64-md/arc/` (`Engine.hpp`'s `ExecutablePathUtf8`, line 24, is that exe). The runtime exe is `../ArcaneRuntime/ArcaneRuntime.exe`, and the same shape names the editor and the server (Task 16). `ReportDirFor` with an empty `dumpDir` is `<exe dir>/diagnostics` (`Diagnostics.hpp:310-318`). The descriptor has no exe path. Do not add one.

Define `HostDiagnosticsDir` in `Arcane::Arc::Cli`:

```cpp
std::filesystem::path HostDiagnosticsDir(Arcane::Arc::HostKind kind) {
    const auto bin = std::filesystem::path(Arcane::ExecutablePathUtf8()).parent_path().parent_path();
    const char* folder = kind == Arcane::Arc::HostKind::Editor ? "ArcaneEditor"
                       : kind == Arcane::Arc::HostKind::Server ? "ArcaneServer"
                       : "ArcaneRuntime";
    return bin / folder / "diagnostics";
}
```

```cpp
    CHECK(scan.live[0].hung);

    opt.hostKind = "runtime";
    Arcane::Arc::Envelope err;
    auto selected = Arcane::Arc::Cli::Select(scan, opt, &err);
    REQUIRE(selected);
    CHECK(selected->hung);
    std::filesystem::remove_all(root);
}
```

`DescriptorPath` includes kind, so the editor and runtime files of this pid are two paths. Both are live because pid and `processStart` match this process. The stale file uses pid 1 and `start + 1`, and `ScanProject` deletes it. Empty options are `target.ambiguous` exit 2. `--pid` of this process plus `--host runtime` selects port 2.

**Interfaces:**

- Produces:

```cpp
namespace Arcane::Arc {
struct WaitArgs {
    std::string what;          // ready | settled | frames | reload | state
    std::uint32_t frames = 0;  // Optional
    std::string state;         // Optional
    std::uint32_t timeoutMs = 30000; // milliseconds, copied from Call::timeoutMs; host.wait does not scale
};

Envelope WaitPoll(const HostSession& session, const WaitArgs& args, std::uint64_t startedFrame,
                  std::uint32_t generationAtArm, bool hasRenderer);
}
```

`host.wait` copies `Call::timeoutMs` into `WaitArgs::timeoutMs` and does not scale it. `--timeout 1` on the CLI is 1000 ms on this job. The test that sets `args.timeoutMs = 0` is an in-process assignment meaning already timed out. `ParseGlobals` never stores 0.

`arc launch editor|runtime|server` adds `--arc` (and `--arc-descriptor` when the user passed one) to the child command line and polls until that pid's descriptor `IsLive` and `state` is `ready`, or `timeoutMs` elapses (`host.busy` exit 5). The exe path is beside `arc.exe`: `../ArcaneEditor/ArcaneEditor.exe`, `../ArcaneRuntime/ArcaneRuntime.exe`, `../ArcaneServer/ArcaneServer.exe`.

`host.quit` sets `RequestQuit`. The host loop notices `TakeQuit()` and exits `arc-quit`. `--force` is the CLI flag that sends `host.quit` with `args.force = true`. Editor dirty documents: a function pointer `bool (*dirty)(void*)` on `HostSession::Config`. When it returns true and `force` is false and `--discard-changes` was not passed, the command returns `host.busy` `detail.state = "modal"` exit 5 and does not quit. The pointer is a function pointer, not `std::function`.

`host.wait` is `ExecThread::Job`. It arms a job and binds the waiter. Each `StepAll` calls `WaitPoll` once.

- `ready`: done when `gate.State()` is `ready`, `playing`, or `paused`.
- `settled`: if `hasRenderer` is false, return `MakeError("host.wait", "capture.no-renderer", "this host has no renderer", "wait on an editor or a runtime")` immediately, exit 2, job `Failed`. If true, done when the host's `settled()` pointer (installed by editor and runtime, calling `SettleConverged` and `ShaderCompiler::IsIdle`) returns true.
- `frames`: done when `gate.frame >= startedFrame + args.frames`.
- `reload`: done when `pluginGeneration != generationAtArm`. `NoteReload` is what changes it. `Reload`'s return type stays `bool`.
- `state`: done when `gate.State() == args.state`.

Timeout: the step sees `now - started > timeoutMs`, finishes the job with `MakeOk("host.wait", {{"timedOut", true}, {"what", args.what}}, gate.Block())`, `ok` true, empty `errors`. `ExitFromHost` is 8. Do not put `wait.timeout` in `errors` (that would force `ok` false). The code `wait.timeout` is still mapped to 8 if present; the wire for this path is `data.timedOut`.

`n` is not this command. `what` missing → `arg.missing` exit 2.

```cpp
    session.Stop("test");
```
