
`WaitPoll` returns `data.done == false` when the condition is not met and the timeout has not fired, so the job step keeps the job `Running`. Timeout and success return a finished envelope. `timeoutMs == 0` means already timed out.

### Task 17: commands, list, describe, schema, call — and the offline manifest

**Files:**
- Create: `ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Arc/Cli/Commands.hpp`
- Create: `ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Arc/Cli/Commands.cpp`
- Test: `ArcaneTests/src/Arc/CommandsCliTest.cpp`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `CommandRegistry::Catalogue` (Task 4) for the **host** catalogue. A static table in this cpp for the **client** manifest.
- Produces:

```cpp
namespace Arcane::Arc::Cli {
nlohmann::json StaticManifest(); // no socket, no descriptor
int PrintStaticManifest(const GlobalOptions& opt); // exit 0
}
```

`arc commands --format json` with no host and no project prints `StaticManifest()` and returns 0. It does not call `ScanProject` and it does not return `target.none`. The manifest is a JSON array of `{name, summary, access}` for the P0 verbs: `status`, `launch`, `quit`, `wait`, `world`, `commands`, `list`, `describe`, `schema`, `call`, `cvar`, `console`, `logs`, `problems`, `entity`, `ecs`, `resource`, `scene`, `selection`, `stats`, `screenshot`, `compare`, `play`, `stop`, `pause`, `resume`, `step`. `arc commands` without `--format json` prints the same rows as human text and also returns 0.

`arc list`, `arc describe <cmd>`, and `arc schema component|resource <Name>` talk to a host: `GET /arc/v1/catalog`, then `catalog.describe` / `schema.type` via `POST /arc/v1/call`. No host → `target.none` exit 4. That is the opposite of `arc commands`.

`arc call <command>` sends `POST /arc/v1/call` with `{"command": <command>, "args": {...}}`. Unknown CLI flags are exit 2 (`arg.unknown`), not ignored.

`PrintStaticManifest` writes the JSON to a string the test can also read via a test seam `std::string RenderStaticManifest(const GlobalOptions&)`. `PrintStaticManifest` returns 0 and the string contains `"name"` and `"entity"`. Assert that. It must not try to open a socket: the function takes no `Scan` and no port.

### Task 18: `--all` fan-out (AM2)

**Files:**
- Create: `ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Arc/Cli/FanOut.hpp`
- Create: `ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Arc/Cli/FanOut.cpp`
- Test: `ArcaneTests/src/Arc/FanOutTest.cpp`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Scan`, `WorstExit`, `ExitFromHost`. `std::function` is allowed only in this client helper. Registered commands stay function pointers.
- Produces:

```cpp
namespace Arcane::Arc::Cli {
struct FanOutResult {
    nlohmann::json wrapper; // {"hosts":[{"pid","hostKind","envelope"}]}
    int exitCode = 0;
};

using FanOutPost = int (*)(const Descriptor& descriptor, const nlohmann::json& call,
                           Envelope* out, void* user);

FanOutResult FanOut(const Scan& scan, std::string_view hostKindFilter, bool allKinds,
                    const nlohmann::json& call, bool drive, bool yes, Format format,
                    FanOutPost post, void* user);
}
```

`--all` is every live host of the project. `--all editor` (the token after `--all` when it is a kind) filters. A Drive command (the static manifest's `access` is `drive`, or the call's command is in the drive set `cvar.set`, `console.exec`, `host.quit`, `play.*`, `capture.compare` with bless) requires `yes == true`. Otherwise `arg.inapplicable` exit 2 and **zero** sends. Observe does not require `--yes`.

The wrapper is `{hosts:[{pid, hostKind, envelope}]}`. Ndjson is one frame per host, then the result frame. `exitCode` is `WorstExit` of the per-host exits. A busy host (5) beside a dead host (7) exits 7.

The test does not open sockets. `post` is the function pointer in **Produces**. The first call's `drive` flag is false (`cvar.get` is observe). The second passes `drive = true` and `yes = false`.

```cpp
    a.descriptor.hostKind = Arcane::Arc::HostKind::Runtime;

    Arcane::Arc::Cli::LiveHost b = a;
    b.descriptor.pid = 2;
```

**Interfaces:**

```cpp
namespace Arcane::Arc {
struct WorldInfo {
    std::string name;
    std::string role;          // ToString(Runtime::Mode())
    Astra::Registry* registry; // not serialized
};

using WorldListFn = std::vector<WorldInfo> (*)(void* user);

struct WorldTable {
    WorldListFn fn = nullptr;
    void* user = nullptr;
};

Envelope WorldList(const WorldTable& table);
// data.worlds = [{name, role}] sorted by name. registry pointers are not in JSON.

enum class WorldPick { Ok, Missing, Unknown };
struct PickedWorld {
    WorldPick pick = WorldPick::Missing;
    WorldInfo world;
    Envelope error;
};

PickedWorld ResolveWorld(const WorldTable& table, const std::string& requested);
// requested empty and one world → that world.
// requested empty and several → arg.missing exit 2, error.data.worlds lists names.
// requested names one → that world.
// requested names none → arg.unknown exit 2, candidates are the names.
}
```

`HostSession::Config` gains `WorldTable worlds`. P0 hosts install one world. The check still handles two, so a second world cannot silently win. `world.list` is Observe, `needsFrame` false. Command name `world.list`.

### Task 20: `cvar.get`, `cvar.set`, `cvar.list`, `cvar.explain`

**Files:**
- Create: `ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Arc/Commands/CvarCommands.cpp`
- Modify: `ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Arc/HostSession.cpp` (call `RegisterCvarCommands` from `Start`)
- Modify: `arc/src/main.cpp`
- Test: `ArcaneTests/src/Arc/CvarCommandTest.cpp`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `RemoteCVarService::Handle` (Task 0). `CVarDesc` (`CVarRegistry.hpp:29`). `CVarFlags::Protected` (`CVarTypes.hpp:50`). `ApplyMode::Restart` (`CVarTypes.hpp:129`). `render.vsync` is PlayerSafe and inherits `ApplyMode::Restart` from the type attribute (`RenderDeviceSettings.hpp:89`, field `:93`). The spec example that says `"apply":"Live"` is the old shape.
- Produces:

```cpp
namespace Arcane::Arc {
struct CvarCommandUser {
    CVarRegistry* registry = nullptr;
    CVarContext context = CVarContext::Editor;
};
void SetCvarCommandUser(CvarCommandUser* user);
void RegisterCvarCommands(); // HostSession::Start calls this. A second call returns.
}
```

Contexts the hosts pass: `Editor` on ArcaneEditor, `LocalHost` on ArcaneRuntime, `ServerAdmin` on ArcaneServer.

`cvar.get` / `cvar.explain` / `cvar.list` are Observe, `needsFrame` false. `cvar.set` is Drive and `Mutates::Preferences` (`Register` rejects `Drive` with `Mutates::None`). Do not use `ARC_REMOTE_COMMAND` here: that macro runs at static init and collides with Task 4. `CommandRegistry::Get()` is the process singleton Task 4 owns, and `UnregisterModule("")` does not drop a host command, so a `cvar.get` registered before `main` stays registered in every later test. `RegisterCvarCommands` runs from `HostSession::Start` (Task 11).

The command builds a `RemoteCVarRequest` (`op`, `name`, `value`, `callerId`, `context` last) and copies the response. Protected get, in every context including Editor: `ok` false, `data.value` JSON null, `data.masked` `"<protected>"`, error code `cvar.protected`, `ExitFromHost` 3. `errors[0].message` is the service sentence (it contains `protected` and does not contain the secret). Unknown name: `cvar.unknown`, exit 6. A value the registry rejects as the wrong type: `cvar.type-mismatch`, exit 6. A successful set's `data` is `{name, type, value, previous, setBy, audience, apply, context}` and `undo` is `{label: null, undoable: false, reason: "not-undoable-command"}`. Explain fills `setBy`, `audience`, `apply`, `published`, `pending`, `history` from the structured response. `ToJson` of `UndoBlock` emits `label`, `undoable`, `transaction`, and `reason` when `reason` is non-empty.

CLI: `arc cvar get <name>`, `arc cvar set <name> <value>`, `arc cvar list [prefix]`, `arc cvar explain <name>`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

```cpp
#include <catch2/catch_test_macros.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Arc/Commands/CvarCommands.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Arc/Protocol.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Config/CVarRegistry.hpp>

static void AddBool(Arcane::CVarRegistry& reg, const char* name, bool def,
                     Arcane::Audience audience, Arcane::ApplyMode apply,
                     Arcane::CVarFlags flags = Arcane::CVarFlags::None) {
    Arcane::CVarDesc d;
    d.name = name;
    d.type = Arcane::CVarType::Bool;
    d.defaultValue = Arcane::CVarValue::Bool(def);
    d.audience = audience;
    d.apply = apply;
    d.flags = flags;
    d.help = name;
    REQUIRE_FALSE(reg.Register(d).IsStale());
}

    Arcane::Arc::CvarCommandUser user;
    user.registry = &reg;
    user.context = Arcane::CVarContext::Editor;

    Arcane::Arc::RegisterCvarCommands();

    call.command = "cvar.get";
    call.args = nlohmann::json{{"name", "editor.secret"}};
    const auto env = Arcane::Arc::CommandRegistry::Get().Invoke(call).envelope;
    CHECK_FALSE(env.ok);
    CHECK(env.data["value"].is_null());
    CHECK(env.data["masked"] == "<protected>");
    REQUIRE_FALSE(env.errors.empty());
    CHECK(env.errors[0].code == "cvar.protected");
    CHECK(env.errors[0].message.find("protected") != std::string::npos);
    CHECK(env.errors[0].message.find("editorhorse") == std::string::npos);
    CHECK(Arcane::Arc::ExitFromHost(env) == 3);
    CHECK_FALSE(env.data.contains("result"));
}

TEST_CASE("explain of render.vsync reports Restart and PlayerSafe", "[arc][cvar]") {
    Arcane::CVarRegistry reg;
    AddBool(reg, "render.vsync", true, Arcane::Audience::PlayerSafe, Arcane::ApplyMode::Restart);
    Arcane::Arc::CvarCommandUser user{&reg, Arcane::CVarContext::LocalHost};
    Arcane::Arc::SetCvarCommandUser(&user);
    Arcane::Arc::RegisterCvarCommands();
    Arcane::Arc::Call set;
    set.command = "cvar.set";
    set.args = nlohmann::json{{"name", "render.vsync"}, {"value", false}};
    const auto wrote = Arcane::Arc::CommandRegistry::Get().Invoke(set).envelope;
    CHECK(wrote.ok);
    CHECK(wrote.data["value"] == false);
    CHECK(wrote.data["apply"] == "Restart");
    CHECK(wrote.data["audience"] == "PlayerSafe");
    CHECK(wrote.data["setBy"] == "Console");
    CHECK(wrote.data["context"] == "LocalHost");
    CHECK(wrote.undo->undoable == false);
    CHECK(wrote.undo->reason == "not-undoable-command");
    const auto dumped = Arcane::Arc::ToJson(wrote);
    CHECK(dumped["undo"]["reason"] == "not-undoable-command");
    CHECK_FALSE(dumped.contains("hostFrame"));
    Arcane::Arc::Call bad;
    bad.command = "cvar.set";
    bad.args = nlohmann::json{{"name", "render.vsync"}, {"value", "nope"}};
    const auto mismatch = Arcane::Arc::CommandRegistry::Get().Invoke(bad).envelope;
    CHECK(mismatch.errors[0].code == "cvar.type-mismatch");
    CHECK(Arcane::Arc::ExitFromHost(mismatch) == 6);
    Arcane::Arc::Call missing;
    missing.command = "cvar.get";
    missing.args = nlohmann::json{{"name", "no.such.cvar"}};
    const auto unknown = Arcane::Arc::CommandRegistry::Get().Invoke(missing).envelope;
    CHECK(unknown.errors[0].code == "cvar.unknown");
    CHECK(Arcane::Arc::ExitFromHost(unknown) == 6);
}
```

### Task 21: `console.exec`

**Files:**
- Create: `ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Arc/Commands/ConsoleCommands.cpp`
- Modify: `arc/src/main.cpp`
- Test: `ArcaneTests/src/Arc/ConsoleCommandTest.cpp`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `CVarRegistry::RegisterCommand` (`CVarRegistry.hpp:353`) and `Execute` (`:504`). `CVarFlags::ServerCanExecute` (`CVarTypes.hpp:51`). `Arcane::CommandResult` (`CVarRegistry.hpp:154`) is the console reply. `Arcane::Arc::CommandResult` is the envelope. Do not mix them.
- Produces: `void RegisterConsoleCommand();` and `void SetConsoleCommandUser(CvarCommandUser*);` (same user struct as Task 20). Command name `console.exec`, Drive, `Mutates::Preferences`, `needsFrame` false. CLI: `arc console <line>`.

`Execute(line, context, SetBy::Console, &caller)` with `caller.id` equal to `call.callerId`. `cvar_explain` is refused: the envelope is `ok` false, code `command.unavailable`, and the registry command does not run. A command registered with `ServerCanExecute` runs and its text lands in `data.text`. A command without that flag is `permission.denied` exit 3. `undo.reason` is `not-undoable-command`.

### Task 22: `logs` (including `--follow`) and `problems` (including `--search` and `--follow`)

**Files:**
- Create: `ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Arc/Commands/LogCommands.cpp`
- Create: `ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Arc/Commands/ProblemCommands.cpp`
- Create: `ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Arc/Follow.hpp`
- Create: `ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Arc/Follow.cpp`
- Modify: `ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Arc/HostSession.cpp` (call `ApplyFollowEvents` at the start of `Drain`)
- Modify: `arc/src/main.cpp`
- Test: `ArcaneTests/src/Arc/LogCommandTest.cpp`
- Test: `ArcaneTests/src/Arc/ProblemCommandTest.cpp`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Log::ReadSince` / `Log::Engine()` (Task 2). `Log::FileSinkPath()` (`Log.hpp:103`). `DiagnosticStore::Filtered` (`DiagnosticStore.hpp:69` after the move in Task 1; the method is the one at the editor header line 69). `MatchesDiagnosticFilter` (`DiagnosticStore.hpp:48`). `Diagnostics::Publish` (`Diagnostics.hpp:812`). `DiagLocator::Entity` (`Diagnostics.hpp:760`). `JobTable::Arm`, `SetSubscriber`, `CancelBySubscriber` (Task 9). `TakeSseEvents`, `EnqueueSse`, `SseLife` (Task 6).
- Produces: `log.read` and `problems.list`, both Observe, `needsFrame` false, one-shot. `args.follow == true` on either command is `arg.inapplicable`, exit 2, `completesJob` 0, and arms nothing. Ruling P2 (f): the only follow path is `GET /arc/v1/stream`. `ApplyFollowEvents(JobTable& jobs, ListenerAtoms& atoms)` takes the SSE events. An `Opened` event whose topic is `logs` or `problems` arms one job, `SetSubscriber` to that event's id, and the step returns false. A `Closed` event calls `CancelBySubscriber` for that id. The step publishes new records with `EnqueueSse` and does not outlive the socket. CLI: `arc logs [--since N] [--level info|warn|error] [--follow]`, `arc problems [--severity error|warning|info] [--search TEXT] [--follow]`. `--follow` opens `GET /arc/v1/stream?topics=logs` or `topics=problems` and prints each SSE `data:` line as ndjson until the process timeout or the socket closes. It does not `POST /arc/v1/call` `log.read` or `problems.list` with `follow`.

`log.read` data is `{records:[{seq, time, level, logger, text}], dropped}`. `--since` is exclusive. A gap between `since` and the oldest retained seq sets `dropped` to that gap. `--level` drops records below it. When the client has no live host, it reads `FileSinkPath()` if that file exists and adds notification `{code:"logs.from-file", message: <path>}`. It does not invent lines.

`problems.list` calls `store.Filtered(mask, search)`. `--search` is that second argument, the same string `MatchesDiagnosticFilter` sees. `--severity error` is `SeverityMask::Error`. A row whose `locator.kind` is `Entity`: if that entity is alive and has `Identity`, `data` carries `locator.guid` as `{"hi","lo"}`; otherwise `locator.guid` is null, `locator.handle` is the packed id, and `warnings` contains `entity.no-identity`.

```cpp
TEST_CASE("log.read returns the ring and follow on the call is refused", "[arc]") {
    Arcane::Log::Engine()->info("arc-log-read-marker");
    Arcane::Arc::RegisterLogCommand();

    CHECK(once.envelope.ok);
    CHECK(once.completesJob == 0);

TEST_CASE("the SSE subscriber owns the follow job", "[arc][listener]") {
    Arcane::Arc::RequestQueue queue;
    Arcane::Arc::ListenerAtoms atoms;
    atoms.status.store(std::make_shared<const Arcane::Arc::HostStatus>());
    Arcane::Arc::ArcListener listener(queue, atoms, Arcane::Arc::MakeToken());
    listener.Start();
    REQUIRE(Arcane::InitSockets());
    const SocketType client = ::socket(AF_INET, SOCK_STREAM, IPPROTO_TCP);
    sockaddr_in addr{};
    addr.sin_family = AF_INET;
    addr.sin_port = htons(listener.Port());
    inet_pton(AF_INET, "127.0.0.1", &addr.sin_addr);
    REQUIRE(::connect(client, reinterpret_cast<sockaddr*>(&addr), sizeof(addr)) == 0);
    const std::string req = "GET /arc/v1/stream?topics=logs HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: 127.0.0.1:" +
        std::to_string(listener.Port()) + "\r\nAuthorization: Bearer " + listener.Token() + "\r\n\r\n";
    REQUIRE(::send(client, req.data(), static_cast<int>(req.size()), 0) == static_cast<int>(req.size()));
    std::string headers;
    char buf[1024];
    while (headers.find("\r\n\r\n") == std::string::npos) {
        const int n = ::recv(client, buf, sizeof(buf), 0);
        REQUIRE(n > 0);
        headers.append(buf, buf + n);
    }
    CHECK(headers.find("200") != std::string::npos);
    CHECK(headers.find("text/event-stream") != std::string::npos);
    Arcane::Arc::JobTable jobs;
    Arcane::Arc::SetLogJobs(&jobs);
    Arcane::Arc::ApplyFollowEvents(jobs, atoms);
    Arcane::Arc::JobId followId{};
    const Arcane::Arc::Job* opened = nullptr;
    for (std::uint64_t id = 1; id < 8 && opened == nullptr; ++id) {
        const Arcane::Arc::Job* candidate = jobs.Find(Arcane::Arc::JobId{id});
        if (candidate && candidate->subscriberId != 0) {
            opened = candidate;
            followId = candidate->id;
        }
    }
    REQUIRE(opened != nullptr);
    CHECK(opened->state == Arcane::Arc::JobState::Running);
    CHECK(opened->command == "log.follow");
    Arcane::Log::Engine()->info("arc-follow-while-open");
    Arcane::Arc::HostGate gate;
    jobs.StepAll(gate);
    std::string pushed;
    const DWORD timeoutMs = 1000;
    ::setsockopt(client, SOL_SOCKET, SO_RCVTIMEO, reinterpret_cast<const char*>(&timeoutMs), sizeof(timeoutMs));
    const int n = ::recv(client, buf, sizeof(buf), 0);
    if (n > 0) pushed.assign(buf, buf + n);
    CHECK(pushed.find("arc-follow-while-open") != std::string::npos);
    CloseSocket(client);
    Arcane::Arc::JobState state = Arcane::Arc::JobState::Running;
    for (int i = 0; i < 50; ++i) {
        Arcane::Arc::ApplyFollowEvents(jobs, atoms);
        const Arcane::Arc::Job* now = jobs.Find(followId);
        REQUIRE(now != nullptr);
        state = now->state;
        if (state == Arcane::Arc::JobState::Cancelled)
            break;
        ::Sleep(20);
    }
    CHECK(state == Arcane::Arc::JobState::Cancelled);
    Arcane::Log::Engine()->info("arc-follow-after-close");
    jobs.StepAll(gate);
    CHECK(jobs.Find(followId)->state == Arcane::Arc::JobState::Cancelled);
    listener.Stop();
}

    std::filesystem::remove(path);
}
```

`--search` is passed through to `Filtered` unchanged. `args.follow` on `log.read` and `problems.list` returns `arg.inapplicable` and does not arm. `ApplyFollowEvents` is the only arming path: topic `logs` arms command `log.follow`, topic `problems` arms command `problem.follow`, both with `SetSubscriber(id)`. Any other topic arms nothing. `Closed` calls `CancelBySubscriber`. The step calls `EnqueueSse(subscriberId, line)` for records newer than `progress.done` and returns false. `HostSession::Drain` calls `ApplyFollowEvents` before `dispatcher.Drain`. ### Task 23: `entity.get`, `ecs.query` (`--fields`), `ecs.stats`, `ecs.archetypes`

Resources are Task 24. This task does not register `resource.list` or `resource.get`.

**Files:**
- Create: `ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Arc/Commands/EcsCommands.cpp`
- Modify: `arc/src/main.cpp`
- Test: `ArcaneTests/src/Arc/EcsCommandTest.cpp`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `ResolveWorld` (Task 19). `Registry::CreateEntity` (`Registry.hpp:95`), `AddComponent` (`:338`), `GetComponentByHash` (`:857`), `HasComponentByHash` (`:913`), `GetEntityManager` (the range `EntityList.cpp:118` walks). `Inspector::Capture` (`Inspector.hpp:82`), `ArchetypeInfo` (`:47`). `ReflectionJsonWriter` (`ReflectionJson.hpp:351`). Quat JSON is `[x,y,z,w]` (`ReflectionJson.hpp:217`). `Identity` (`Components.hpp:284`). `Transform` (`Components.hpp:50`). `Guid` (`Guid.hpp:23`).
- Produces:

```cpp
namespace Arcane::Arc {
std::string ShortName(std::string_view qualified); // text after the last "::", or the whole string
enum class NamePick : std::uint8_t { One, None, Many };
struct NameResolution {
    NamePick pick = NamePick::None;
    std::string qualified;
    std::vector<std::string> candidates; // sorted qualified names
};
NameResolution PickComponent(const std::vector<std::string>& qualifiedNames, std::string_view requested);
void RegisterEcsCommands();
}
```

`PickComponent`: one qualified name whose `ShortName` equals `requested`, or whose full string equals `requested`, is `One`. Two or more short-name hits are `Many` and `candidates` lists the qualified names. Zero is `None`.

`entity.get` args: `entity` (`guid:` or `name:` or a raw integer handle), `components` (array; empty means every reflected component on the entity). `guid:` matches `Identity.id`. `name:` must match exactly one `Identity.name`, else `entity.ambiguous` exit 2 with `candidates`. Unknown guid or name: `entity.not-found` exit 6. A raw handle addresses an entity with no `Identity` and adds warning `entity.no-identity`; `data.entity.guid` is null and `data.entity.handle` is the id. `data.entity.guid` when present is `{"hi","lo"}`. `data.components` is an object keyed by short name. Rotation arrays have 4 numbers, x,y,z,w.

`ecs.query` args: `with` (array), `without` (array), `fields` (array of `Component.member`), `limit` (default 100), `strict` (default true). Unknown component when `strict`: `component.unknown` exit 6. `--no-strict` (`strict:false`): warning `component.unknown`, `ok` true, `rows` empty. `data` is `{count, truncated, rows:[{guid, name, components}]}`. `count` is the match count before the limit. `truncated` is true when `rows.size() < count` or the JSON exceeded the inline cap. Default inline cap is 262144 bytes. Over the cap, `rows` is `[]`, `truncated` is true, and one artifact `kind:"ecs"` holds the rows. Sort matches by `guid.hi`, then `guid.lo`, then `name`. `--fields Transform.position` keeps `position` and drops `rotation` and `scale`.

`ecs.stats` data is `{entityCount, archetypeCount, chunkCount, bytesUsed}` from `Inspector::Capture`. `ecs.archetypes` data is `{archetypes:[{signature, entityCount, chunkCount}]}`. All four commands are Observe, `needsFrame` false, and call `ResolveWorld` first.

CLI: `arc entity get <ref> [--component C]`, `arc ecs query --with A,B [--without C] [--fields A.x,B] [--limit N] [--no-strict]`, `arc ecs stats`, `arc ecs archetypes`.

```cpp
TEST_CASE("ecs.query --fields keeps one member and entity.get reads Transform", "[arc][ecs]") {
    Astra::Registry reg;

    Arcane::Transform xf;
    xf.position = glm::vec3(0.4f, -0.3f, 0.0f);
    xf.rotation = glm::quat(1.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f);
    xf.scale = glm::vec3(4.0f, 5.0f, 6.0f);
    reg.AddComponent<Arcane::Transform>(entity, xf);
    Arcane::Arc::WorldInfo world;
    world.name = "main";
    world.role = "Standalone";
    world.registry = &reg;
    Arcane::Arc::SetEcsWorld(world);
    Arcane::Arc::RegisterEcsCommands();

    Arcane::Arc::Call query;
    query.command = "ecs.query";
    query.args = nlohmann::json{
        {"with", nlohmann::json::array({"Transform"})},
        {"fields", nlohmann::json::array({"Transform.position"})},
        {"limit", 100}};
    const auto rows = Arcane::Arc::CommandRegistry::Get().Invoke(query).envelope;
    CHECK(rows.ok);
    REQUIRE(rows.data["rows"].size() == 1);
    const auto& comp = rows.data["rows"][0]["components"]["Transform"];
    CHECK(comp["position"][0] == Approx(0.4).margin(1e-4));
    CHECK(comp["position"][1] == Approx(-0.3).margin(1e-4));
    CHECK_FALSE(comp.contains("scale"));
    CHECK_FALSE(comp.contains("rotation"));
    CHECK(rows.data["rows"][0]["name"] == "MeshCube");

    Arcane::Arc::Call get;

    const auto failed = Arcane::Arc::CommandRegistry::Get().Invoke(strict).envelope;
    CHECK_FALSE(failed.ok);

    CHECK(Arcane::Arc::ExitFromHost(failed) == 6);

    Arcane::Arc::Call missing;
    missing.command = "entity.get";
    missing.args = nlohmann::json{{"entity", "name:NoSuchEntity"}};

    CHECK(absent.errors[0].code == "entity.not-found");

}
```

A null selection pointer returns `permission.denied` exit 3, not `command.unknown`. CLI: `arc scene tree [--depth N]`, `arc selection`.

`scene.tree` data is `{roots:[{guid, name, handle, children:[...]}]}`. Roots are entities where `HasParent` is false. Children come from `GetChildren`. Siblings are sorted by `Identity.id` (`hi`, then `lo`); an entity without `Identity` sorts last and adds warning `entity.no-identity` with `guid` null. `--depth` stops the walk. Depth 0 is roots only.

`editor.selection.get` data is `{entities:[...], primary}`. Order is `Entities()` order, not sorted. `primary` is the primary entity's guid object, or null when the selection is empty.

### Task 27: `screenshot viewport|window` as a render-ticket readback job

ABI: next ABI after main at execution time; expected 56 then 57 once feat/physics-events (55) merges; re-read main, restamp both manifests. This task does not bump. Tasks 0 and 10 bump. Task 30 checks the manifests.

**Files:**
- Create: `ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Arc/Commands/CaptureCommands.cpp`
- Modify: `ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Assets/ImageIo.hpp` (add inline `NormalizeCaptureRows` next to `WritePngRgba` at line 43. Add `#include <cstring>`.)
- Modify: `ArcaneClient/src/Arcane/Render/Nri/NriGraphContext.cpp` (`ReadCapture` at 2073 calls `NormalizeCaptureRows` in place of the loop at 2103–2124. `DeviceWaitIdle` at 2093 stays in this function only.)
- Modify: `ArcaneEditor/src/App/EditorApp.cpp` (install the viewport and window hooks beside `CaptureGraphViewportPng` at line 2050. That function stays the cover path. Arc does not call it.)
- Modify: `ArcaneRuntime/src/RuntimeFrame.cpp` (the `--screenshot` write stays `WritePngRgba` at lines 930–933 and 1071–1074. Arc adds a second, slotted readback. It does not call that write.)
- Modify: `ArcaneRuntime/src/RuntimeApp.cpp` (the render half at line 829 publishes retired tickets. `AdvanceSim` at 814 does not.)
- Test: `ArcaneTests/src/Arc/CaptureCommandTest.cpp`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `JobTable` (Task 9). `Job::progress.label`. `Dispatcher` order is `StepAll` then commands (Task 8), so the `Drain` that runs the command does not step the job it just armed. `needsFrame` true, so Task 13's minimized gate returns `host.busy` / `state:"minimized"` / exit 5 before `Arm`. `WritePngRgba` (`ImageIo.hpp:43`) and `LoadPngRgba` (`:37`) run on the completing step. `Sha256Hex` (Task 8) hashes the PNG file bytes. `FramesInFlight()` is `FramePacing.hpp:42` (latched default 2, `RenderDeviceSettings.hpp:80`). `kMaxFramesInFlight` is 3 (`FramePacing.hpp:38`). Ruling P3 (a) and (b).
- Produces:

```cpp
namespace Arcane::Arc {
struct CaptureMapped {
    std::uint32_t width = 0, height = 0, rowPitch = 0;
    bool bgra = false;
    const std::uint8_t* mapped = nullptr; // null and drain returns true: the map failed
    std::uint32_t mappedBytes = 0;
};
using ArmCopyFn = bool (*)(std::uint64_t ticket, void* user);
using DrainSlotFn = bool (*)(std::uint64_t ticket, CaptureMapped* raw, void* user);
struct CaptureHooks {
    std::uint32_t framesInFlight = 2; // host ring only; the job does not wait on host.frame
    ArmCopyFn armViewport = nullptr, armWindow = nullptr, armHost = nullptr;
    DrainSlotFn drainViewport = nullptr, drainWindow = nullptr, drainHost = nullptr;
    bool occludedRisk = false;
    void* user = nullptr;
    std::filesystem::path outDir;
};
void SetCaptureHooks(CaptureHooks hooks);
void RegisterCaptureCommands(JobTable* jobs);
CommandResult CaptureBusy(const Call& call);
bool CaptureInFlight(const JobTable& jobs);
}

namespace Arcane {
inline void NormalizeCaptureRows(const std::uint8_t* mapped,
    std::uint32_t width, std::uint32_t height,
    std::uint32_t rowPitch, bool bgra,
    std::vector<unsigned char>& rgba)
{
    rgba.resize(static_cast<std::size_t>(width) * height * 4);
    for (std::uint32_t y = 0; y < height; ++y)
    {
        const std::uint8_t* src = mapped + static_cast<std::size_t>(y) * rowPitch;
        unsigned char* dst = rgba.data() + static_cast<std::size_t>(y) * width * 4;
        std::memcpy(dst, src, static_cast<std::size_t>(width) * 4);
        if (bgra)
        {
            for (std::uint32_t x = 0; x < width; ++x)
            {
                unsigned char* p = dst + static_cast<std::size_t>(x) * 4;
                const unsigned char b = p[0];
                p[0] = p[2];
                p[2] = b;
            }
        }
    }
}
}
```

`capture.viewport` and `capture.window` are Observe, `ExecThread::Job`, `needsFrame` true. The command validates args. The matching source is one pair: viewport is `armViewport` + `drainViewport`, window is `armWindow` + `drainWindow`. `armHost` + `drainHost` are the pixels `--compare` captures (Task 28). Server leaves all six pointers null. When either pointer of the pair is null the command returns immediately: `ok` false, code `capture.no-renderer`, `ExitFromHost` 2, `completesJob` 0, and it does not `Arm`. When both are set, it `Arm`s a job with `total` 1 and returns `completesJob` set. The command function does not call either pointer. Ruling P4 (a). One capture or compare is in flight per host, not per source. `capture.viewport`, `capture.window`, and `capture.compare` share that slot, because the ring has one `wanted`. `armViewport`, `armWindow`, and `armHost` are the same slot. After the null-hook `capture.no-renderer` return and before `JobTable::Arm`, `CaptureInFlight(*jobs)` true means the command returns `CaptureBusy(call)`: `ok` false, code `host.busy`, `errors[0].detail["state"]` = `"capture-pending"`, `ExitFromHost` 5, `completesJob` 0. It does not call `Arm` and it does not call `arm`, so `ring.wanted` is not overwritten by a second ticket and no second drain waits on a lost ticket. Minimized `host.busy` with `state` `"minimized"` (exit 5) still happens first, in the dispatcher `needsFrame` check (Task 13), and does not reach this command. `CaptureInFlight` is `jobs.AnyRunning({"capture.viewport", "capture.window", "capture.compare"})`. `CaptureBusy` is the helper below. A host passes one `JobTable*` into both `RegisterCaptureCommands` and `RegisterCompareCommand`.

The job step is the PickNode ticket, split across render submissions. Ruling P3 (a). `PickNode::RecordReadback` (`PickOutlineNodes.cpp:617-693`) drains the slot with `memcpy` at `:639-641`, publishes `m_ticket[frameSlot]` at `:645`, then records `CmdReadbackTextureToBuffer` and stores the ticket at `:692`. The comment at `:628-635` is the rule: no fence query, no idle. The job does not compute a slot and does not read `gate.frame`. The host render loop owns the slot (`submission % framesInFlight`). The command `new`s a `CaptureJobState { arm, drain, hookUser, ticket, outDir, occludedRisk }` and passes it as `job.user`. `hookUser` is `CaptureHooks::user` (the test's `CapUser`), not the state itself. `progress.label` is the phase. `progress.done` stays the job-progress counter; it is not the ticket. The step `delete`s the state when it returns true. It does not write `job.startedFrame`.

- Label is not `"copied"`. `ticket = NextCaptureTicket()`, a monotonic `std::atomic<std::uint64_t>` starting at 1. `0` is never issued, so "not armed" stays distinct from a stored PickNode ticket. Call `arm(ticket, hookUser)`. Store `state.ticket`. Set `progress.label = "copied"`. Return false. The job stays `Running`. A false arm finishes `Failed` on this step: `capture.no-frame`, exit 6, the drain pointer is not called, and the state is deleted.
- Label is `"copied"`. Call `drain(state.ticket, &raw, hookUser)`. Do not read `gate.frame`. Do not call `arm` again. A false return means that ticket has not been retired yet: stay `Running`, leave the state allocated, and do not finish `capture.no-frame`.
- A true return with `raw.mapped == nullptr` finishes `Failed`, `capture.no-frame`, exit 6, and deletes the state.
- A true return with `raw.mapped != nullptr` calls `NormalizeCaptureRows(raw.mapped, raw.width, raw.height, raw.rowPitch, raw.bgra, rgba)`, then `WritePngRgba` and `Sha256Hex` of the PNG bytes. The artifact is `{kind:"capture", path, bytes, sha256, width, height, format:"RGBA8_UNORM"}`. The PNG is tight RGBA, so the format string is `RGBA8_UNORM`. `occludedRisk` adds warning `capture.occluded-risk` and still `ok` true. Delete the state and return true.

`CaptureGraphViewportPng` (`EditorApp.cpp:2050`) calls `ReadCapture` (`NriGraphContext.cpp:2073`), and `ReadCapture` calls `DeviceWaitIdle` at `:2093`. That path stays the cover and the runtime `--screenshot` path. The arc hooks do not call `ReadCapture`, do not call `DeviceWaitIdle`, and do not query a fence. `ReadCapture`'s loop at `:2103-2124` is replaced by one call:

```cpp
NormalizeCaptureRows(mapped, m_captureWidth, m_captureHeight, m_captureRowPitch,
                     IsBgraFormat(m_format), rgba);
core.UnmapBuffer(*m_capture);
```

`IsBgraFormat` stays at `:79-82`. Do not leave a second copy of the loop.

The editor and the runtime each own one `HOST_READBACK` of `kMaxFramesInFlight` slices and one ring:

```cpp
struct CaptureRing {
    std::uint64_t ticket[3]{};
    bool pending[3]{};
    std::uint64_t wanted = 0;
    std::uint64_t retiredTicket = 0;
    bool haveRetired = false;
    std::vector<std::uint8_t> retiredBytes;
    std::uint32_t retiredW = 0, retiredH = 0, retiredPitch = 0;
    bool retiredBgra = false;
    std::uint32_t submission = 0;
    std::uint32_t framesInFlight = 2;
};
```

`arm` stores `ring.wanted = ticket` and returns true. It does not record a copy itself. A second capture or compare does not reach this assignment: the command returns `host.busy` `capture-pending` before `JobTable::Arm`, so `arm` is not called and `wanted` is never overwritten by a second ticket. There is no second drain waiting on a lost ticket. The render submit calls `NoteRenderSubmission` and nothing else does. Slot is `ring.submission % ring.framesInFlight`. If `pending[slot]`, `memcpy` that slice the way `PickOutlineNodes.cpp:639-641` copies its dword, set `retiredTicket = ticket[slot]`, `haveRetired = true`, and clear `pending[slot]`. If `wanted != 0`, record `CmdReadbackTextureToBuffer` with `layout.offset = slot * slicePitch` (today's single copy is `NriGraphContext.cpp:1566-1576`), store `ticket[slot] = wanted`, set `pending[slot]`, and clear `wanted`. Then increment `submission`. `drain` returns false until `haveRetired && retiredTicket == ticket`. It then fills `CaptureMapped` from the retired bytes and returns true. The pacing wait inside backbuffer acquire is what makes the slot safe. The editor calls `NoteRenderSubmission` from the viewport graph submit on every non-minimized frame, including while the editor is not playing. The runtime calls it from `RuntimeFrame::RenderGraph` (`RuntimeApp.cpp:829`). That call still runs when the sim is idle: `AdvanceSim` is `:814`, and the `skipFrame` continues at `:809` and `:823` are the minimized path, which does not render. Do not call `NoteRenderSubmission` from `Advance`, `AdvanceSim`, `Consume`, or the play tick. `host.frame` still counts sim advances only. A headless runtime has one target, so its viewport, window, and host pairs are the same two functions. The server installs null hooks. Idle editor and idle headless runtime therefore retire tickets. Task 30 is the live proof: the editor bless, before any play or step, keeps `host.frame` equal to the settled frame, and a runtime `screenshot window` with no `--frames` exits 0 while `stats.frame` stays put.

CLI: `arc screenshot viewport [--out FILE]`, `arc screenshot window [--out FILE]`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

```cpp
#include <catch2/catch_test_macros.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Arc/Commands/CaptureCommands.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Arc/Dispatcher.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Assets/ImageIo.hpp>
#include "ArcDemoArgs.hpp"
#include <cstdint>
#include <filesystem>
#include <vector>

static const std::uint8_t kProbe[12] = {10, 20, 30, 255, 1, 2, 3, 40, 9, 9, 9, 9};
static const std::uint8_t kProbeB[12] = {40, 50, 60, 255, 7, 8, 9, 40, 9, 9, 9, 9};

struct CapUser {
    int copies = 0;
    int reads = 0;
    int idles = 0;
    std::uint64_t armedTicket = 0;
    std::uint64_t wanted = 0;
    bool retired = false;
    bool failMap = false;
};

static bool ArmSlot(std::uint64_t ticket, void* user) {
    auto* u = static_cast<CapUser*>(user);
    ++u->copies;
    u->armedTicket = ticket;
    u->wanted = ticket;
    return true;
}
static bool DrainSlot(std::uint64_t ticket, Arcane::Arc::CaptureMapped* raw, void* user) {
    auto* u = static_cast<CapUser*>(user);
    if (!u->retired || ticket != u->armedTicket)
        return false;
    raw->width = 2;
    raw->height = 1;
    raw->rowPitch = 12;
    raw->bgra = true;
    if (u->failMap) {
        raw->mapped = nullptr;
        raw->mappedBytes = 0;
        return true;
    }
    ++u->reads;
    raw->mapped = kProbe;
    raw->mappedBytes = sizeof(kProbe);
    return true;
}
static Arcane::Arc::CommandResult Ping(const Arcane::Arc::Call& call, void*) {
    Arcane::Arc::CommandResult r;
    r.envelope = Arcane::Arc::MakeOk(call.command, nlohmann::json::object(), {});
    return r;
}
static bool ArmNo(std::uint64_t, void* user) {
    ++static_cast<CapUser*>(user)->copies;
    return false;
}

    CHECK(rgba[1] == 20);
    CHECK(rgba[2] == 10);
    CHECK(rgba[3] == 255);
    CHECK(rgba[4] == 3);
    CHECK(rgba[5] == 2);
    CHECK(rgba[6] == 1);
    CHECK(rgba[7] == 40);
}

struct OverlapUser {
    int copies = 0;
    int reads = 0;
    int frames = 0;
    int framesInFlight = 2;
    std::uint64_t wanted = 0;
    std::uint64_t ticket[2]{};
    int retireFrame[2]{-1, -1};
};

static bool ArmOverlap(std::uint64_t ticket, void* user) {
    auto* u = static_cast<OverlapUser*>(user);
    REQUIRE(u->copies < 2);
    u->ticket[u->copies] = ticket;
    u->wanted = ticket;
    u->retireFrame[u->copies] = u->frames + u->framesInFlight;
    ++u->copies;
    return true;
}

static bool DrainOverlap(std::uint64_t ticket, Arcane::Arc::CaptureMapped* raw, void* user) {
    auto* u = static_cast<OverlapUser*>(user);
    int slot = -1;
    if (u->copies > 0 && ticket == u->ticket[0])
        slot = 0;
    else if (u->copies > 1 && ticket == u->ticket[1])
        slot = 1;
    if (slot < 0 || u->frames < u->retireFrame[slot])
        return false;
    raw->width = 2;
    raw->height = 1;
    raw->rowPitch = 12;
    raw->bgra = true;
    raw->mapped = slot == 0 ? kProbe : kProbeB;
    raw->mappedBytes = 12;
    ++u->reads;
    return true;
}

static void ExpectPng(const Arcane::Arc::Artifact& art, unsigned char r, unsigned char g,
                       unsigned char b) {
    CHECK(art.format == "RGBA8_UNORM");
    CHECK(art.width == 2);
    CHECK(art.height == 1);
    CHECK(art.sha256.size() == 64);
    std::uint32_t w = 0, h = 0;
    std::vector<unsigned char> png;
    REQUIRE(Arcane::LoadPngRgba(art.path, w, h, png));
    CHECK(w == 2);
    CHECK(h == 1);
    REQUIRE(png.size() == 8);
    CHECK(png[0] == r);
    CHECK(png[1] == g);
    CHECK(png[2] == b);
    CHECK(png[3] == 255);
}

TEST_CASE("overlapping captures: B is capture-pending until A's ticket retires, then the retry succeeds", "[arc][capture]") {
    OverlapUser user;
    Arcane::Arc::JobTable jobs;
    Arcane::Arc::CaptureHooks hooks;
    hooks.framesInFlight = 2;
    hooks.armViewport = &ArmOverlap;
    hooks.drainViewport = &DrainOverlap;
    hooks.armWindow = &ArmOverlap;
    hooks.drainWindow = &DrainOverlap;
    hooks.user = &user;
    hooks.outDir = std::filesystem::temp_directory_path() / "arc-cap-busy";
    std::filesystem::create_directories(hooks.outDir);
    Arcane::Arc::SetCaptureHooks(hooks);
    Arcane::Arc::RegisterCaptureCommands(&jobs);
    auto& reg = Arcane::Arc::CommandRegistry::Get();
    Arcane::Arc::HostGate gate;
    gate.frame = 10;

    Arcane::Arc::Call captureA;
    captureA.command = "capture.window";
    captureA.args = nlohmann::json::object();
    const auto armed = reg.Invoke(captureA);
    REQUIRE(armed.completesJob != 0);
    jobs.StepAll(gate);
    const auto idA = Arcane::Arc::JobId{armed.completesJob};
    REQUIRE(jobs.Find(idA) != nullptr);
    CHECK(jobs.Find(idA)->state == Arcane::Arc::JobState::Running);
    CHECK(user.copies == 1);
    CHECK(user.reads == 0);
    CHECK(user.frames == 0);
    CHECK(user.wanted != 0);
    const auto ticketA = user.wanted;
    CHECK(user.retireFrame[0] == 2);
    CHECK(gate.frame == 10);

    Arcane::Arc::Call captureB;
    captureB.command = "capture.viewport";
    captureB.args = nlohmann::json::object();
    const auto busy = reg.Invoke(captureB);
    CHECK(busy.completesJob == 0);
    CHECK_FALSE(busy.envelope.ok);
    CHECK(busy.envelope.errors[0].code == "host.busy");
    CHECK(busy.envelope.errors[0].detail["state"] == "capture-pending");
    CHECK(Arcane::Arc::ExitFromHost(busy.envelope) == 5);
    CHECK(user.copies == 1);
    CHECK(user.reads == 0);
    CHECK(user.wanted == ticketA);
    CHECK(jobs.Find(idA)->state == Arcane::Arc::JobState::Running);
    CHECK(gate.frame == 10);

    for (int i = 0; i < 2; ++i) {
        ++user.frames;
        jobs.StepAll(gate);
    }
    REQUIRE(jobs.Find(idA) != nullptr);
    CHECK(jobs.Find(idA)->state == Arcane::Arc::JobState::Done);
    CHECK(jobs.Find(idA)->envelope.ok);
    CHECK(user.reads == 1);
    CHECK(user.frames == 2);
    CHECK(user.wanted == ticketA);
    CHECK(user.copies == 1);
    CHECK(gate.frame == 10);
    ExpectPng(jobs.Find(idA)->envelope.artifacts[0], 30, 20, 10);

    const auto retried = reg.Invoke(captureB);
    REQUIRE(retried.completesJob != 0);
    jobs.StepAll(gate);
    const auto idB = Arcane::Arc::JobId{retried.completesJob};
    CHECK(jobs.Find(idB)->state == Arcane::Arc::JobState::Running);
    CHECK(user.copies == 2);
    CHECK(user.wanted != ticketA);
    CHECK(user.retireFrame[1] == user.frames + 2);
    CHECK(user.reads == 1);
    CHECK(gate.frame == 10);
    for (int i = 0; i < 2; ++i) {
        ++user.frames;
        jobs.StepAll(gate);
    }
    CHECK(jobs.Find(idB)->state == Arcane::Arc::JobState::Done);
    CHECK(jobs.Find(idB)->envelope.ok);
    CHECK(user.reads == 2);
    CHECK(user.frames == 4);
    CHECK(gate.frame == 10);
    ExpectPng(jobs.Find(idB)->envelope.artifacts[0], 60, 50, 40);
    ExpectPng(jobs.Find(idA)->envelope.artifacts[0], 30, 20, 10);
}

TEST_CASE("a server with null hooks returns capture.no-renderer and arms nothing", "[arc][capture]") {
    CapUser user;
    Arcane::Arc::JobTable jobs;
    Arcane::Arc::SetCaptureHooks({});
    Arcane::Arc::RegisterCaptureCommands(&jobs);
    Arcane::Arc::Call call;
    call.command = "capture.window";
    const auto result = Arcane::Arc::CommandRegistry::Get().Invoke(call);
    CHECK(result.completesJob == 0);
    CHECK_FALSE(result.envelope.ok);
    CHECK(result.envelope.errors[0].code == "capture.no-renderer");
    CHECK(Arcane::Arc::ExitFromHost(result.envelope) == 2);
    CHECK(user.copies == 0);
    CHECK(user.reads == 0);
    CHECK(jobs.FinishedCount() == 0);
}

TEST_CASE("a false arm finishes capture.no-frame without a read", "[arc][capture]") {
    CapUser user;
    Arcane::Arc::JobTable jobs;
    Arcane::Arc::CaptureHooks hooks;
    hooks.framesInFlight = 2;
    hooks.armWindow = &ArmNo;
    hooks.drainWindow = &DrainSlot;
    hooks.user = &user;
    hooks.outDir = std::filesystem::temp_directory_path() / "arc-cap-arm";
    std::filesystem::create_directories(hooks.outDir);
    Arcane::Arc::SetCaptureHooks(hooks);
    Arcane::Arc::RegisterCaptureCommands(&jobs);
    Arcane::Arc::Call call;
    call.command = "capture.window";
    call.args = nlohmann::json::object();
    const auto armed = Arcane::Arc::CommandRegistry::Get().Invoke(call);
    REQUIRE(armed.completesJob != 0);
    Arcane::Arc::HostGate gate;
    gate.frame = 4;
    jobs.StepAll(gate);
    const auto* job = jobs.Find(Arcane::Arc::JobId{armed.completesJob});
    REQUIRE(job != nullptr);
    CHECK(job->state == Arcane::Arc::JobState::Failed);
    CHECK_FALSE(job->envelope.ok);
    CHECK(job->envelope.errors[0].code == "capture.no-frame");
    CHECK(Arcane::Arc::ExitFromHost(job->envelope) == 6);
    CHECK(user.copies == 1);
    CHECK(user.reads == 0);
    CHECK(gate.frame == 4);
}

TEST_CASE("a published ticket with a null map finishes capture.no-frame", "[arc][capture]") {
    CapUser user;
    user.failMap = true;
    Arcane::Arc::JobTable jobs;
    Arcane::Arc::CaptureHooks hooks;
    hooks.framesInFlight = 2;
    hooks.armWindow = &ArmSlot;
    hooks.drainWindow = &DrainSlot;
    hooks.user = &user;
    hooks.outDir = std::filesystem::temp_directory_path() / "arc-cap-drain";
    std::filesystem::create_directories(hooks.outDir);
    Arcane::Arc::SetCaptureHooks(hooks);
    Arcane::Arc::RegisterCaptureCommands(&jobs);
    Arcane::Arc::Call call;
    call.command = "capture.window";
    call.args = nlohmann::json::object();
    const auto armed = Arcane::Arc::CommandRegistry::Get().Invoke(call);
    REQUIRE(armed.completesJob != 0);
    Arcane::Arc::HostGate gate;
    gate.frame = 4;
    jobs.StepAll(gate);
    const auto id = Arcane::Arc::JobId{armed.completesJob};
    CHECK(jobs.Find(id)->state == Arcane::Arc::JobState::Running);
    CHECK(user.reads == 0);
    CHECK(gate.frame == 4);
    user.retired = true;
    jobs.StepAll(gate);
    const auto* job = jobs.Find(id);
    REQUIRE(job != nullptr);
    CHECK(job->state == Arcane::Arc::JobState::Failed);
    CHECK_FALSE(job->envelope.ok);
    CHECK(job->envelope.errors[0].code == "capture.no-frame");
    CHECK(Arcane::Arc::ExitFromHost(job->envelope) == 6);
    CHECK(user.reads == 0);
    CHECK(user.idles == 0);
    CHECK(gate.frame == 4);
}
```

`RegisterCaptureCommands` is safe to call twice. The second call keeps the latest hooks and the latest `JobTable*`. `DrainSlot` increments `reads` only when it copies bytes. A false return is "this ticket is not retired", not a failure. `failMap` returns true with a null `mapped` and does not increment `reads`. The `idles` field has no writer. It stays 0 because the step has no idle call. `gate.frame` is set to 10, or to 4, and every later check requires the same value. A step that waits for `gate.frame` to advance never finishes this test.

- [ ] **Step 2: Run the test to verify it fails**

Generate, Debug build, filter `the copy step reads nothing`. Expected: FAIL to compile.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the ticket job and the shared normalizer**

Paste `NormalizeCaptureRows` into `ImageIo.hpp` and replace the `ReadCapture` loop with the call in **Interfaces**. The command does not call the hooks. The first step only calls `arm(ticket)`. Later steps only call `drain` for that same ticket. `drain` returning false leaves the job `Running`. `drain` returning true with a null map finishes `Failed` with `capture.no-frame` and exit 6. `drain` returning true with bytes calls `NormalizeCaptureRows`, then `WritePngRgba` and `Sha256Hex`. Do not call `NriGraphContext::ReadCapture` (`NriGraphContext.cpp:2073`) or `DeviceWaitIdle` (`:2093`) from this job, from the editor hook, or from the runtime hook. Do not query a fence. `CaptureGraphViewportPng` (`EditorApp.cpp:2050`) and the runtime `--screenshot` writes (`RuntimeFrame.cpp:930-933` and `:1071-1074`) stay synchronous and are not on this path. Do not read `gate.frame` and do not write `startedFrame` in this step. The host ring is `CaptureRing` above. `NoteRenderSubmission` runs on the editor viewport submit and on `RuntimeFrame::RenderGraph` (`RuntimeApp.cpp:829`). It does not run from `AdvanceSim` (`:814`). Before `Arm`, `CaptureInFlight` of `capture.viewport`, `capture.window`, and `capture.compare` returns `CaptureBusy` and does not call `arm`. `CaptureBusy` sets `detail.state` to `capture-pending` and leaves `completesJob` 0.

- [ ] **Step 4: Run the test**

Filter `[arc][capture]`. Expected: PASS. On the arm step, copies is 1, reads is 0, idles is 0, `startedFrame` is 0, `gate.frame` is still 10, and `cap.ping` has already returned. The next step, with the ticket not retired, stays `Running` and reads stays 0. Reads becomes 1 only after the test sets `retired`. The PNG is width 2, height 1, and the first pixel is RGBA `(30, 20, 10, 255)`. Exit on the null hooks is 2. Exit on a false arm is 6 with reads 0. Exit on a published null map is 6 with reads 0. `gate.frame` is unchanged in every case. The overlap case arms capture A, refuses capture B with exit 5 and `capture-pending` while A's ticket is not retired (`copies` stays 1, so B was not run), advances two render frames until A retires, then retries B. Both PNGs are valid: A's first pixel is RGBA `(30, 20, 10, 255)` and B's is `(60, 50, 40, 255)`. `gate.frame` stays 10.

### Task 28: `compare`, pixel ratio, and the `--bless` round trip

ABI: next ABI after main at execution time; expected 56 then 57 once feat/physics-events (55) merges; re-read main, restamp both manifests. This task does not bump.

**Files:**
- Create: `ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Arc/Commands/CompareCommands.cpp`
- Modify: `arc/src/main.cpp`
- Test: `ArcaneTests/src/Arc/CompareCommandTest.cpp`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `ImageCompareOptions` (`ImageCompare.hpp:208`: `maxDiffPixels`, `maxDiffPixelRatio`; both unset means the budget is zero; if both are set the smaller budget wins). `CompareImages` (`:272`). `ImageCompareResult` (`:227`). `LoadPngRgba` / `WritePngRgba` (the functions `RuntimeApp.cpp:676` and `RuntimeFrame.cpp:932` already call). `CaptureHooks` (Task 27). `BlessReference` (`ReferenceImages.hpp:77`) and `ResolveReference` (`:71`) stay in Client. Core calls function pointers.
- Produces:

```cpp
namespace Arcane::Arc {
using ResolveFn = bool (*)(const std::filesystem::path& projectRoot, const std::string& slot,
                           std::filesystem::path* png, std::string* level, void* user);
using BlessFn = bool (*)(const std::filesystem::path& projectRoot, const std::string& slot,
                         std::uint32_t width, std::uint32_t height, const unsigned char* rgba, void* user);
struct CompareHooks {
    ResolveFn resolve = nullptr;
    BlessFn bless = nullptr;
    std::filesystem::path projectRoot;
    void* user = nullptr;
};
void SetCompareHooks(CompareHooks hooks);
void RegisterCompareCommand(JobTable* jobs);
}
```

Command `capture.compare`, Observe unless `args.bless` is true, in which case the dispatcher treats it as Drive (Task 18's `--yes` rule). `ExecThread::Job`, `needsFrame` true. Args: `reference` (required), `source` (`host` default, or `viewport`, or `window`), `maxDiffPixels`, `maxDiffPixelRatio`, `bless`. The source selects one Task 27 pair: `host` is `armHost`/`drainHost`, `viewport` is `armViewport`/`drainViewport`, `window` is `armWindow`/`drainWindow`. The job uses that same ticket step. The copy step calls `arm` only. `CompareImages` and `BlessFn` run only on the drain that returns the retired ticket's bytes. The job does not read `gate.frame`. Data is `{verdict, reference, resolvedLevel, triedPaths, diffPixels, diffRatio, maxLocalDifference, thresholds:{maxDiffPixels, maxDiffPixelRatio}}`. Verdict `Passed` / `PassedOnFallback` / `Skipped` is `ok` true, empty errors, exit 0. Verdict `Failed` is `ok` true, empty errors, exit 8.

When `args.bless` is false, the command calls `resolve` before `Arm`. A false resolve returns immediately: `ok` false, `compare.missing-reference`, exit 6, `completesJob` 0, and it does not call `arm`, `drain`, or `CompareImages`. When `args.bless` is true, a false resolve does not return that error. The job still arms, the completing drain still reads the pixels, `BlessFn` writes them, and the envelope is `ok` true, verdict `Passed`, exit 0. `CompareImages` is not called on that creating bless. A null arm or drain pointer for the source is `capture.no-renderer`, exit 2, `completesJob` 0, no job left `Running`. After that null-hook return and before resolve and before `Arm`, compare calls `CaptureInFlight`. True returns `CaptureBusy`: `host.busy`, `detail.state` `capture-pending`, exit 5, `completesJob` 0, and `arm` is not called. A screenshot that is `Running` and a compare that is `Running` are the same slot. `wanted` stays the first ticket. `RegisterCompareCommand`, like `RegisterCaptureCommands`, stores that `JobTable*` and a later call replaces it. A busy return does not call `resolve`.

A bless of a reference that did resolve calls `BlessFn` with the captured RGBA after `CompareImages`, on the completing drain. The editor's function calls `ResolveReference` (`ReferenceImages.hpp:71`) and `BlessReference` (`:77`). A missing safe name blesses the shared file: `ResolveReference` sets `blessTarget` to `projectRoot/Verify/References/<name>.png` when nothing exists (`ReferenceImages.cpp:65-69`). The unit test does not use the slot `editor-ui`. It uses `arc-cli-bless-probe` under a temp project. Acceptance (Task 30) uses `arc-cli-accept-scratch`.

CLI: `arc compare <reference> [--source host|viewport|window] [--bless] [--max-diff-pixels N] [--max-diff-pixel-ratio R]`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

```cpp
#include <catch2/catch_test_macros.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Arc/Commands/CompareCommands.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Arc/Commands/CaptureCommands.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Assets/ImageCompare.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Assets/ImageIo.hpp>
#include <cstdint>
#include <filesystem>
#include <vector>

static std::filesystem::path g_ref;
static int g_blesses = 0;
static int g_copies = 0;
static int g_reads = 0;
static std::uint64_t g_ticket = 0;
static bool g_retired = false;
static const std::uint8_t kWhite[4] = {255, 255, 255, 255};

static bool ArmWhite(std::uint64_t ticket, void*) {
    ++g_copies;
    g_ticket = ticket;
    g_retired = false;
    return true;
}
static bool DrainWhite(std::uint64_t ticket, Arcane::Arc::CaptureMapped* raw, void*) {
    if (!g_retired || ticket != g_ticket)
        return false;
    ++g_reads;
    raw->width = 1;
    raw->height = 1;
    raw->rowPitch = 4;
    raw->bgra = true;
    raw->mapped = kWhite;
    raw->mappedBytes = 4;
    return true;
}
static bool ResolveProbe(const std::filesystem::path&, const std::string& slot,
                         std::filesystem::path* png, std::string* level, void*) {
    if (slot != "arc-cli-bless-probe") return false;
    if (!std::filesystem::exists(g_ref)) return false;
    *png = g_ref;
    *level = "temp";
    return true;
}
static bool BlessProbe(const std::filesystem::path&, const std::string&,
                       std::uint32_t w, std::uint32_t h, const unsigned char* rgba, void*) {
    ++g_blesses;
    g_ref = std::filesystem::temp_directory_path() / "arc-cli-bless-probe.png";
    return Arcane::WritePngRgba(g_ref, w, h, rgba);
}

TEST_CASE("compare Failed is ok true exit 8, and bless then matches", "[arc][compare]") {
    g_blesses = 0;
    g_ref = std::filesystem::temp_directory_path() / "arc-cli-bless-probe-black.png";
    unsigned char black[4] = {0, 0, 0, 255};
    REQUIRE(Arcane::WritePngRgba(g_ref, 1, 1, black));
    Arcane::Arc::JobTable jobs;
    Arcane::Arc::CaptureHooks cap;
    cap.framesInFlight = 2;
    cap.armHost = &ArmWhite;
    cap.drainHost = &DrainWhite;
    cap.outDir = std::filesystem::temp_directory_path() / "arc-compare-out";
    std::filesystem::create_directories(cap.outDir);
    Arcane::Arc::SetCaptureHooks(cap);
    Arcane::Arc::CompareHooks hooks;
    hooks.resolve = &ResolveProbe;
    hooks.bless = &BlessProbe;
    hooks.projectRoot = std::filesystem::temp_directory_path();
    Arcane::Arc::SetCompareHooks(hooks);
    Arcane::Arc::RegisterCompareCommand(&jobs);
    auto run = [&](bool bless, double ratio) {
        g_copies = 0;
        g_reads = 0;
        g_retired = false;
        Arcane::Arc::Call call;
        call.command = "capture.compare";
        call.args = nlohmann::json{{"reference", "arc-cli-bless-probe"}, {"source", "host"}, {"bless", bless}};
        if (ratio >= 0.0)
            call.args["maxDiffPixelRatio"] = ratio;
        auto result = Arcane::Arc::CommandRegistry::Get().Invoke(call);
        REQUIRE(result.completesJob != 0);
        const auto id = Arcane::Arc::JobId{result.completesJob};
        Arcane::Arc::HostGate gate;
        gate.frame = 10;
        jobs.StepAll(gate);
        CHECK(g_copies == 1);
        CHECK(g_reads == 0);
        CHECK(gate.frame == 10);
        REQUIRE(jobs.Find(id) != nullptr);
        CHECK(jobs.Find(id)->state == Arcane::Arc::JobState::Running);
        CHECK(jobs.Find(id)->startedFrame == 0);
        CHECK_FALSE(jobs.Find(id)->envelope.data.contains("verdict"));
        jobs.StepAll(gate);
        CHECK(g_reads == 0);
        CHECK(gate.frame == 10);
        CHECK(jobs.Find(id)->state == Arcane::Arc::JobState::Running);
        g_retired = true;
        jobs.StepAll(gate);
        CHECK(g_reads == 1);
        CHECK(gate.frame == 10);
        return jobs.Find(id)->envelope;
    };
    const auto failed = run(false, -1.0);
    CHECK(failed.ok);
    CHECK(failed.errors.empty());
    CHECK(failed.data["verdict"] == "Failed");
    CHECK(Arcane::Arc::ExitFromHost(failed) == 8);
    const auto ratioPass = run(false, 1.0);
    CHECK(ratioPass.ok);
    CHECK(ratioPass.data["verdict"] == "Passed");
    CHECK(ratioPass.data["thresholds"]["maxDiffPixelRatio"] == 1.0);
    CHECK(Arcane::Arc::ExitFromHost(ratioPass) == 0);
    const auto blessed = run(true, -1.0);
    CHECK(g_blesses == 1);
    CHECK(blessed.ok);
    const auto again = run(false, -1.0);
    CHECK(again.ok);
    CHECK(again.data["verdict"] == "Passed");
    CHECK(Arcane::Arc::ExitFromHost(again) == 0);
    g_ref.clear();
    const auto created = run(true, -1.0);
    CHECK(created.ok);
    CHECK(created.errors.empty());
    CHECK(created.data["verdict"] == "Passed");
    CHECK(Arcane::Arc::ExitFromHost(created) == 0);
    CHECK(g_blesses == 2);
    CHECK(std::filesystem::exists(g_ref));
    g_copies = 0;
    g_reads = 0;
    g_ref.clear();
    Arcane::Arc::Call missingCall;
    missingCall.command = "capture.compare";
    missingCall.args = nlohmann::json{{"reference", "arc-cli-bless-probe"}, {"source", "host"}, {"bless", false}};
    const auto missing = Arcane::Arc::CommandRegistry::Get().Invoke(missingCall);
    CHECK(missing.completesJob == 0);
    CHECK(g_copies == 0);
    CHECK(g_reads == 0);
    CHECK_FALSE(missing.envelope.ok);
    CHECK(missing.envelope.errors[0].code == "compare.missing-reference");
    CHECK(Arcane::Arc::ExitFromHost(missing.envelope) == 6);
}

TEST_CASE("compare while a screenshot is in flight is host.busy capture-pending", "[arc][compare]") {
    g_copies = 0;
    g_reads = 0;
    g_retired = false;
    g_ticket = 0;
    g_blesses = 0;
    Arcane::Arc::JobTable jobs;
    Arcane::Arc::CaptureHooks cap;
    cap.framesInFlight = 2;
    cap.armWindow = &ArmWhite;
    cap.drainWindow = &DrainWhite;
    cap.armHost = &ArmWhite;
    cap.drainHost = &DrainWhite;
    cap.outDir = std::filesystem::temp_directory_path() / "arc-compare-busy";
    std::filesystem::create_directories(cap.outDir);
    Arcane::Arc::SetCaptureHooks(cap);
    Arcane::Arc::RegisterCaptureCommands(&jobs);
    Arcane::Arc::CompareHooks hooks;
    hooks.resolve = &ResolveProbe;
    hooks.bless = &BlessProbe;
    hooks.projectRoot = std::filesystem::temp_directory_path();
    Arcane::Arc::SetCompareHooks(hooks);
    Arcane::Arc::RegisterCompareCommand(&jobs);
    g_ref = std::filesystem::temp_directory_path() / "arc-cli-bless-probe-busy.png";
    unsigned char black[4] = {0, 0, 0, 255};
    REQUIRE(Arcane::WritePngRgba(g_ref, 1, 1, black));

    Arcane::Arc::Call shot;
    shot.command = "capture.window";
    shot.args = nlohmann::json::object();
    const auto armed = Arcane::Arc::CommandRegistry::Get().Invoke(shot);
    REQUIRE(armed.completesJob != 0);
    Arcane::Arc::HostGate gate;
    gate.frame = 10;
    jobs.StepAll(gate);
    CHECK(g_copies == 1);
    CHECK(g_reads == 0);
    const auto firstTicket = g_ticket;
    CHECK(firstTicket != 0);
    CHECK(gate.frame == 10);

    Arcane::Arc::Call cmp;
    cmp.command = "capture.compare";
    cmp.args = nlohmann::json{{"reference", "arc-cli-bless-probe"}, {"source", "host"}, {"bless", false}};
    const auto busy = Arcane::Arc::CommandRegistry::Get().Invoke(cmp);
    CHECK(busy.completesJob == 0);
    CHECK_FALSE(busy.envelope.ok);
    CHECK(busy.envelope.errors[0].code == "host.busy");
    CHECK(busy.envelope.errors[0].detail["state"] == "capture-pending");
    CHECK(Arcane::Arc::ExitFromHost(busy.envelope) == 5);
    CHECK(g_copies == 1);
    CHECK(g_ticket == firstTicket);
    CHECK(g_reads == 0);
    CHECK(gate.frame == 10);
}
```

The lambda `run` is test code. The first `StepAll` arms, at frame 10, and it must not read pixels or store a verdict. The second `StepAll` is the same frame with the ticket not retired, so reads stays 0 and there is still no verdict. The third `StepAll`, after `g_retired` is set, is the only step that calls `CompareImages` or `BlessFn`. `gate.frame` stays 10. White BGRA `(255, 255, 255, 255)` is still white after `NormalizeCaptureRows`, so the black reference fails and the ratio pass still passes. `Invoke` itself must not call either hook. The ratio `1.0` is the only budget set, so a fully different pixel still passes. The no-budget call fails. Bless writes the white capture over the reference the next resolve sees. A bless of a missing file still grabs, returns `Passed` and exit 0, and does not return `compare.missing-reference`. The same missing file without `--bless` returns before `Arm` and reads nothing. Do not call `BlessReference` on `editor-ui`.

- [ ] **Step 2: Run the test to verify it fails**

Generate, Debug build, filter `compare Failed is ok`. Expected: FAIL to compile.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the job**

Copy `maxDiffPixels` and `maxDiffPixelRatio` onto `ImageCompareOptions` only when the arg is present. Leave them unset otherwise, which is the zero budget. The compare job calls the Task 27 arm on its first step and the Task 27 drain on later steps. `drain` returning false leaves the job `Running`. `CompareImages` runs only on the drain that returns bytes, and only when resolve already returned a file. Before `CompareImages` or `BlessFn`, call `NormalizeCaptureRows` on those bytes, the same function Task 27's capture step calls. White BGRA `(255, 255, 255, 255)` stays white. The job does not read `gate.frame`. `BlessFn` runs on that same drain when `args.bless` is true and the drain returned pixels, and it receives the normalized RGBA. A creating bless (resolve returned false, `args.bless` true) skips `CompareImages`, calls `BlessFn`, and completes `Passed` / exit 0. Editor `BlessFn` calls `ResolveReference` then `BlessReference` (`ReferenceImages.hpp:77`). The backend argument is `CompareBackendName(m_config.backend)`, the same call `EditorAppFrame.cpp:285` already makes. An empty backend is refused (`ReferenceImages.cpp:40`). When the resolution's level is `None`, `blessTarget` is already `projectRoot/Verify/References/<name>.png` (`ReferenceImages.cpp:65-69`), so the first bless creates that shared file. The compare step keeps the armed ticket on its own `CaptureJobState` (Task 27), and it does not store that ticket in `progress.done`. It does not write `startedFrame`. The test's `BlessProbe` does not call `BlessReference`.

- [ ] **Step 4: Run the test**

Filter `[arc][compare]`. Expected: PASS. After the arm step, reads is 0, `gate.frame` is still 10, and there is no verdict. A second step without a retired ticket stays `Running` with reads 0. Reads becomes 1 only after the ticket is published. Failed compare exits 8 with `ok` true. A missing reference without bless exits 6 and reads nothing. Bless of a missing name exits 0 with verdict `Passed`. The bless round trip exits 0. A compare issued while `capture.window` is `Running` exits 5 with `capture-pending` and does not call `arm`.

### Task 29: `play`, `stop`, `pause`, `resume`, `step` as frame-spanning jobs

**Files:**
- Create: `ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Arc/Commands/PlayCommands.cpp`
- Modify: `ArcaneCore/src/Arcane/Arc/TickGate.hpp` (add `void ClearSteps()`)
- Modify: `ArcaneRuntime/src/RuntimeApp.cpp` (the loop around `AdvanceSim` at line 814 already `Consume`s in Task 12)
- Modify: `ArcaneServer/src/ServerApp.cpp` (the loop before `Advance` at line 191)
- Modify: `ArcaneEditor` play loop (`PlaySession::Play` / `Stop` at `PlayMode.hpp:108` and `:115`; pause and step through `RunLoop::SetPaused` at `RunLoop.hpp:63` and `RequestSingleStep` at `:75`)
- Modify: `arc/src/main.cpp`
- Test: `ArcaneTests/src/Arc/PlayCommandTest.cpp`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `TickGate::Consume` (Task 12). `JobTable` (Task 9). `RequestQueue::Wait` (Task 6). `FrameStats` (Task 26). `PlaySession` has no pause and no step.
- Produces:

```cpp
namespace Arcane::Arc {
struct PlayHooks {
    TickGate* gate = nullptr;                 // runtime and server
    bool (*editorPlay)(void*) = nullptr;      // PlaySession::Play
    bool (*editorStop)(void*) = nullptr;      // PlaySession::Stop
    void (*editorSetPaused)(bool paused, void*) = nullptr;
    void (*editorRequestSingleStep)(void*) = nullptr;
    void* editorUser = nullptr;
    bool editor = false;
    FrameStats* stats = nullptr;
    std::string undoReason; // "play-mode" on the editor, "runtime-host" on runtime and server
};
void SetPlayHooks(PlayHooks hooks);
void SetPlayGate(HostGate* gate);
void RegisterPlayCommands(JobTable* jobs);
}
```

All five are Drive, `Mutates::PlayMode`, `needsFrame` false. `play.step` is `ExecThread::Job`. The others return in the command.

`play.start` does not call `Advance`. Editor: `editorPlay`. Runtime and server: `TickGate::SetRunning(true)` and `SetPaused(false)`. `play.stop`: editor `editorStop`; runtime and server `SetRunning(false)`, `SetPaused(true)`, `ClearSteps()`. `play.pause`: `SetPaused(true)` (editor function or the gate). `play.resume`: `SetPaused(false)` and `Running` stays true. `play.step`: `n` from `args.n`, default 1. `n < 1` or `n > 100000` is `arg.inapplicable`, exit 2, and it does not `Arm`. Otherwise it arms a job with `progress.total = n`, calls `RequestSteps(n)` (runtime and server) or stores `n` for the editor, and returns. It does not loop `Advance`. Each later `Drain` steps that job once. The job step reads `HostGate::frame` minus the frame at arm time. When the delta reaches `n` it completes the waiter. The host loop's `Consume` performs one `Advance` per iteration. After the job, `Consume` is `Idle`.

`undo` is `{label: null, undoable: false, reason: <PlayHooks::undoReason>}`. Do not emit a `recorded` field.

CLI: `arc play [--topology standalone|listen|client+server]`, `arc stop`, `arc pause`, `arc resume`, `arc step [N]`. `client+server` is `PlayTopology::EmbeddedServer`. `listen` is `ListenServer`. `standalone` is `Standalone`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

```cpp
#include <catch2/catch_test_macros.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Arc/Commands/PlayCommands.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Arc/Listener.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Arc/Dispatcher.hpp>
#include <Arcane/Net/TcpSocket.hpp>
#include <thread>

static std::string HttpExchange(std::uint16_t port, const std::string& token,
                                const std::string& requestLine, const std::string& body) {
    REQUIRE(Arcane::InitSockets());
    const SocketType sock = ::socket(AF_INET, SOCK_STREAM, IPPROTO_TCP);
    REQUIRE(sock != INVALID_SOCK);
    sockaddr_in addr{};
    addr.sin_family = AF_INET;
    addr.sin_port = htons(port);
    inet_pton(AF_INET, "127.0.0.1", &addr.sin_addr);
    REQUIRE(::connect(sock, reinterpret_cast<sockaddr*>(&addr), sizeof(addr)) == 0);
    std::string req = requestLine + "\r\nHost: 127.0.0.1:" + std::to_string(port) +
        "\r\nAuthorization: Bearer " + token + "\r\nContent-Type: application/json\r\nContent-Length: " +
        std::to_string(body.size()) + "\r\n\r\n" + body;
    REQUIRE(::send(sock, req.data(), static_cast<int>(req.size()), 0) == static_cast<int>(req.size()));
    std::string raw;
    char buf[4096];
    for (;;) {
        const int n = ::recv(sock, buf, sizeof(buf), 0);
        if (n <= 0) break;
        raw.append(buf, buf + n);
        const auto hdr = raw.find("\r\n\r\n");
        if (hdr == std::string::npos) continue;
        const auto key = raw.find("Content-Length:");
        if (key == std::string::npos || key > hdr) break;
        const int len = std::atoi(raw.c_str() + key + 15);
        if (static_cast<int>(raw.size() - (hdr + 4)) >= len) break;
    }
    CloseSocket(sock);
    return raw;
}

TEST_CASE("step 10 advances host.frame by exactly 10 while status still answers", "[arc][play]") {
    Arcane::Arc::TickGate tick;
    tick.SetRunning(false);
    tick.SetPaused(true);
    Arcane::Arc::FrameStats stats;
    Arcane::Arc::PlayHooks hooks;
    hooks.gate = &tick;
    hooks.stats = &stats;
    hooks.undoReason = "runtime-host";
    Arcane::Arc::JobTable jobs;
    Arcane::Arc::SetPlayHooks(hooks);
    Arcane::Arc::RegisterPlayCommands(&jobs);
    Arcane::Arc::RequestQueue queue;
    jobs.SetQueue(&queue);
    Arcane::Arc::HostGate gate;
    gate.frame = 100;
    gate.kind = "server";
    gate.state = "ready";
    Arcane::Arc::ListenerAtoms atoms;
    auto snap = std::make_shared<Arcane::Arc::HostStatus>();
    snap->state = "ready";
    snap->frame = 100;
    atoms.status.store(snap);
    auto& reg = Arcane::Arc::CommandRegistry::Get();
    const auto audit = std::filesystem::temp_directory_path() / "arc-play-audit.ndjson";
    Arcane::Arc::ArcDispatcher dispatcher(queue, reg, gate, jobs, audit);
    Arcane::Arc::ArcListener listener(queue, atoms, Arcane::Arc::MakeToken());
    listener.Start();
    const std::string body = R"({"command":"play.step","args":{"n":10}})";
    std::string posted;
    std::thread worker([&] {
        posted = HttpExchange(listener.Port(), listener.Token(), "POST /arc/v1/call HTTP/1.1", body);
    });
    const auto start = gate.frame.load();
    for (int spin = 0; spin < 200 && queue.Depth() == 0; ++spin)
        std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(5));
    dispatcher.Drain(1000.0, 8);
    const std::string status = HttpExchange(listener.Port(), listener.Token(), "GET /arc/v1/status HTTP/1.1", "");
    CHECK(status.find("200") != std::string::npos);
    CHECK(worker.joinable());
    int renders = 0;
    int advanced = 0;
    while (tick.Consume() != Arcane::Arc::TickDecision::Idle) {
        gate.frame.fetch_add(1);
        Arcane::Arc::NoteAdvance(stats, gate.frame.load(), 0.016, 1.0, 0.0);
        ++advanced;
        dispatcher.Drain(1000.0, 8);
        snap->frame = gate.frame.load();
        atoms.status.store(std::make_shared<Arcane::Arc::HostStatus>(*snap));
    }
    worker.join();
    listener.Stop();
    CHECK(advanced == 10);
    CHECK(gate.frame.load() - start == 10);
    CHECK(renders == 0);
    CHECK(tick.Consume() == Arcane::Arc::TickDecision::Idle);
    CHECK(posted.find("\"ok\":true") != std::string::npos);
    CHECK(posted.find("\"frame\":110") != std::string::npos);
    CHECK(posted.find("runtime-host") != std::string::npos);
    CHECK(stats.frame == 110);
    CHECK(stats.renderMs == 0.0);
    Arcane::Arc::Call later;
    later.command = "stats.frame";
    Arcane::Arc::RegisterStatsCommand(&stats);
    const auto after = Arcane::Arc::CommandRegistry::Get().Invoke(later).envelope;
    CHECK(after.data["frame"] == 110);
}

TEST_CASE("step rejects n outside 1..100000 and does not arm", "[arc][play]") {
    Arcane::Arc::TickGate tick;
    Arcane::Arc::PlayHooks hooks;
    hooks.gate = &tick;
    hooks.undoReason = "runtime-host";
    Arcane::Arc::JobTable jobs;
    Arcane::Arc::SetPlayHooks(hooks);
    Arcane::Arc::RegisterPlayCommands(&jobs);
    Arcane::Arc::Call call;
    call.command = "play.step";
    call.args = nlohmann::json{{"n", 0}};
    const auto zero = Arcane::Arc::CommandRegistry::Get().Invoke(call);
    CHECK(zero.completesJob == 0);
    CHECK(zero.envelope.errors[0].code == "arg.inapplicable");
    CHECK(Arcane::Arc::ExitFromHost(zero.envelope) == 2);
    CHECK(tick.StepsRemaining() == 0);
    call.args["n"] = 100001;
    const auto huge = Arcane::Arc::CommandRegistry::Get().Invoke(call);
    CHECK(huge.completesJob == 0);
    CHECK(tick.StepsRemaining() == 0);
}

TEST_CASE("play start does not advance, and stop returns to Idle", "[arc][play]") {
    Arcane::Arc::TickGate tick;
    tick.SetRunning(false);

    CHECK_FALSE(tick.Running());

}

static int g_editorSteps = 0;
static void EditorOneStep(void*) { ++g_editorSteps; }

TEST_CASE("editor step calls RequestSingleStep once per drain, not n times", "[arc][play]") {
    g_editorSteps = 0;
    Arcane::Arc::PlayHooks hooks;
    hooks.editor = true;
    hooks.editorRequestSingleStep = &EditorOneStep;
    hooks.undoReason = "play-mode";
    Arcane::Arc::JobTable jobs;
    Arcane::Arc::SetPlayHooks(hooks);
    Arcane::Arc::RegisterPlayCommands(&jobs);
    Arcane::Arc::HostGate gate;
    gate.frame = 4;
    Arcane::Arc::SetPlayGate(&gate);
    Arcane::Arc::Call call;
    call.command = "play.step";
    call.args = nlohmann::json{{"n", 3}};
    const auto armed = Arcane::Arc::CommandRegistry::Get().Invoke(call);
    CHECK(armed.completesJob != 0);
    CHECK(g_editorSteps == 0);
    jobs.StepAll(gate);
    CHECK(g_editorSteps == 1);
    gate.frame = 5;
    jobs.StepAll(gate);
    CHECK(g_editorSteps == 2);
    REQUIRE(armed.envelope.undo.has_value());
    CHECK(armed.envelope.undo->reason == "play-mode");
}
```

`SetPlayGate` stores the `HostGate*` the job step reads. The command function does not call `EditorOneStep`. One `StepAll` calls it once. The listener thread in the step-10 case blocks inside `POST /arc/v1/call` until the job finishes. `GET /arc/v1/status` is answered on that same listener while the POST is outstanding. `renders` stays 0: the server counts ticks. `play.start` must not increment `gate.frame`. The editor envelope's `undo.reason` is `play-mode`. Write that reason onto the envelope inside the command, before the job is armed, so `armed.envelope.undo->reason` is `play-mode` even while the job is still running.

`HostStatus` needs a copy constructor the `shared_ptr` store can use. It already is a normal struct (Task 3). `atoms.status` is `atomic<shared_ptr<const HostStatus>>`. Storing a fresh `shared_ptr` each advance is the publish. The POST body the listener parses uses the field `command`.

`ClearSteps` sets the remaining count to 0. The job records `startedFrame` from `gate.frame` at arm time. The step sets `progress.done` to the delta and returns true only when `delta >= total`. Do not call `Advance`, `AdvanceSim`, or `RequestSingleStep` inside `play.step`'s command function. The editor job step calls `editorRequestSingleStep` once and returns false until the frame delta reaches `n`. Wire runtime `ArcaneRuntime/src/RuntimeApp.cpp` and server `ServerApp.cpp` so `Drain`, then `Consume`, then at most one `Advance`, is the loop order from Task 12. `NoteAdvance` runs in that `Advance` branch only.

Flipping one pixel of that PNG and comparing again, still without `--bless`, must exit 8 with `ok` true, verdict `Failed`, and empty `errors`. There is no branch that treats exit 8 as an acceptable bless failure. The 60 s compare timeout covers the render-ticket readback. The job does not wait on `host.frame`. The editor bless runs before any play or step, so it is the idle-editor capture: `blessJson["host"]["frame"]` equals `settledJson["host"]["frame"]`. The runtime is started without `--frames`. Before `step`, `screenshot window` on that runtime exits 0 and the `stats.frame` value after it equals the value before it. Self-test exits, in this Catch2 case, not in an `.arcscenario` file (`arc run` is P2):

- `entity get name:NoSuchEntity` exits 6, code `entity.not-found`.
- `wait state playing --timeout 1` while the host is not playing exits 8, `data.timedOut` true, `ok` true, `errors` empty.
- `screenshot window` on the server exits 2, code `capture.no-renderer`.
- `screenshot window` on the idle headless runtime (no `--frames`, before `step`) exits 0, and `stats.frame` does not move.

Death: spawn `ArcaneRuntime.exe --headless --project <ReferenceProject> --arc --hang-main 60`. A bare `--hang-main` stays 0 (`HostConfig.cpp:178`, stored at `:259`) and `RuntimeFrame.cpp:484` never sleeps. The counter compared there is `io.frameCount` (`RuntimeFrame.cpp:795`), not `host.frame`. There is no `--frames`, so the tick gate stays Idle and `RenderGraph` (`:452`) still runs. Poll `arc status --format json` until the `data.hosts` row whose `pid` equals this child's `pi.dwProcessId` has `hostKind` `runtime` and `hung` true. The deadline is 90 s (boot, 60 frames, the 10 s heartbeat, and margin). A non-zero status, or a body that is not JSON yet, keeps polling. Then `cvar get render.vsync` with `--host runtime`, `--pid` of that same pid, and `--timeout 10` (10 seconds, so the harness wait of 15000 ms is longer than the client). Require that this child's hung row was seen, then require exit 7, `errors[0].code` `host.hung`, and `client.exitCode` 7. `GetExitCodeProcess` on the runtime is `STILL_ACTIVE`: the sleep is 15 s (`kHangMainSeconds`, `HostConfigTest.cpp:196`) and a zero-exit hang report leaves the process running (`Diagnostics.hpp:236`). The call is issued on the same iteration that first sees hung, with no extra sleep. The hung window is the 5 s from the 10 s heartbeat until the sleep ends. If `detail.arcdiag` is present, the file exists, its extension is `.arcdiag`, and its mtime is after the cvar call started. The first hung sample is usually before the watchdog's 12 s report (`Diagnostics.cpp:1919`), so the key is often absent. Task 14's `ObserveTarget` case is the deterministic `.arcdiag` pin. A client that blocks makes `RunArc` return 124, and `CHECK(code == 7)` fails. The case's destructor deletes `ReferenceProject/Saved/arc` so a `REQUIRE` abort still removes the descriptors. `HostGuard` is not widened to do that. `--crash-gpu` is a separate case, gated like G1 (`CrashWitnessTest.cpp:121-123`): when `ARCANE_DIAG_DESK` is unset it `SKIP`s with `ARCANE_DIAG_DESK not set -- desk-only TDR lane`. When the variable is set, spawn `ArcaneRuntime.exe --headless --project <ReferenceProject> --frames 60 --crash-gpu 30` and require the process to exit 1 (the hosts' device-loss code, `CrashWitnessTest.cpp:112-113`). An agent must not set `ARCANE_DIAG_DESK=1`. A deliberate GPU fault triggers a machine-wide TDR (`CrashWitnessTest.cpp:115-120`).

The token inside `Saved/arc/*.json` must not appear in any captured `arc.exe` stdout or stderr. Delete the descriptor files in the test's cleanup. Ruling P4 (b): the acceptance owns its child hosts through an RAII scope guard (`HostGuard`). The destructor kills every child (`TerminateProcess`, then `CloseHandle`) and calls `DeleteScratch`. Catch2 `REQUIRE` aborts by throwing, so the destructor runs on an assertion failure and on the success path. Do not use a local cleanup lambda; a later `REQUIRE` skips it. `DeleteScratch` only removes the scratch PNGs and a second call is a no-op. Handles that are already null are skipped, so the destructor does not double-close. Timeouts are 60 s for settled, 20 s for a cvar call, 10 s for quit. A hung child is a test failure after the kill. Screenshots and compares in this case each wait for `RunArc` to return before the next one starts, so two captures are not in flight together. The `crash-gpu` child and the `--hang-main` child use the same guard.
